{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-06-11T05:12:00Z |title=It's finally time for some activity! |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As was mentionned in my earlier posts, not much activity had to be expected until our CGI sub-domain would be up, so I can start working on restructuring the script framework of the site and finally configure the accounts for all staff to start working publically on their tasks. Well, after almost three weeks of waiting, I've finally been able to ping it correctly tonight, which means that it is going to be causing a lot of changes up shortly.

What does it concretely means? First, **most** of your bookmarks relating to CGI scripts of some sort(download links, Camelot's BBS, etc.) will no longer be working as they will be moving to {{highlight |text=cgi.camsys.org}}, which is going to be much easier to manage. On this view, expect these scripts to be down for a short time, especially for the boards, starting later tonight as I'll be moving the scripts. As well, some parts of our site's contents might not be accessible due to some URL references changing. Don't worry, everything should be fixed up within a few days, at most.

Among these changes, I'm glad to announce that the two sites we were hosting since quite a while, {{highlight |text=Star Wars: Vengeance}} and {{highlight |text=Open Rebellion}} will be making a return quite soon, which should be done around the same time as I configure all the accounts needed here. The rest of the operations will be left on their hands naturally ;) So expect these to be coming back shortly, and speak of which, I will be looking about the old affiliation requests I have been receiving since the past weeks, to see which ones will be working out, so if you have sent an e-mail about this subject, expect an answer soon.

Speak of which, I've started answering back to e-mails since a couple of weeks, so it's safe to send those now ;)

A short note about the last post, I mentionned about many of our tutorials to be coming up soon, unfortunately they are still not active. I have received work from {{highlight |text=Otak}} to delay them a little, as he reworked on them to provide a better linking as a few others have been added. Since our staff will have site access up soon, he should be able to upload them by himself, so expect this to be part of our activity increasing ;)

That should be all for today. In the mean time, have fun!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-06-03T04:07:00Z |title=MIA (Internet acronym) : 1) Missing In Action; 2) Mission Impossible: Activity |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Only this definition above can describe the state in which I have been for the past weeks. I'm very sorry to have been inactive for so long without giving any news; in this way, so you don't keep wondering what's going on so, I'll promise I will try to make a news update **at least** once a week so we always get updated news about the status of Camsys. I'll program a few reminders in Windows so I won't forget; is that a deal? Good :)

To make a long story short, I'm sure you probably guessed that I was too busy to update the site as of shortly; I'm glad to inform you that you are right! Well, things like that happen, there's some balance that must be kept between RL and VL apparently, otherwise one of them keeps banging on your mind ;) Anyway, I'm sure you don't want details, so I'll pass on with the actual news.

As you probably noticed, a couple of things have been uploaded since the last news post; more programs have been uploaded to our Software Live Wire, and a chat applet has been activated so you can access #camsys chatroom more easily(although we do recommend you to get a decent software like [mIRC](https://web.archive.org/web/20021020195536/http://www.mirc.com/) to access it). But other than that, you probably wonder if the promises of seeing more stuff up during the next weeks have faded out. I'm glad to inform you that you're wrong! ;) Among things, {{highlight |text=Otak}} has been working hard reforming the old tutorials we were having before the downtime, except naturally for the large {{highlight |text=Starcraft Editing Bible}}, which will remain in its original format. So where are they? On my disk drive ;) They still need to be SHTMLized and I need to make a couple of checks on them before putting them online; if the guys at #camsys don't keep me too busy, I'll probably do that tonight :)

As for other things, you haven't seen any other staff activity here in the news either; the reason is simple, I didn't create any account yet. Not because of lack of time, but because I currently am restructuring the CGIs of the site so they are accessible via a sub-domain. However, because I didn't have enough knowledge on how our domain has been configured over at [ReconGamer](https://web.archive.org/web/20021020195536/http://www.recongamer.com/), so I uselessly waited for one or two weeks, until, after asking, the tech support staff told me that {{highlight |text=Mutant}} from our {{highlight |text=ORG Network}} was having control over DNS. So I reached him yesterday and the subdomains should be activated shortly, meaning that you will actually see other staff post in a matter of short time :) I'm also thinking of changing the news script to another one, as the support for this one seems to go away, and thus the plug-ins information that I wanted to have(their homepage can't even get resolved anymore, no good). So if you see no news for a little while, you'll know why ;) I'll create all the accounts once the CGI domain is up and configured correctly.

Patch news this time, as you've probably heard of on [battle.net](https://web.archive.org/web/20021020195536/http://www.battle.net/), a new patch is currently on the development to fix all the bugs caused by the latest 1.08B. Hearing of this news, {{highlight |text=KramerBoy}}, who was in charge of updating StarGraft to 1.08B, has announced that he won't work onto fixing the software until the 1.09 patch is released, because it would be useless work anyway. So pray that the patch comes out soon so you can get the latest update of the #2 program at Camsys :) As for StarDraft, its replacement should be arriving up shortly, making it invulnerable to any changes a patch could make.

Speak of Starcraft, {{highlight |text=Langweilig}} has attracted me into battle.net again, and it did a good effect; it boosted my energy a little so that I still care for Starcraft, with the interesting new patch changes that it brings. In this view, I announce that the long-awaited {{highlight |text=AI Script tournament}} will be finally making its return! After two tries which never made it to the end, the first because ScAIEdit wasn't stable enough for use, and the second because Camsys went into its long sleep(i.e. downtime), I do hope the third try will be successful! So prepare yourselves, AI Scripters, and work on your little beast of code that will challenge many others in the battle arena we'll prepare :) With the replays feature, not only will it be easier to make battle reports, but you'll be able to actually see the match as well. It sounds like good Starcraft battle will be finally coming :) You should receive more details about that on the following weeks.

What else? On the last miscellaneous things, I have changed my name identity to {{highlight |text=Veldhar[GD]}}, as some of you have seen in #camsys, and I'm on the way to update the important references; note that the {{highlight |text=Gold Dragon}} naming is naturally still in use(the GD acronym is too popular anyway ;)), but I'm just adding a more meaningful name to it ;) On other things, I have received many complaints that our BBS is slow; be sure that I do know about that, our servers seem to be very picky on Perl resources and thus making them slow to proceed. I'll be looking for ways to improve that, but know that it is out of question to change the board. I worked hard enough on that one, and it provides about a perfect look that boards should have ;) Okay, not perfect, but close enough...

That's all for today folks! Expect more updates to be coming up shortly, and finally breathe in some fresh Camsys air :)
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=May 2001}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-05-19T05:54:00Z |title=Some quick news from the front line |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

**We are momentarily interrupting your current program schedule for this news flash.**

Coincidences are strange sometimes, a few minutes after I made the last post, I received a message from {{highlight |text=Kramerboy}}; after some talk, I now have quick news to add about the 1.08 patch that has been released for StarCraft.

{{highlight |text=Kramerboy}} has confirmed that he'll be taking care of updating StarGraft to be supported on the 1.08 patch. He will start looking onto that in the following days and should bring news about that by himself, as I'll start creating the accounts soon :) As for StarDraft, it will be taken care of by another way; you should get more news on that soon too ;)

So all is good, the sky is clear and the sea is calm; 1.08 support should be coming soon! :)

**We are now returning to your regular program schedule. Thanks for choosing Camsys!**
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-05-19T05:25:00Z |title=Usually it's good news... but for us, it's another story. |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, the alpha test was leaked, so most of the Starcraft community was aware this would happen soon; the SC 1.08 patch has been finally released! With its new features and balances changes, it can be more or less considered as a refreshing wind on a game that's really starting to get old... but for the customization community, what does it means?

Usually, {{highlight |text=King Arthur}} and {{highlight |text=Merlin}} would immediately take their weapons and start tearing apart the new version to discover what is needed to make the customization softwares compatible with them; but as you all know, {{highlight |text=King Arthur}} has left Camsys and no one has ever got a sign of life from him ever since, and as for {{highlight |text=Merlin}}, the poor wizard is totally swamped with college work; hopefully, as he said, he should be able to reappear during the summer. But in the meanwhile, what?

The compatibility tests seem to be different for many; some have reported that StarDraft still works with 1.08 despite a few errors at the start, and others can't make it work at all. What is the verdict? Well... I'd like to bring mine, but I currently am on Windows 2000, which is incompatible with our patching softwares as you all know; sure, I am on dual boot, but the Windows 98 I have on this same disk is... somewhat... well, to summarize, I have trouble booting in it ^-^ Who knows what happened, I suspect some applications don't bother checking where the windows directory is and when uninstalling themselves, they delete win98 files instead of the win2k ones. The only valid explanation(and experimentation) I can get since I get a lot of missing files fatal errors if I try to boot. So to come back with the subject, I cannot verify the StarDraft compatibility by myself, not until I can somehow test it on the other computers here ;)

However, the second patching tool, StarGraft, has a unanimous verdict: it is totally incompatible(doh, it was to be expected as a EXE patcher). Immediately after, obviously, an avalanche of requests, demands and beggings are made to update it. Well, well, well... For the same reasons as StarDraft, it becomes extremely complicated to update it.

So what to do? {{highlight |text=KramerBoy}} has some experience with SD's source code, but I have no idea if he would be able to update StarDraft for 1.08, as it is quite different than just updating source code, but also patching with new numbers. I doubt anyone here has experience with StarGraft code either. As of me, I never looked into either of these program's source codes, thus I'm not sure of my ability of updating them. So to make a long story short, it becomes extremely complicated to make an update on these. Unless some staff here have extensive knowledge on any of these two softwares workings(if so, [please let me know](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org?Subject=[STAFF] About updating StarDraft and StarGraft)), or that {{highlight |text=Merlin}} becomes available sooner than expected, I don't suggest holding your breath for any updates. The best solution as of now is to maintain two versions of StarCraft, 1.07 and 1.08, one for customization and another one for your playing needs. The procedure is simple: before patching to 1.08, copy your Starcraft dir somewhere else. Apply the patch. Here you go, two Starcraft versions ;) If there's something you didn't understand, well... ask someone else to help! *chuckles*

So that should clear the matter about 1.08. As for Camsys? Well, I'm sure that you'll be pleased to know that {{highlight |text=Otak}} has been working on new pages, that is those about the chatroom and a few WarCraft 2 and Starcraft editing tutorials. I would have uploaded them tonight, but he isn't currently online. Oh well, just to let you know you should be seeing these soon! =) As for me, I should be working on a few specs pages in a matter of time, so expect these to be online soon too.

Another news concerning staff management: the staff list is now closed! While the current list doesn't mention of everyone(yet), I expect to be receiving e-mails containing **COMPLETE** informations(i.e. if you're missing the half of the fields, I'll ask you to resend your mail with all the infos this time) in the next few days, so you should see everyone aboard soon. Besides, I should update it tonight as well to reflect the newest changes. I also mentionned that the staff will be receiving the accounts they need when the staff list is going to be closed; well, this is done now. What next? Well, you guessed it =) It is time for all the staff to submit what you need! Here are the different accounts you can receive, according to what you'd like to have.

*(This part concerns only Camsys staff)*
- **E-mail:** Every staff get it. Just mention the e-mail name you'd like to have, along with your e-mail password.
- **Special rank:** As a Camsys staff, you are allowed to pick an original title as your BBS rank. If you are interested, just mention your account name and the title you'd like to receive.
- **News:** If you can spell, are interested into posting and won't make utterly pointless posts, just mention your nickname with your news password.
- **#Camsys Auto-Op:** If you come regularly to #Camsys, and are interested into operating the channel too, register your nickname at Nickserv and mention it. Note that you are subject to a few rules regarding the channel operating.
- **Moderator status:** If you come regularly to the boards, and are interested into moderating them, mention your account name. Note that you are subject to a few rules regarding board moderating.
- **FTP access:** This is a restrained access. If you can justify the use you'll make out of it(mention of your reasons), then you will get this account. Mention of your desired username and FTP password. *Note that the FTP accounts won't be created until the CGI subdomain is up and running.*

Once you have determined which accounts you need, just [e-mail the account creation request](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org?subject=[STAFF] Account creation) with the required informations as showed above. Please note that you **MUST** use the same e-mail that you used to show a sign of life, as it is the only way I have to make sure you are from the staff. It should take a few days at most to get your accounts created after I receive your e-mails. As for FTP, for the reason mentionned above, it will take a bit longer.

That is all for today! Now that I have finished this college's session(and related things, just finished **today**), so I now have more free time at my disposition; for how long, I can't predict, but you should be seeing more movement within the next days :) Besides, I'll be uploading other Camsys softwares tonight, so watch the Software Live Wire grow a little as my 56k slowly finishes them ;)

P.S. I still haven't replied to e-mails, I apologize once again. But if you have unconstructive complaints, whining, or pointless e-mails, please send them to /dev/null. It will save me time for when I'll find some to reply. I'm really sorry to all those who have been waiting.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-05-14T04:37:00Z |title=Finals time, deadlines time, free... time?! Wow, it exists? |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I finally got some free time for me to do some staff management at Camsys! Finals are still not over with though. I'm glad to see many missing staff have finally answered to my request for giving a sign of life. Among the staff that is now considered officially aboard, there's old names, new names, people more or less known, so you better get used to these names! ;) Here they are: {{highlight |text=Ace Calhoon}}, {{highlight |text=Daduke}}, {{highlight |text=Defenderz}}, {{highlight |text=Mibz}}, {{highlight |text=Sean}}, {{highlight |text=Sir Galahad}} and {{highlight |text=Vortex}}. The staff who have provided me their informations have been added in the updated [Staff List](/web/20010714051402/http://www.camsys.org/about/staff.shtml) which should be exhaustive soon. That means that if you're a staff, and still haven't send me an [e-mail](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org?subject=Hey! I'm part of the staff too!), you better do so immediately! It's going to be a matter of time until I consider this list done. It doesn't mean that we won't recruit staff in the future, but it'll take some time... If you see what I mean ;)

Among other things, you can notice the [History](/web/20010714051402/http://www.camsys.org/about/history.shtml) section is finally up! I know it's just a page, but at the time this is written, {{highlight |text=Otak}} is working on a few new pages, despite being quite verbal about a certain lack of a FTP(hehe). Mind you, I still have a restructuration of the site before giving FTP access to anyone, so the security can be made optimal. This should take a few days before the final configurations are done, so I will be able to handle account management later this week.

One last thing, despite having found most of our softwares, you can notice that there's still very little up our Software Live Wire. This situation is mostly because of me, because as I mentionned earlier, I have little time at my disposal and a 56k isn't really helping on the upload =P Don't worry though, this should be my first priority right after staff management!

During the last days, I have still received a large number of e-mails, however I haven't been able to reply a single one of them. My most sincere apologies to those who are waiting for a reply; the time I have at my disposal is limited. Be sure that I read every message you send, just that I can't find the time to answer them. However, for matters relating to technical supports, instead of sending these e-mails to me, when I'm not a tech support staff anyway, ask your questions on the board, or wait for the technical support e-mails to come up. As for matters regarding me, try to contact me online on #camsys IRC at Dalnet, or by ICQ at #107158619(don't add me if it's just for a little question or so though).

This is all for today's news. Have fun, and enjoy your stay! =)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-05-08T03:58:00Z |title=Staff requests, missing staff... and I'm not talking of wooden sticks. |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, as I expected, staff management is going to be a lot of fun. Among the e-mails received lately, I was surprised to find many were about staff; not only reports from missing staff, but mostly submissions for entering Camsys! In fact, this has gotten so large that it can get troublesome handling all this if I don't put a brake soon.

First of all, I'm not accepting new staff submissions for now. So please don't hold your breath for a reply. I think you'll understand that I first urgently have to know who's aboard, and complete the staff list so I know what positions would be available for more to join, and so on. So I thank everyone who had the kindness to propose themselves, but I'm not ready to handle these for now.

Second, I think you'll have guessed it, I have finally got a sign of life from a few of our missing staff ;) {{highlight |text=Fantom}}, {{highlight |text=Langweilig}}, {{highlight |text=Rangsk}} are now considered as being officially new/back in the staff, so greetings to all of you and have fun being overworked to death! *chuckles* I would need your informations to add up to the staff page; just check out the staff page and send an e-mail containing the information for all the fields(yes, real first name + first letter of last name is required ;)). I'll add you there as soon as I receive the e-mail.

Third, I'll repeat the message for missing staff: if you were in the team in the past, or received a confirmation letter from {{highlight |text=KramerBoy}}, please [e-mail me](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org?subject=Hey! I'm part of the staff too!) as soon as possible; I won't put an official ultimatum date, but expect that I'll consider the staff list to be complete within the next week. Of course, I won't put you out if you arrived a day or two late... but don't wait too much, please :)

Last of all, thanks to everyone who are still supporting Camelot Systems, even after this long downtime! I must say that I am still surprised that a community can survive even when long months have passed without very little way to keep contact, except for the #camsys chatroom which has fortunately remained lively(okay, so sometimes it's dead, sometimes it's overheated... that's the fun of IRC!) Just give it a week or two more, and you should see the same flow of activity here as before :)

Well, that is all for today. I'm still quite loaded with finals, so my available time to do more work on the site is still limited, but they aren't lasting forever ;) Fortunately...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-05-06T04:52:00Z |title=What's wrong? Take off that look! |author=Veldhar[GD] |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Don't start blaming hallucinations, tireness or computer viruses, because it's real now; I know I have done much promises about getting Camsys back online VERY soon(tm) a few weeks ago, but college homeworks, finals preparation and a RL project had to get my attention first before I could work seriously on this coming back. Well, the finals are coming next week, so I expect my available time to be about as short, but at least I'm thinking of all of you who are still waiting patiently(and sometimes not ;)). Well, here it is!

**We are now finally back online!** So resitance is futile and all your base are belong to us. Err... You know what I mean ;) This new design has been nicely made by KramerBoy, and I have rewritten the HTML files to reduce the size of the design, so it works on 800x600 resolution too, and added up the contents. In case you have missed the official announcement made earlier on April, we are now heading for larger goals than just the customization of games; extending to this, we will be putting much focus on everything around as well. Concretely, that means everything 'game' on the technical side is aimed to be part of Camsys now. What brought the glory of Camsys 1.0 was the StarCraft customization tools, but we can't stay on that forever; while we will maintain a SC program development division(little or large, depends of staff interest), we will develop many other subjects now. Programming topics will obviously be maintained, as it is my intention to write up some articles on the subject, now that I barely have a program project for Camsys anymore ;) Merlin(yeah, he's alive, hard to believe isn't it? ;)) is also intending to do some writing this summer as well, and perhaps a few other Camsys staff as well if they're interested =)

Speak of which, I only have a little idea now of who's aboard! KramerBoy has done recruiting earlier on, but it looks like he didn't maintain a list of recruited staff, so I'm kinda on trouble keeping up with everyone. A short, non-exhaustive staff list has begun [here](/web/20010714051402/http://www.camsys.org/about/staff.shtml), but I can be positive there is many more here. So if you were part of Camsys team, or received the confirmation letter from KB mentionning your application has been accepted, and that you are not listed, **please** [contact me](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org?subject=Hey! I'm part of the staff too!) **as soon as possible** so I can get it updated and keep in touch with everyone in the team. **DON'T** try tricking me out, because I will ask for an official document in case I have any doubts, so if you can't justify or provide it, you'll be part of my next barbecue! As the meal, of course =)

A second message to the staff, if you want your accounts(news posting, e-mail, BBS moderator status, FTP access, depending of what your positions are about) set up, you need to [contact me](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org?subject=Dear dragon... can you create my account(s)?) as well! You only have to provide me your username and password, and you'll get access in little time; isn't it nice? ;)

As of now, you probably noticed it, we have little contents activated right now! Well, it was either that or you wait one or two weeks more... you prefer? ;) Right now, I have uploaded what little my 56k has to offer me, that is StarDraft, StarGraft, ScAIEdit 3.1, and the BBS. You can expect more to be coming soon, as the rest of the softwares are fairly little so my modem takes them well. The rest of the main pages and of the design should be coming up in the next days/1-2 weeks too. So stay tuned, as we're not dead!

Many have answered my request for our softwares, and I thank everyone who has been kind enough to send me an e-mail and upload these somewhere so I can put them back online. Thanks goes to, in alphabetical order: {{highlight |text=[COH]AH}}, {{highlight |text=Cliff Adair}}, {{highlight |text=Dark Shadow}}, {{highlight |text=Defenderz}}, {{highlight |text=Domterran}}, {{highlight |text=Duran}}, {{highlight |text=Eric Feldbauer}}, {{highlight |text=Eric Peterson}}, {{highlight |text=Evan Quasney}}, {{highlight |text=Gabriel Matthews}}, {{highlight |text=KarlKFI}}, {{highlight |text=Jaguar}}, {{highlight |text=Krono}}, {{highlight |text=Le Duc Trung}}, {{highlight |text=Lucy Bahamondes Margery}}, {{highlight |text=Pikamaster}}, {{highlight |text=PSX}}, {{highlight |text=Sir Galahad}} and {{highlight |text=Suicidal Insanity}}, for helping me out retrieving our files =) This is my way to reply to all your e-mails, otherwise it would take me forever to write up all those ;) As for everyone else who sent me an e-mail, first, NO, I won't send you any of our softwares by e-mail; I'm not a living download server, so please be patient. For all other matters, you should receive a reply soon.

And, last of the news, in case you missed the announcements again, I'm now in charge of Camsys, but I guess it makes little difference for most of you ;) Still, I'll really do my best to at least post once in a while, and remain decently active on the boards. I'm eager to put up a full website soon, and, to all the staff, please contact me as mentionned above. I need all of you to restore Camsys! =)
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=February 2001}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2001-02-08T20:23:35Z |title=Camelot Systems : Jobs |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Camelot Systems is growing!  Job opportunities are opening and we need people to fill them.  From basic news reporting to advanced programming, from creating customs to creating art, we have countless spots that need to be filled.  If you have what it takes, then WE NEED YOU.

No, sadly, you will not receive monetary compensation for your work, but you will receive a number of other benefits!  Just the sheer popularity is more than enough!  You'll get a direct and popular medium in which to display your work, while improving the internet community as a whole.  Just think of the possibilities!

More information on specific application procedures is available below, but for the most part, you can expect to have to submit some sample work, as well as a little background info.  Easy enough!


**Reporter**

Camelot Systems is in search of motivated, dedicated people to help keep our news section up to date.

Your main responsibilities would include:

- Regularly updating and maintaining news portions of the site
- Keeping track of community and general news
- Answering general viewer EMail

Requirements for the position are as follows:

- Excellent English and grammatical skills
- Basic knowledge of newswriting

For more information on applying, EMail [KramerBoy](https://web.archive.org/web/20010208202335/mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org?subject=Reporter%20Position)


**Webmaster**

Camelot Systems is in search of a knowledgeable, professional person to look after and maintain our website.  In addition to updating and creating new pages and layouts, occasional news reporting will also be expected.

Your main responsibilities would include:

- Upkeep of all Camelot Systems web files
- Creation and design of new layouts
- Overseeing of news pages

Requirements for this position are as follows:

- Previous experience managing a website
- Very fluent in HTML and related languages
- Fluent in Javascript (CGI/Java experience a plus)
- Good motivational skills
- Excellent English and grammatical skills
- Basic knowledge of news writing

For more information on applying, EMail [KramerBoy](https://web.archive.org/web/20010208202335/mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org?subject=Webmaster%20Position)


**Programmer**
Camelot Systems is looking for programmers to fill the following positions:

*Customization Programmer*

It will be your task to create new and maintain existing customization programs that have made Camelot Systems so popular.  You will work to decode data used in popular games and express them in a form easily understandable and editable by the end user.

Requirements for this position are as follows:

- Experience using popular programming languages (C/C++ preferred)
- Experience decoding file formats
- Ability to work on team projects

*Senior Customization Programmer*

You will be involved in the more complex aspects of game customization utilities, such as creating editing schemes, coordinating programming efforts, and doing some of the more advanced programming and research.  You will also be involved in assisting other programmers in their efforts.

Requirements for this position are as follows:

- In depth experience and knowledge of popular programming languages (C/C++ preferred).
- Experience using Win32 API debugging functions
- Extensive knowledge of Win32 in general
- Experience working on large projects
- Experience decoding file formats (EXEs are a plus)
- Understanding of 80x86 assembly language is a plus


Although we are currently only seeking customization programmers, we certainly will be expanding in the future, so that's not all you'll be limited to!

For Programming Positions: [KramerBoy](https://web.archive.org/web/20010208202335/mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org?subject=Programmer)
For Senior Programming Positions: [KramerBoy](https://web.archive.org/web/20010208202335/mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org?subject=Senior%20Programmer)


**Customs Designer**
Camelot Systems is looking for talented and creative individuals with an interest in game customization, to participate in the creation of customs including, though not limited to, those using Camelot Systems programs.  You will be responsible for designing and distributing patches in an effort not only to demonstrate our programs, but also to create a unique and fun type of game-play.  You will be required to document your patches to help educate the end users.

Requirements for this position are as follows:

- Experience using Camelot Systems and other game customization tools
- Ability to learn and understand basic customization programs in a short period of time
- Intense interest in game customization
- Excellent English and grammatical skills
- Interest in a wide variety of games
- Programming experience a plus

For more information on applying, EMail [KramerBoy](https://web.archive.org/web/20010208202335/mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org?subject=Custom%20Designer%20Position)


**Artist**
Camelot Systems is in search of artistically proficient individuals to supplement our website and programs with graphical content.  You will also create various graphics to be used in our customs.

Requirements for this position are as follows:

- Extensive experience using Photoshop and other graphical editors
- Experience creating web-based graphics and content
- Experience creating graphics for games is a plus
- Experience creating graphics for 3D games is a big plus

For more information on applying, EMail [KramerBoy](https://web.archive.org/web/20010208202335/mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org?subject=Artist%20Position)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-08-25T16:58:26Z |title=A 'new' direction is taken(at least for me) |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A completely off-topic introduction, my human form is currently in college, at a computer lab. Mostly killing time, as it lasts 3 hours(up to 3PM), and we only had to type an easy document to "get used to Word". Obviously I finished it in 10 minutes(there was a good amount of text to type). Then I thought, why not spend some time at Camsys? Which is what I'm doing basically ;)

From the amount of responses I got from my last post, I've got a fairly good interest at the programming subject, which was equaled by Diablo 2, but because of some reasons I mentionned in the feedbacks, this is unfortunately not possible for now. However, I'm more than interested in developing the programming subject, mostly taking care of the programming section and board we'll have(the board is already up, check it out!). I'll also write some tutorials on this subject, although I don't know whether I should concentrate writing basic or advanced stuff... That's why I'll once again ask for what kind of programming topic you'd like to see ;) This way I might be able to start writing some documents, and everyone else wanting to participate is welcome too. Speak of suggestions, forget ultra-advanced stuff like "Making Quake IV from scratch", I won't accept such things, for an obvious reason. This is way too easy, ask something harder ;) j/k

So that's basically the goal I'll aim for a while. Some may wonder what's the state of my released projects at Camsys... I consider them finished. Not in the sense 'perfect', but in the sense 'I think they're doing the job, and are stable enough to be used without problems'. In other words, I won't go through new features and such for them. At least for now. My only 'public' priorities right now are taking care of Camsys, working on my personal/secret project and now, the programming section. This last one will be up soon, when I'll have at least a document ready I guess ;) Until then, don't hesitate to suggest topics you'd like to see treated, that will be of a great help. Don't forget to use the new board either :)

That's enough for now, I'll kill some other time in the boards :) Until next update!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-08-20T03:16:59Z |title=500,000! |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

StarDraft was obviously our most popular piece of software, racking up nearly 120,000 downloads on it's own, while StarGraft came in second at almost 90,000. {{begin-newsitem |time=2000-08-18T03:22:25Z |title=*opens wings and gives a sign of life* |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It's been quite a while I haven't given a single bit of news... It makes 18 days tonight. I thought perhaps it would be good to get rid of that "inactivity", to let you know I'm still alive :)

I'll start immediately with the sad news for Camelot Systems, about KA's departure as you probably read below. This is indeed a big loss for Camelot Systems, as he did a wonderful job with customization softwares, and many projects wouldn't have been possible without his work. Thanks King Arthur for your great help to the SC community, you'll be missed! Although it's not THAT sad, since he seems to have fun(and probably lots of work) with his Blizzard job :) Good luck at Blizzard!

Now you might wonder what I've did during these 18 days? Actually, I've done a lot, but nothing related to Camsys. In fact, I've been taking care of my last days of vacation, so I took some "vacations" from Camsys. This means, I haven't replied any e-mails during that time(I sincerely apologize) and this is the same for the forums and other Camsys-related things. But all in all, since ScAIEdit 3.1 seems to be stable overall, I don't think you have suffered much on it ;)

This said, it brings an interesting question. Now that my biggest project has been achieved(I'll probably do a little maintenance update, but there won't be new things added, as far as I consider), I'm now with no projects. Or is it? Yes, I'm currently developing a personal project which will remain secret for a bit of time until it's advanced enough to risk unveiling it(although many already know, but it's not that important :). But concretely, it's still not a Camsys project. I've been thinking a bit of it, and we can no longer hide the fact SC customization software is reaching an end, except for Mutagen being developed by KramerBoy, which is the new leader(congrats!). Just before you say SC customization isn't dying, please notice I was talking about softwares :)

This brings a really interesting question : What new goal should Camsys head to? I'm not asking you to answer(although you're welcome to suggest if you want). Should we head to customize another popular game(as we already tried in the past), or start writing programming tutorials as KA did some time ago? This is an example of the decisions hard to take for now. It's most likely we'll have to choose a path, but there is no urge to decide now. The "normal" path would have been to design Diablo 2 customization tools, but the cheating issue is so big we can hardly do that without helping cheaters. So...

I guess this completes what I wanted to say on that subject. So what am I going to do? Probably work on my personal project and devote more time to college(which is starting Monday). :) Many have been asking of the AI Tournament I planned to organize, and this is a good question. Since time will surely start to be even more precious next week, it's probable I'll leak time to organize it. Knowing this, I might end up proposing someone I know to take care of it, as I think it's an idea worth to be done :) I still have to think of it, so please don't ask for tournament update until I take a clear decision :)

I'll start getting more active by replying my e-mails and taking care of the things I'm used to in the next days :) With no Camsys project from me on the works for now, this should actually free up some time ;) So, until next update...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-08-14T03:30:49Z |title=The return... and farewell |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've finally returned from my summer sojourn at Blizzard. Unfortunately it is only to leave you again in three short weeks when I return to work for Blizzard full time for at least a year. I've decided to take a year off of school to work for them in an internship position as a full time software engineer. Alas this means that my time at Camelot Systems has come to a rather abrupt end. Although the prospects of working here as I had before were already fairly grim due to a rather hectic schedule, this new situation changes things even more, and I will no longer have any free time left to devote to the site. Besides it would really be a conflict of interests to continue to work here while I'm working at Blizzard full time. As such, I have decided to pass on the leadership role to KramerBoy, knowing that he is now quite prepared to take full control of the site. Merlin will remain his usual reclusive self... I know he still wants to work on CamSys tools, but time may elude him yet... In any case, Camelot Systems will continue after my departure, and I intend to check in on things from time to time and to lend my aid when I can. My profound thanks to everyone who helped to make this site what it is, especially my coworkers of the last... wow 4 years I guess. I would not have gotten this opportunity to work at Blizzard without the support from the community, most notably our stalwart affiliates at the Org Network, at Campaign Creations, and at Diabloii.net. I thank you all for your everlasting support of Camelot Systems and all that we create, and I hope that you will continue to extend that support and your gratitude towards KramerBoy as he prepares to lead Camelot Systems into new territory.

Thank you all and fare thee well!
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-08-02T21:24:08Z |title=Diablo II |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Many of you have been asking whether or not we are going to be doing customization tools for Diablo II.  Up until now, I have been skeptical about that possibility because of the high probability that the editors would be used for cheating on Open Battle.net.  Still, two days ago I began work on such a program and finished it up today.  The power of the utility was astounding.  Thanks to Blizzard's method of storing the game data, five-hundred lines of code and two days of work was all it took to create an editor that could (theoretically) add units/weapons/objects to the game, (proven) edit most all of the game stats, (theoretically) change the layout of many of the games maps, etc.  I was able to alter the gambling prices, change item drop rates, and add more sockets to items, along with a myriad of other tweaks and hacks.

Unfortunately, it became apparent to me that my initial impression of the game was correct; it is incredibly fragile.  Don't take my word for it though, just look at some of the screen shots below (~400k a piece):

[This took 5 minutes to collect](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/images/Diablo/Screenshot3.jpg)

[Check the defense are Str requirement](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/images/Diablo/Screenshot6.jpg)

[Awfully high damage for a Wand](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/images/Diablo/Screenshot8.jpg)

[**De**flation?](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/images/Diablo/Screenshot11.jpg)

[Would **you** pay that much?](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/images/Diablo/Screenshot12.jpg)

[That's a few too many gems...](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/images/Diablo/Screenshot13.jpg)

Now, many of these don't pose a direct threat so far.  The Defense and the socket increases don't show up on another player's computer, nor does the damage increase.  My most serious concern is the drop rate and gambling alteration I was able to make.  This would pose a serious threat to online trading.  Also, mind you, I've barely scratched the surface with these hacks.  Anyone with any sort of time on their hands could probably do far more damage to the game than I've done in a just a matter of hours.

Keep in mind, I'm not completely ruling out the possibility of our creating customization programs.  We were firm in our belief that we would never create a command editor for StarCraft, yet it eventually became our second most popular piece of software.  But, there's a lot more work we have to do to determine if there's a safe way in which we can edit Diablo and still preserve the game's security.

Any feedback you have on this subject will be appreciated.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=July 2000}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-31T03:16:05Z |title=The new version sees the light of the day... |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It took more time than originally expected, but one thing for sure, it was worth it. ScAIEdit 3.1 is now publically released! With 26 changes over the previous version, you'll certainly want to download it if you're already using ScAIEdit 3.0. Since it's still a big file, I'll give you the download link right away so you can continue reading while downloading ;)

[ScAIEdit 3.1](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=scaiedit31) (2.34 MB) - written by [Gold Dragon](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org)

I'd like to apologize for the lack of news from me these last days, especially on the progress of ScAIEdit 3.1. I've been quite silent for the past days/weeks, except on ICQ/#CamSys. I've been really busy during that time, and haven't been able to work much on ScAIEdit 3.1 until the last days. And when I started working heavily on it, I simply didn't want to post news because I thought it would be better to wait for the release, which took a bit longer than I expected. I still have a little number of unreplied e-mails and didn't write much on the feedbacks/forums, but know that I read every e-mail I receive and I also read the feedbacks, only that I can't always reply them in a short amount of time. But all in all, the 3.1 update is now released, so it should compensate ;)

On a side note, many have complained about the fact I didn't release the opcode name list. I haven't answered this yet, and I'll be doing right now : the list was not as useable as you might think. I didn't receive the complete opcode knowledge database, but an helpful list of the opcode names. It wasn't useable in its original form, so there was a lot of tweaking/testing necessary to convert them in the opcode database, and that's precisely why I decided to not release it before ScAIEdit 3.1. Also note that it was my own decision to share these good news with you before time. If I get too much of such reactions, I'll simply stop to tell you important progress news and wait until the product is released. I won't be doing it, but I'm only saying that getting this kind of reactions is a bit annoying. Thanks for those who reacted well ;)

Others have wondered how ScAIEdit 3.1 can export to MPQ files, and the answer is simple : ScAIEdit 3.1 generates a batch file executing [MPQ2k](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.campaigncreations.com/starcraft/mpq2k/) to save the MPQ file. That means you'll need to install it in order to activate this feature. Then you'll only have to point to the location of MPQ2k in the ScAIEdit III options. As simple as that ;)

Script making should be now quite interesting, but it's now your turn to explore the new possibilities. I wish you good luck and feel free to ask any questions about ScAIEdit 3.1 and scripts. Feedback is very appreciated too :)

Until next update! :)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-25T03:36:51Z |title=Poll results, new one coming... |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It was supposed to be done saturday night, but I ran out of my poll questions list, and I was a bit busy so I hope you don't mind the little delay ;) I kept saturday night results though. Here they are :

*What is your primary criteria of evaluation in a game?*

**Gameplay:** 288(55%)

**Customization:** 84(16%)

**Graphics:** 64(12%)

**Lifetime:** 33(6%)

**Innovation:** 30(5%)

**Sounds:** 13(2%)

**Popularity:** 3(0%)

Total votes: 515

Unsurprisingly, it seems Gameplay is the criteria of choice for more than half the gamers around here :) Customization still gets second place, followed closely by Graphics. It's good to see some still consider Customization as a primary choice, because that's what we're here for, right? ;) You can now vote on the next poll, see the right menu bar!

On a side note, some of you seem to wait impatiently for ScAIEdit 3.1(with good reasons ;), so I'm going to make a little update :) I've been doing a lot of work on it lately, on my spare time, and it should see the light of day very soon. Just to give you an idea of the changes, you can now import/export AI Files from/to MPQs :) This has been added after many requests to support MPQs, by e-mail. You see, suggesting things can sometimes make them reality :) Now don't start pumping lots of insane suggestions though ;) I apologize for the time taken for the 3.1 update, but remember I can't control everything, and I don't always have all the time you'd want to ;) Thanks a lot for understanding.

Until next update! :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-20T14:11:39Z |title=Windows Adventures part 3 |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

**Important note:** If you don't want to read the whole post, at least read this paragraph. *I've lost all my e-mails that were received after July 13th and before July 19th.* If you sent your e-mail on this range of time, please [e-mail](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org) it again, else it will be considered as lost. Thanks for reading. For more details, read below :)

I think you've already guessed my computer has crashed another time. Seems to happen often, eh? :) This has not happened today though, but yesterday morning. At least now I know why, and it shouldn't happen again before a long time. I'll describe a bit later on this post. This time is similar to the previous one, NDD crashed the C: drive by correcting lost strings. That's why I've lost e-mails, as I described above, and since I had around 16 unreplied e-mails obtained in these days, it would be good to send them again :)

I said I know why my computer crashed all these times. Well it's quite simple : the FAT table of my C: drive was wrong since the beginning. Since I always took my backups to avoid reinstalling everything, these backups always had a wrong FAT table, because this error seems to have happened a long time ago. How comes I had a wrong FAT table when I could still use my computer? For those who don't know, there is 2 FAT tables on a partition, for security matters I think. The problem is, one of these 2 copies was corrupted, and when NDD wanted to correct the mistakes, it used the corrupted copy as well, screwing up the C: drive. I'm pretty sure it would have been the same with ScanDisk. At least, I'm happy it happened with C:, because if it would have happened with G: for example, it would have been less fun(G: is the drive for all my programmation stuff :). Although I backup my vital stuff often on my other computer AND my CD-RW backup disk, it would have obviously been a bit dated(of a week, for now).

So am I really unlucky? Not at all. All these crashes were linked together, and now that this is fixed, I shouldn't have the same problem for quite a while. I'm back with a completely clean system, once again, and I'm not sad with this. In fact, for strange reasons, msgsrv32, a core Windows module, always crashed at midnight on my last system. I've tried to figure out why, but it seems that Windows has gone really far this time :) I even set my clock to 23:59:30 and waited 30 seconds for it to crash, and it did! Funny, but annoying ;) At least it doesn't do that anymore, so I prefer having a clean system now :)

For those waiting anxiously at ScAIEdit 3.1 to be out, you may have wondered why I haven't given any news these last days, and what I've been doing. This is due to 2 things : first, I've been really busy for few days, which disallowed me to work on the update. I won't go on details with this, you don't really care about that ;) And second, I've got my new video card I was waiting for a long time :) For those wondering, it's an Asus AGPV3800-TNT2/32MB. It's true, this is not the state-of-the-art, but I'm quite satisfied with it and compared to my old Voodoo1 4MB, it's a lot better :) So I took another few days to test it and play with it :)

As for ScAIEdit 3.1 progress, I've worked on it yesterday, and it's advancing well. I've gathered all expected changes in a TO-DO file, and I'm progressing on the list :) It shouldn't be too long before I release ScAIEdit 3.1, unless something unexpected happens again ;) Please be patient, you won't regret it! :)

Until next update!
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-12T03:08:51Z |title=The Gold Dragon reveals some informations... |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After a successful launch and appreciation from many people(thanks again :), ScAIEdit III is finally available to everyone. Unfortunately, some bugs have been found and I've been working hard to solve most of them, as only few are remaining now. One day after the release, I announced in the feedbacks there would be an update called ScAIEdit 3.01 very soon, fixing all reported bugs. Time has passed, and I didn't show any visible activity sign. What happened?

First of all, I took a little break of 2-3 days after the release to relax. I played Diablo 2 during that time ;) I guess it was a good time to take it, as I spent most of my computer time these last weeks to program ScAIEdit III, and I wanted to explore D2. This done(I've not finished D2, but played enough to be satisfied ;), this doesn't complete the time of my "public short inactivity". So what happened?

What if I told you that the "set_gencmd" command that must be put after "build" commands wasn't used for that purpose? That instead, the "set_gencmd" represented the build priority, and that it also applies to "upgrade" and "tech" commands? That the headers used in the beginning of scripts are clear and meaningful? That I now understand many problems that could have occured when making scripts? That there is no "unk[0xXX]" commands in the Developer Version I'm working on?

Nothing of what I said above is false. How am I able to reveal this? Recently, my friend -=|Artanis|=- told me he has been able to establish an internal contact with Blizzard, and sent me the contact information to this source. Thanks to him, I've been able to have a very interesting talk by e-mail with this source, and Blizzard was kind enough to provide me an unofficial list of the opcode names they've used to make their scripts. Thanks to this source and Blizzard for that really nice action :)

What does it means concretely? I've been able to assign the correct names to all of my defined opcodes, and even on some undefined ones! I still have to decipher these opcodes, but with a correct name assigned to them, this helps a lot :) It will take some time to find the definition of the undefined opcodes, because there is almost 20 commands that are never used in Blizzard scripts, but that can be used in the scripts. So I still have a lot of work to do :)

I'm sure many of you would want to see this list immediately, but please be patient. The list itself will be kept private for now, to avoid any big troubles with persons complaining they don't know what to do with it, any messes with unauthorized distributions of "opcode.dat" files, or anything related. I will keep you informed of the new commands I decipher, and you will get the same commands contained in the opcode names list on the next ScAIEdit III update. You just have to be patient, okay? :) This will actually be worth it, and the AI Script Tournament will just be even more interesting. **DO NOT** ask Blizzard about this. As written above, this is an unofficial and unsupported list, so for any questions on this, please direct them to my [e-mail](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org). Thanks in advance.

So the ScAIEdit 3.01 update is aborted, to leave place to the ScAIEdit 3.10 update. Expect many changes to come :) Just knowing that you won't deal with unk[0xXX] opcodes and "set_gencmd" anymore should make you happy, right? ;) I'll keep you informed!

It's a really fortunate turn of events :) I hope it will continue that way ;) That's all for now, see you on next update!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-07T14:42:01Z |title=The Gold Dragon unveils his newest software... |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It's not a dream. And I can still hardly believe it. But the day has finally come, and after 362 days of waiting, and countless hours of work, it can now see the light of the day. Drum roll, please!

*Drum Roll*

The third rewrite of the first StarCraft AI Editor ever made for public, ScAIEdit III, is now released! You've waited it for a long time, but now you're getting the result of your patience and support! :) With more than 15 new and fixed commands, a completely remodeled interface, and an help file to get you started quickly, it's definately the greatest ScAIEdit version you'll see. I have put lots(and lots) of time on this project, and I really hope you will appreciate it. Don't forget to give your feedback and comments! :) Here's the download link :

[ScAIEdit III](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=scaiedit3) (2.36 MB) - written by [Gold Dragon](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org)

This is the last rewrite of ScAIEdit you'll ever see :) It's highly probable there will be updates, but don't even expect a ScAIEdit IV. This third rewrite has been pretty long and I've put all my energies on it, so the last thing I want to do is to rewrite it again :) Now, with the help file, I hope I won't get the same basic questions over and over ;) Please read the help file before asking any questions, I haven't put more than 3 days of work on it for nothing :)

Thanks to my beta testers, who have given their time to hunt the bugs and giving suggestions for ScAIEdit III. I've been able to squash a good amount of bugs with your help, I really appreciate it :) You should re-read the help file and commands again though, there is some important changes from Beta Version 2.

On a side note, this was supposed to be released yesterday night. Everything was ready, until I had to upload it on the server. I'm on a 56k modem, but I was uploading at about 100 bytes/sec, with a very laggy transfer. This was totally unacceptable, so I tried many things but nothing helped to improve the speed. Very disappointed, I aborted the yesterday release, hoping that today would be better. It wasn't, and I was still uploading at 100 bytes/sec, with a very laggy transfer. I checked my ISP settings, everything was all right, and I remembered that the 56k technology isn't very reliable for uploading, as it only uploads at 33.6k, with unstability on certain cases. So I reconnected at 28.8k, I uploaded and... got a 2.3 kilobytes/sec upload transfer rate! If only I knew that yesterday... Anyways, now I know how to upload quickly, and you now have the software, so everything is good :)

Please give your feedback on the Feedbacks section of this post, or at my [e-mail](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org), I'll **really** appreciate :) The poll question will be updated in the next minutes, asking for your appreciation of ScAIEdit III, so vote for your choice :) Now go start writing your AI Scripts ;) And for those who laughed by saying ScAIEdit III would be out when StarCraft 2 is released, as I told you, I get the last laugh :) Have fun!
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-05T02:56:33Z |title=How do you want to crash today? |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sigh... You guessed right, here's another episode of "The marvellous world of crashes with computers".

There was an electricity storm outside, few hours ago. It wasn't too big, and I was too busy preparing ScAIEdit III release to keep my computer off anyway :P Then we lost electricity for a few seconds. I awaited it because I saved my files almost every minute ;) Then Norton Disk Doctor scanned my disk, really slowly... I don't know why it's so slow, but anyways. Then it found out 2 mistakes on my C: drive, one about a file in the C:\WINDOWS\TEMP dir, which was one of the HTML Help files of ScAIEdit III, kept there probably for some file swapping purpose. I still had the originals in their directory, so it's not bad at all. And the other was a log file of Outlook Express. Big deal. I made NDD correct these mistakes and after Norton finished his slow disk check, I got a big surprise. Windows was back again with its DOS error message, telling me VMM32.VXD is missing, once again!!(see my last crash episode to understand) I was particularly on the nerves, because I already knew something was messed up. I listed the directories and files on the C: drive, and obviously, it was badly messed up. I don't know why, but my C: drive has an annoying tendancy to crash since 1 or 2 months...

But I still smiled : that mad fool won't get me, because I backuped my C: drive on June 25th, so I only lost 9 days of file changing on the C: drive, and since I keep very little data on the C:, it's not bad. Well, it wasn't bad, until I thought of Outlook Express which has the bad habit of saving the e-mail files there. Remembering this, I no longer smiled. All the beta-testing e-mails and such are wiped now. Frustrating. So, I would ask everyone who sent me or received from me any e-mails on or after June 25th to resend it to my [e-mail](mailto:GoldDragon@camsys.org), so I can update it with the e-mails I received between the backup and crash time. I would be really glad about this. Don't be surprised if your e-mail isn't answered if you sent it in the last few days, unless you resend it again, okay? :) Thanks.

All in all, this bad adventure made me lost some precious time which was really needed to have a chance of releasing ScAIEdit III tonight. Now it's obvious I won't be able to do as I planned. So it's delayed, thanks to the crash. I worked on it all day to have a chance of releasing it tonight, and now I see the destiny playing a bad trick on me. But that's how life is composed of, sadly :P

But worse, I trusted Norton softwares for a long time. Now I see even them can crash my main partition. I just hope it won't happen again :( I'm deceived, so I'm going to reinstall Diablo 2, which has its character files on its own directory fortunately, and play it until I have to sleep. At least I'll finish my bad evening on a good tone :) Sorry everyone, but you'll have to wait a bit more... but you'll get it! :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-07-04T04:57:43Z |title=My apologies... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sorry.  I've done absolutely no work on any CamSys related project for the past few days.  But I have a valid excuse:  Diablo II =).  This game rocks.  I've been playing the Paladin and the Necromancer the most lately, and I've gotten them both up to level 13 and 15 respectively (I know, it's not that much, but I'm proud).

The good news (for you anyway)?  Andariel is whipping my ass =[.  My Necromancer can't even touch her; she kills off all of my spawned guys in like one shot, and then I have to run around taking pot shots with my wand (which actually does like 10-34 damage, but it's slow).  And poison damage really sucks =[.  I have to knock her down a little bit at a time, and the return to town to get more supplies.  The real problem is, the bitch keeps recharging her life.  Anyway, I'm getting really frustrated over it all, so I'll probably take a break get back to doing some programming tomorrow.  And for anyone who has any suggestions on how I should go about killing her, please do post them in the feedback (Omit the "You must suck" and such comments from your post though plz).

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-06-30T05:33:41Z |title=ScAIEdit III goes Gold! |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yes, it's finally true : ScAIEdit III reaches the Gold status! The beta testers have been contacted by e-mail to beta test ScAIEdit III and hunt the most bugs possible, so I can kill these annoying insects after receiving the reports.

It means an interesting thing : ScAIEdit III will be publically released in a few days! I won't give out the exact date of the release, in case the beta test period needs to be extended, but it shouldn't take too long. Meanwhile the beta test days, I will do opcode research to decipher even more commands, and I will also fix the bugs as soon as they're discovered. All in all, it's the beginning of the end ;) Your patience will be finally over in few days, and you will certainly get the greatest version of ScAIEdit :)

Btw, the archive size is simply surprising : it takes 2.2 megabytes. I will try to do something in order to shorten it up for the final release, because I think it's a bit too much :P But most of the size is taken by the CWAD module and Self-Executable CWAD information, so that's understandable. For possible future versions of ScAIEdit III after the initial public release, I'll make a seperate update file to reduce the download time. Anyways, ScAIEdit III is really close now. And it's sure :) Now will my e-mail get bombed of bug reports when I wake up tomorrow? :P
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-06-28T03:22:20Z |title=It is almost gold... |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I know, I said my next post should be about the announcement of ScAIEdit III going Gold. It could have been. But I always get other ideas at the last minute :P

I've been working hard these last days. First of all, I've been typing a lot, and it wasn't lines of code :) In case you haven't guessed, I've been working on the ScAIEdit III Help File. Yes, there will be one. The very first version of ScAIEdit having its help file ;) I spent a lot of time writing it(and I'm about 90% done on it, for "my" part) so I hope you will appreciate it. I have written 87 kilobytes of HTML files on it so far, and it's getting really long :) Now I expect to see the number of people wondering what to do to decrease dramatically or I think I'm going to burn something ;) Just kidding.

As for ScAIEdit III itself, there is also a lot of work done. I've done lots of improvements the HDD navigator, and it now contains most of the features of Windows Explorer : it can now cut/copy/paste/create/rename/delete files and folders :) On another side, I've been redesigning a bit the AI Script Files management. Now you will have to create your own directory and manage the scripts from there. Don't worry, a single little button does all the job, you only have to type a folder name ;) I could explain the changes of this system, but it's mostly technical related and I don't want to bother you :P Third, I completely finished the macro system, and it can now take parameters(it was planned but the code wasn't written yet). Fourth, few days ago, I've implemented a nice little feature. I called it "Instant Command Description", but beyond that complex name resides something really simple. If your keyboard cursor is over a command name, you only have to press F2 and a little box appears with an extended description of the command. It disappears as soon as you click or press a key in the text box. I really like this feature :)

There is very little left before ScAIEdit III goes Gold. I only have to finish and polish a few remaining features, complete the help file and make some vital internal tests. After this, ScAIEdit III will finally enter Gold and thus, the Public Release will be imminent :) If you wonder about the Help File, it contains many informations about script basics and a few advanced topics. I didn't have time to make a better design and I will still need some people to write documents about ScAIEdit III, so stay near :) But we'll look on this after ScAIEdit III is released, right? ;)

I've uploaded 4 new screenshots of ScAIEdit III, mostly showing the new features. I hope you like them :)

[The improved HDD Navigator](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/golddragon/scai3/scai3_shot11.jpg)

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-06-23T03:13:47Z |title=This is for soon. Really. |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It's been a little while, so I figured it might be a good time to post :)

I've made considerable progress on ScAIEdit III. Well, not on ScAIEdit III itself, but more on the opcode database. So far, there is 9 new deciphered opcodes over ScAIEdit II. My plan for ScAIEdit III is to get all important opcodes deciphered. It means there will still be some left, as they're really tough. And even though I deciphered them all, there would still be some left... I've been told there is opcodes that aren't used in the Blizzard scripts, meaning it's in the good old EXE file. Fun, eh? :) So that's why I'll take this objective instead, which is more realistic and less time consuming. Oh, you already want to know the new opcodes? Hehe, I could tell them right away, but I still have to make some final tests before classifying them as totally stable. Oh well, maybe I'll unveil a few, since you've been so patient for a long time :)

I've also took a new development decision : I will focus on ScAIEdit III itself from now on(rather than opcode deciphering). The reason is quite simple. The only thing to be done before ScAIEdit III goes gold is to make internal tests to eradicate pesky and obvious bugs(which would cripple the utility of beta testing). Beta testing will take time, in order to be efficient and report as many bugs as possible. Since new opcodes aren't needed for the beta test, I'll put all my energies in making ScAIEdit III gold, and meanwhile the beta testers check ScAIEdit III, I'll continue my job on opcode deciphering. This will be the most efficient way to spend time, I guess :)

I guess this is all for the progress update. Oh, you still want the new opcodes? You won't give up, eh? :) All right, here's a little gift :

[So this is how the computer differenciate ground and island maps...](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/golddragon/scai3/scai3_shot10.jpg)

Until next update... Next one SHOULD be the Gold announcement :) I hope.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-06-18T03:16:09Z |title=ScAIEdit III goes Silver! |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

No, my computer hasn't crashed badly this time :) It's really an ScAIEdit III update.

After reinstalling(read the post below for more details) my vital softwares to continue normally(Delphi 5, Hex Workshop, ICQ, WinAmp and yeah, StarCraft :), I've looked at ScAIEdit III and thought it was the time to make it Silver. What does it mean? Well it was Bronze since the beginning: the heavy development phase you've followed for the last few weeks. The Silver is the intensive test and opcode deciphering period, where the program has all the main, important features and many extras added, and is constantly retouched.

To start the Silver development, I spent today a lot of time on looking at the bwscript.bin scripts. As expected, there is a lot of treasures in there. Taking my good old blue pen and the Blizzard notepad that came with StarCraft(the very same tools that I used to start deciphering the opcodes, in February 1999 :) I looked on the unknown opcodes and tried to make sense out of them. Results : I've redefined a lot of unknown opcodes, this time with a correct definition and I managed to decipher a bunch of them. If you want to have an idea of the progress made, you can always look at this screenshot :

[The Nuke script almost deciphered completely](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043445/http://www.camsys.org/golddragon/scai3/scai3_shot9.jpg)

The pass to the Silver phase has another meaning : the beta test is really near. Tomorrow I plan to decipher the rest of the bunch of unknown opcodes I've isolated in bwscript.bin, and if I manage to get this done soon enough, will head to isolate and continue deciphering other opcodes, but this time in the Customs and Insanes scripts. I could let this down, but I really think it's important to have more knowledge on the script opcodes. It might delay ScAIEdit III public release, but in exchange, you will get an AI Editor with much more possibilities. What do you think? :) There still a lot to know and I want to get this done, at least most of it. You shouldn't see that much of unknown opcodes anymore :)

So that's basically what I'm doing. After I finish the deciphering process, there will be really little left. Mostly lots of internal tests to see if ScAIEdit III is stable, and if it generates the files well. Some time will be spent on optimizing the slowest modules, because there's still some improvements that can be made. I'll also write a basic help file on how to get started making AI scripts, and it will also contain a FAQ so I stop answering the same questions over and over ;) After this is done, it will be the Gold phase. I'll get my beta testers on work to extensively test ScAIEdit III on all features and sides, then after the eventual bug fixes, the day you wait : Release Time :)

All of this to say the software I've told you to wait for since a long, long time(almost 1 year now) is finally at a point of no-return, even though I "only" worked on it for the last few weeks :) You'll definately see it and my objective is to make it worth the wait :) I hope you'll appreciate the work I've put on ScAIEdit III, as I've devoted a lot of time to AI scripts and ScAIEdit III will be the final showing of this work. Thanks everyone for your support to have made it go so far, I couldn't have done it without you :)

Until next update...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-06-17T05:04:52Z |title=Long live to Windows! |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It was supposed to be an ScAIEdit III Update. It **WAS**. But it's incredible how unexpected things always come in to break your way. Here's the story :

It was a night like others. Few hours ago(10PM EST), I wanted to connect on the Internet to do my usual surf, chat with some people and post an update on ScAIEdit III since it's coming really, really good. I click on the Internet Connection icon I've put on the QuickStart bar on the task bar and it freezes. Nothing surprising, we're all so used to Windows crashes that it's not even funny anymore.

Not even surprised, I shut down my computer and restart it. My surprise started when I saw Windows loading in 640x480x16 colors, when I usually run on 1024x768x24bits and even more when it wasn't on Safe Mode. Few seconds later, that wonderful Blue Screen of Death came up with all sorts of useless stuff printed on(can someone really make use of random crashes data? :). Cool. I restart my comp once again, only to find out Windows telling me my Registry Base is scrap and I need to repair it using SCANREG. It was the first time I actually saw Windows warning me about something going wrong that badly. They've improved on 2nd edition ;) They improved it so much that I restarted my comp again after running SCANREG only to find out a unknown program has crashed, causing obviously the whole Windows to crash. Dammit. Restarting my comp another time, Windows doesn't even start and says vmm32.vxd is missing or unable to load. After restarting once again, this time in Safe Mode, the module VKD refuse to load and thus disallow me completely from entering Windows. Wow.

Big troubles. I picked up the Norton Rescue CD and executed Norton Disk Doctor. Result : The whole FAT of my C: drive is screwed up. Wow. Windows always like to do real, big mess, but I never knew it would go so far. It's a real chance I always make multiple partitions on my disks, and thus all my important data is always somewhere else than C:. But still, I've lost(and will be losing) a lot of time repairing a mistake I don't even know where it comes from. Ain't it wonderful?

So, in brief, all of this to say I have absolutely nothing installed on my comp. I only installed Win98 2nd edition, configured the Internet and now posting about my bad luck. Who would have guessed a simple click to connect on the Internet could actually reset a whole system in less than 2 hours and a half(mostly because I tried many times to recover my system before formatting everything)? *[irony]* I like Windows. That's why I want to say thanks to Uncle Bill for making all of us pay a lot of money for such a stable OS. It's really awesome. *[/irony]*

So if you want to know, I'll have to wait for tomorrow to make the ScAIEdit III progress update. With a bit of luck I'll be able to reinstall most of my important stuff tomorrow morning so I can get back to work on tomorrow afternoon. I can only be happy that none of my vital data has been affected by this bad event. I still had some backups on the other comp but they weren't all up-to-date. **[edit]**To remove any confusion, I only wanted to say that if I had lost any vital data, I could have still used my backups but they were not all up-to-date, so I'm really lucky that I haven't lost anything, else I would have lost some progress.**[/edit]** Sorry to haven't posted earlier, but I really planned to make an update tonight. Oh well...
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-06-12T02:23:40Z |title=The more it continues, the better it looks |author=Gold Dragon |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As you probably guessed, it's another ScAIEdit III Progress Update :) Time you get used to Gold Dragon ;)

First thing to say, this week-end has been quite productive on ScAIEdit III, as I've got many other features implemented. CWAD support, ASC3 files support on AI Compiler and Pre-Compiled AI Files are now added to the list. I can't hide the fact there's not much left, except some GUI improvement and little handy features. I also want to make a final test developing my own AI script to see if everything goes okay. I'm excited to announce the beta is now near :)

The only thing that could delay it would be the time I take to recreate the Blizzard bwscript.bin file so these script files can be easily read and used by ScAIEdit III, and also isolate correctly the unknown opcodes, because some of them aren't well defined causing some crash when reading them. This is planned to be done as the last of the features I do on ScAIEdit III before beta. And, unlike I expected, the beta will be the last step before the release. Cool, eh? :) Another obstacle that could delay another time the beta is the finals dangerously approaching. Yeah, I know many of you must have already finished school but please don't laugh, I'll be on your boat soon :) Finals start Tuesday afternoon for me, so a good amount of time will be taken off my available time to study these very important exams. They end Wednesday on the next week. So if you see I'm less active these times, that's highly probable it's because of this. Thanks for understanding.
