{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-31T23:27:00Z |title=ICE Tutorial |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Di (aka Magnus, aka JQpanG, aka whatever the hell else he's called =P) has completed an excellent tutorial on ICE for his StarCraft Editing Bible.  I was very impressed with it, and I recommend it to anyone who doesn't quite understand ICE.  It's very well written, and covers almost every aspect of the program.

[The StarCraft Editing Bible](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.campaigncreations.com/starcraft/bible/)

Thanks Magnus! =)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-31T22:15:00Z |title=ICE |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, since none of you are complaining to me about bug reports/requesting new features, I'll assume that this ICE version satisfied everyone's needs pretty well.  That being said, I think I'll officially make the existing build the final version of ICE in its current form.

Don't fret though; I haven't abandoned the project.  In fact, I'm doing just the opposite.  I've begun work on a brand new, completely rebuilt ICE.  This one will suppport FULL scripting capabilities, IE you'll now *write* code that controls animations, instead of manipulating it in the traditional 'box and button' manner.  I understand that this may be a bit of a challenge for users who don't have any programming experience, and I'm going to do everything in my power to make the program easy to learn.  ICE's language will be based off of C, so anyone with any amount of experience in C programming will be able to pick up on ICE's code very quickly.

The new ICE will be a MUCH more potent editing tool than it is currently, and I hope you'll all be just as happy with it as I assume you are with ICE now (I haven't heard any complaints =]).  Be on the lookout for a brand new ICE in the comming weeks!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-31T05:40:00Z |title=Tutorial News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I'm in the midst of making my first compiler. This is an assembly language compiler/decompiler for win95/98/nt. I'm creating it for use with the new StarGraft stuff (Action/Requirement type adding), and I thought that when I get it finished, it would serve well as a tutorial for people to follow. Not only would it teach you the fundamentals of the assembly language (which I didn't know until I started the compiler), it would also teach you how to make a primitive compiler on your own. I'm not quite sure when I'll get it done, perhaps by the end of the week I'll have a working version, and the tutorial a week or two after that.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-29T23:12:00Z |title=StarGraft News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A lot of people have been asking when the next version of StarGraft will be out (the one with the Action/Requirement Type Adding). My guess is that I'll have something ready to go within the next few days. I'm still working on refining the interface, in hopes that Ius gets all the features in that he needs in order to quickly produce new act/req types. What's probably going to happen is that you're going to need to request that Ius make the new Act/Req types for you; unless you have experience coding Assembly, it's not likely that you're going to be able to write the new act/req types on your own. All that you'll have to do is cut and paste a few lines of hex code (displayed as text) into the appropriate place in the app.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-28T02:42:00Z |title=ScAIEdit III Update |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As promised some time ago, here's the first update on ScAIEdit III.

After leaving this project in the darkness for months, it will now be reappearing in a third new version. I feel shameful to have left inactive ScAIEdit, because this project brought me here after all, and because I have left such a buggy version to the many users still using it. So to buy back my terrible mistake and restart on a complete new basis, ScAIEdit III has been started 2 weeks ago and is on development. With the new forum setup being almost complete, I now have more free time to work on ScAIEdit III. Many new features like CWAD support, ASC3 files, stability(wow, what a feature :), customization support, new opcodes, only to name a few. Thanks to everyone who sent me suggestions. So far I had fun tweaking the design, and you can have a rough idea of what ScAIEdit III is going to be like by clicking on the link below :

[ScAIEdit III on its early development stages](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/scaiedit3alpha1.jpg)

A lot of things will be added to what you see now, so don't be surprised if you don't find the script manipulation interface there :) But I think it's about it will look like in general. The background picture is going to be changed though :) This is only a little artwork I've done quickly, and I'll try to find someone to do a better one ;) I'll keep you updated on the progress of ScAIEdit III. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-27T04:12:00Z |title=Forum (hopefully) fixed |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've finally finished fixing the problem that occured 2 days ago on the new forums. The corrupted posts and accounts have been deleted and everything should be back to normal, with the exception that you will see some threads that have been wiped out, and those who had problems with their accounts will probably have to recreate them. To not create any injustice, the affected users will be able to e-mail me their approximate number of post they had before the problem and I'll boost their account to that number. Please send it **as soon as your account is recreated**. Don't try to fool me though. I visit the new forums frequently and I can easily know how active a user was :) But I trust you on this... I apologize for any inconvenience this problem has brought, but I'm sure you'll all understand that some things are out of our control sometimes...

Thanks to everyone who supported me meanwhile the fix, as well as those who e-mailed me and said the new board sucked, this really helped me too(this last part is sarcasm by the way). So here we go, forums are back online. I hope you'll recreate the missing threads :) I'll also try to do forum backups as many times as possible. Don't forget to e-mail me if your account got affected!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-26T04:29:00Z |title=Corruption nightmare |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm sorry I won't be able to keep my "promise" for the forum... I won't be able to finish fixing it up for tonight. If you would have seen what a corruption nightmare I had while trying to fix up, you would understand :) So all in all, the main Unknown Countries Board file has been corrupted(which means a simple reset), about a dozen of posts have been corrupted in each forum and few accounts too(about 12 in all), to summarize. I'll probably put a little something special to affected accounts in sort of compensation :) If it was a server crash, it was surely big.

But this situation won't reproduce again : I decided to make a backup of the whole forum each week or so to make sure I always have a recent copy of user accounts and forum posts, as soon as the forum is back online. So you can be sure that this problem won't be there anymore, if it would ever have happened again :) I repeat again it's not the fault of the script. So that's it... Unfortunately I won't be able to fix the forum until tomorrow night, because of school but you can be sure it's my #1 priority while online :) Thanks for your patience.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-25T22:49:00Z |title=Forum problems |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Very strange problems with the forum have happened today. It seems that some accounts have mysteriously been deleted(well, a 0 byte file), as well as other vital forum files for the Unknown Countries board. You can be sure I didn't touched anything on this, and I don't have a single idea why such a thing happened. Maybe a server crash could have corrupted some opened files, which would be possible and the best hypothesis yet. It really looks like file corruption. Since the script is protected with file locking, which makes file corruption virtually impossible, it can't be caused by the script. I don't have any other potential idea for the cause of what happened, maybe someone hacked the server but if it was the case, they would not only have "corrupted" these few files. Anyways, whatever is the cause one thing for sure is that it caused damage and I'll close the forums until I fix everything up, to prevent other loss of data. They should be reopened soon though, but these loss of data are unrecoverable, these apply to the few deleted accounts and the corrupted board, The Unknown Countries. :( I really apologize for what happened but some things are beyond my control. I just hope it won't happen again. Thanks to everyone who e-mailed me about this, I wouldn't have seen it so fast if you wouldn't have done so. By tonight you should see the BB active again. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-25T06:12:00Z |title=StarGraft News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well tonight I finally managed to scrape together a new version of SG (not publicly available yet) that lets people like Ius add in new Requirement / Action Types (i.e. add new things to the drop down lists in the buttons tab). What does this mean? Well for example, Ius programmed up a new Act Type that sets a switch based on the Act Var. So I now have a new button on my marines that sets switch one in all maps... and in one particular map that just happens to create reinforcements :) As soon as we have it debugged and stable I'll be distributing it... but keep in mind that for the most part, Ius is the only one who will be able to actually WRITE the code that you can insert in that section. But since the scripts will be made public, you can download them and paste them in their to use in your own TC's, etc.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-24T03:45:00Z |title=StarGraft Updated (Again) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Now that I finally figured out what was causing two major bugs that people were having (marines and/or the mouse being disabled) I've got those bugfixes in this new version, which also contains the ability to load custom string lists for icons and labels (so that you can add new training commands), as well as a search ability for quickly finding an item in the large list on the left side of the patch creator. This version appears to have solved all the previous bugs that have been reported, so hopefully it will be the last version I release for a while. The next features I have planned are related to Ius's work with adding Action/Requirement types, and that probably won't be done for another week or so.

Again my apologies for releasing yet _another_ version, but I thought it important that the bugs get fixed as soon as possible, now that I finally knew what was causing them :)

[Download StarGraft 2.1.7 Build 428](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=sg) [786kb] - by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-23T06:48:00Z |title=StarGraft Updated |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've updated StarGraft to fix several really annoying bugs that were introduced in the last version (which was in and of itself supposed to be just a maintenance release). This new version has two new features, however. The first is the support of MPQ Renaming (so that you can force SC to use your own MPQ's rather than the default), and the second is that it stores various information in the registry to make it remember what patch you last loaded. Both of these were requests from frequent SG users who had found a feature that really needed to be in there. Send me more requests and I'll satisfy them (as long as they're reasonable) as soon as I can.

[Download StarGraft 2.1.6 Build 415](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=sg) [760kb] - by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-22T22:02:00Z |title=Forum results |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, after almost three weeks of testing, here's the final voting results :

Some really wanted to change it, some also really wanted to keep the old one. Well, it's democracy, so the new board will be staying and the old one will probably be disabled or maybe kept in back. **[EDIT]** In my big hurry to post this because I had to go offline, I forgot to thank everyone who took their time to vote and to test the new forum. Thanks a lot! :) **[/EDIT]**

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-22T04:16:00Z |title=StarGraft News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just got the two major bugs fixed that were present in the last release (namely the inability to load multiple patches without the string lists getting corrupted, and the button adding errors). I sent it out to a few people to Alpha Test before I distribute it again, so hopefully I can catch most of the bugs before we have a repeat of the last release (a disaster).

Ius of Campaign Creations and the StarCraft Editing Resource has come up with a way to add new Action Types to StarCraft. He's got one right now where you can set/clear a switch by clicking a unit's button. Hopefully between the two of us we can work out some sort of way to script that sort of thing so that everyone has the ability to do something like that. In other words, new spells should be possible, given enough time on my part to research this new ability.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-21T02:46:00Z |title=ICE Release! |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

First off, I want to apologize for how poorly I've handled ICE so far.  I released a BETA that was extremely buggy, and never made a real attempt at doing fixes on it.  On top of that, I kept you guys in the dark on the status of the project throughout most of existance, and I regret doing that.  But, in the spirit of correcting past mistakes, THE NEW ICE IS OUT!

ICE v2.00R has finally been released!  Sporting a much more functional design, numerous bug fixes, and a plethora of new features, this release blows the BETA out of the water.  ICE now includes:

* Correct adding of new opcodes
* Correct adding of new animations to units
* Adding of new headers
* Delete current headers
* Renaming of headers
* FULL GRP previewing
* FULL WAV previewing
* Other miscellaneous previewing options
* Custom CWAD Support
* Much more traditional layout
* Increased ease of use in Anim Header editing

The one thing that this build DOES lack is documentation, but I favored a faster release.  I will have some kind of tutorial or something done soon, but for right now, just wing it =P.

Anyway, off to the downloads!

[Download ICE v2.00R](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=ice) [656kb]

And, as always, make sure to [Tell me](mailto:kramerboy@camsys.org) what you think!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-21T02:30:00Z |title=Hammered |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well my first week of classes is nearly at an end (thank god). Unfortunately the work does not stop tonight (as I had hoped). I have around 100 pages worth of history reading to do, some physics stuff, and some writing. Strange how I don't have any work for my major (computer science), isn't it? I think that what's going to happen is that I'll be able to make occasional posts during the week, but that the bulk of my work is going to be done on the weekend. I've got a terrible schedule this semester because unlike last semester I actually have to be on campus from 8-5 every day. I'm a commuter so that makes it worse. It's not like I can quick go back to my dorm room in between classes to work on CamSys while I have a minute... I'm guessing that my writing and history courses especially are going to be taking a lot out of me. All of my courses are unfortunately required. I've got a full load and have to take them all this semester. Hopefully I will manage to salvage some time for the weekends, but I'm not entirely sure. As it turns out I probably won't be able to even work at a job I was supposed to start next week, because I have so much to do and so little time to do it in. Oh well. Hopefully next year it will get better (and I won't have any more damned english/wirting stuff)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-17T19:28:00Z |title=Programming Tutorial Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I decided that it was about time I wrote another programming tutorial. This one is supposed to be part of the Game Editor Programming Unleashed series (which I will undoubtedly rename to something more general, given that the table of contents implies that once you've read everything there you'll be able to do a lot more than program game editors). I skipped ahead to the more interesting stuff - the first section in chapter 8: Windows Programming. It requires Borland C++ Builder, so if you're interested you'll need to purchase it. It's a very worthwhile investment if you want to begin windows programming, I highly reccomend it. Anyways, I will probably work on a few more today. My guess is that you'll find that I will be jumping around in the topics that I'm covering, and that I'll be changing the table of contents quite a bit :) In fact I've been thinking what I'll do is instead do a general Intro to Windows Programming course followed by a seires of tutorials based on requests from you guys on what you want to have explained. So if you've already mastered the basics of windows programming, by all means let me know what you would like to see me cover in the advanced areas!

[Intro to Windows Programming](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/programming/c08s01.htm)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-16T06:10:00Z |title=Thanks for the votes. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

StarDraft just won the Infoceptor Best Program Award (StarGraft came in second, followed by Arsenal III), and I won most dedicated webmaster (although you'd never know it the last couple of days since I've been programming and learning a lot during that time period :). {{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-16T06:05:00Z |title=Arsenal and StarGraft Progress Report |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Arsenal news:

Many of you have been asking me about a new release for Arsenal III - I do have one planned, in fact I may start work on it tomorrow. I won't have CWAD support in yet (that will have to wait at this point until BlizzDraft is done), but I will have several improvements, foremost of which will be the addition of several new variables to edit, including for the first time the unit sounds. I may also try to work on implimenting Adding new units, weapons, etc. This would require many changes to the StarCraft EXE, all of which StarGraft _should_ be capable of eventually. I'll be tying StarGraft and Arsenal III closer together as time goes on because they're really inseperable for most editing projects.

StarGraft:

I managed to get AutoComplete mostly working for the string and icon lists. It's not perfect but it's better than scrolling through the entire list trying to find what you were looking for...  I've also fixed (I hope) most of the bugs that were in the last version that I released, most of them were related to the Insert/Delete features. My apologies for them but these kind of things generally don't show up until a lot of people start using the product, usually in ways I never imagined :) I hope to have a new release ready tomorrow.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-13T06:52:00Z |title=StarGraft Updated |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

That Group Removing thing is really complex, and unfortunately I couldn't get it into this release. This new release does however have several really important updates and bugfixes, which is why I went ahead and released it anyways.

Most important is support for inserting/deleting buttons from button and requirement groups. This means that you can easily add one (or more) extra requirements onto the Marine, without having to create a whole new group.

I've also made the Patch Creator and Loader multithreaded so that they're a bit faster to load things, and the interface doesn't lock up / disappear while it's loading patches. I also fixed several important bugs, especially one where it would fail to patch the Status Bar changes. I also added a How Do I...? Help section to the Patch Creator to help answer some common questions people were having. All in all a very important update, and one I highly reccomend that you download.

[Download StarGraft 2.1.4 Build 372](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=sg) [750kb] by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-13T01:12:00Z |title=StarGraft News Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

By popular demand I'm adding support for Insertion and Deletion for existing button and requirement groups. That should make it much easier to modify the existing buttons and requirements, rather than trying to replace them with completely new groups (which for requirements usually didn't work). Both of those are complete, and I'm working on Group Removal right now...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-12T08:09:00Z |title=Movie Review: The Green Mile |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I hadn't planned on seeing the Green Mile tonight (which is why I thought I was going to have the new StarGraft done - sorry for the continual delays - yes I do want it done as much as you). I originally thought I was going to go see Galaxy Quest which was supposed to be very funny, but the friend I was going with changed his mind and wanted to see the Green Mile instead, so we went to see that. I had planned on saving it for my "girlfriend" but lately we've had trouble getting in touch, so I caved. I must say that they've done a fabulous job with this movie. I came very close to crying at least two if not a half dozen times. And yet I wouldn't call it a sad movie. It has many humorous scenes, almost all of which involve the incredible actor who plays Wild Bill (an inmate on the Green Mile). I don't think there's a single lead actor in this movie who isn't giving his best performance ever. In fact, this movie really shows off the talents of quite a few new faces to most people, and for all of them I will always associate them with their Green Mile characters. The Green Mile is pure and simple a masterpiece. Everything that I loved and respected about Shawshank Redemption is here, multipled tenfold in most cases, and mixed in with many new intriguing elements. It's not a stretch to draw such a comparison. After all, both movies were based on books by the same author - Stephen King. Both movies share the same director, and even an actor. Both are about a miracle that occurs in a prision - as Tom Hank's character says, it's a place that makes a miracle that much more incredible. If this movie doesn't win some goddamned awards I'm going to be incredibly pissed off at whoever decides to ignore this magnificent movie. Which reminds me, WTF is up with The Talented Mr. Ripley? Apparently that movie has gotten a lot of critical acclaim, and several nods for the big winner at all the awards shows... I'm sorry but that movie looks incredibly strange (not in the excellent unique sense that Being John Malkovitch was - which also deserves a lot of awards), but in the sense that I couldn't help thinking as I was watching the preview - was this whole movie just a rather lame excuse to get Matt and Gwenyth together? End Rant :P
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-07T17:00:00Z |title=TileEdit update time. |author=Nossirahdrof |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, after sitting on it for a few days I've finally decided to actually release a new version of TileEdit. It adds a few new features that I hope will be useful to everyone. Hopefully this version will be bug free and not need an update, but the additions in this version are tiny compared to the importing/exporting/optimization that was added in the last release, so hopefully there won't be any bugs. Here is a list of the new features, just off the top of my head:

* Added 'Make Blank' button to make MegaTiles truly blank, not just black Tiles.
* Added 'Apply Type To All' buttons to allow for quick changing of all 'types' in a MegaTile, or in all MegaTiles
* Added feature to edit Doodad Placeability. (be sure to go through the readme.txt for an explanation)
* Added different names for Tile Groups to quickly identify Blank Groups, Main Groups, Edge Groups, and Doodad Groups.
* Added palette saving in either .pal or .wpe palette files.
* Labeled Unknowns by what they are believed to do for quick reference. (saves from opening readme.txt all the time.)

Well that's about it. Remember to read the README.TXT file so you know how to use everything and what values you should use for what. [Feedback](mailto:nossirahdrof@yahoo.com) and bug reports are welcome. So here is the download. And to take a page out of King Arthur's book, I'll let you know the build number. :-)

[Download TileEdit 2.1; Build 140](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=note) [381kb] - by Nossirahdrof
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-07T05:22:00Z |title=StarGraft Bug Notice |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

There is a bug in the new version of StarGraft which could end up messing up your button changes because of the new way I am patching the data now. It shows up if you edit a button that's not the first button in a group, and make those changes before editing the first button. In other words, if I changed the Barrack's Firebat button (which is not the first button) and left the marine alone, the patch wouldn't work. My sincere apologies for this error, it should be fixed tomorrow. I also intend to have button and requirement deletion in the next version as well, since that was requested by a lot of people.

If the next version works flawlessly, then I had planned to start work on WarGraft, that is if there's really a high demand for it. At this point there's no file patching utility for War2 BNE (although Merlin's BlizzDraft will support it when it's finished) except for MPQ replacement. To be quite honest I'm not quite sure how many people would find such a utility useful... I don't know how many people play BNE, or how many people would be interested in making customs for it if we made the utilities available. So if you are interested, please let me know. If I don't hear from anyone in a few days, then I'll assume nobody really wants to use it and won't bother making it.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-05T17:47:00Z |title=A pleasant distraction... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

If you stood over my shoulder and watched what websites I frequently visit, foremost among them would be [Rush Magazine](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.rushmagazine.com/). This extremely high quality website is the brainchild of former Simple.Craft webmaster Lockeye, so you can tell where most of the humor, wit, and the wonderful images come from :) I've been thinking of stealing a few pictures here and there from their excellent pictorials for use in my programs :) For those of you concerned that these pictorials are of the risque nature, you needn't be alarmed. They're forced by their host to keep em clean. That doesn't mean they're all of prim and proper women tho :P You'd be amazed at how little can be left to the imagination while still being legal to display :P In any case, this is where I spend most of my free time (what's left of it anyways), and relax/unwind after a long day of programming. I hope you enjoy Rush as much as I do :)

[Rush Magazine](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.rushmagazine.com/)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=2000-01-04T22:53:00Z |title=New forum possibility |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I found recently a new forum script on the web, [UltraBoard](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.ultrasupport.org/). I immediately liked it, for its good design, its wide flexibility and offers lots of options. Best of all, it's FREE! :)

Some may like it, some may dislike it... That's why we won't force the forum change, and offer you the possibility to choose between the [old one](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_gen/), which is still active, and the [new one](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/cbbs/UltraBoard.pl), which currently only have The Round Table board, for test purposes. Feel free to register on it and try different options, so you can make a good decision about it. Please keep your same nick if you register :)

I've modified the script a bit in order to improve the layout, and I'm also currently improving it so it'll hopefully support auto-login feature via cookies as an option, and some other little things... Personally I like that script a lot, for being free and offer so much features. Please tell whether you like or dislike the new board, via the [old forum](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_gen/), the [new one](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043458/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/cbbs/UltraBoard.pl) or via [e-mail](mailto:dkknight@quebectel.com). If you dislike it, please tell what are the reasons leading you to this, so I can possibly improve the board which would maybe satisfy you :)

Have fun! :) I'll post later about the final "results"...
{{end-newsitem}}

Unfortunately due to laziness on my part and some question as to how I should properly do the "button adding" part, this StarGraft Update does not contain the adding feature I had promised. I plan to work on the adding part for next friday, although if all works out well I should have it done much sooner than that. Since I had no bug reports from the last version of StarGraft, this version doesn't fix anything, except maybe speeding up some loading times.

Oh yeah and I guess it's almost the new year :) Have a good one.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-31T03:02:00Z |title=Mac customs |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You remember what I wrote in my last post, that hopefully some persons will take care of SC customization on macs? Well, not only it's true, but it seems that it already started! I've received an e-mail today from OoklaTheMok that he is running a website for mac customs, located at [http://customs.macstarcraft.com](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043505/http://customs.macstarcraft.com/). {{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-30T20:15:00Z |title=Camelot's SC editors and Macs |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After receiving my 30th(rough approximation, but I should approach this number :) e-mail asking me if our SC softwares are or will be compatible with Mac, I'll answer this publically so hopefully I won't receive any of these e-mails for a long time :)

Unless we specify it, none of our SC softwares are compatible with Mac(unless you use an emulator or something like that, but I won't enter into such things), and we don't plan to make them mac-compatible. The reason is simple : None of us(as far as I know) owns a Mac, or knows how to port our programs to Mac, or is interested into it. Maybe some of the mac users may be disappointed about that, but we can't do everything, you know :) Hopefully there will be other persons that will do a StarDraft for mac :)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-30T07:46:00Z |title=StarGraft News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just completed adding support for Self-Exectuing Patches (which can include SECWADs, too if you select one). This ability will be added into the next version of StarGraft, which looks like it should be done sometime tomorrow if all goes well. The Self-Executing Patches will be at least 170kb in size, which will grow depending on whether you have a SECWAD or not, and the size of the patch file itself.

SEPatches will only support the versions of SC that the creator of the patch has support for. I.e. a copy of versions.dat will be tacked onto the end of the SEPatch for use in loading the patch. However that file will only be as up to date as the version the patch creator had. In this way I suppose you could do multiple versions, which would be especially helpful for SC/BW versions, which for the most part are incompatible with each other because of the drastic # of changes involved in the EXE.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-30T02:24:00Z |title=SunForge |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

This is the last time I'm posting about anything related to SunForge (or even Tiberian Sun), and I'm only doing so now because I'm sick of people asking. I'm finished with Tiberian Sun and SunForge. Do not bother asking me for the source for SunForge or to help me on it - the project won't be updated by anyone else and the final version that there will ever be has been available for download for a month or two.

All further emails/forum messages regarding Tiberian Sun will be deleted/ignored, respectively.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-30T02:03:00Z |title=Downtime Explained... and a StarGraft News Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

For those of you wondering (as I had been up until yesterday) what happened to our domain (and for most of you our website, too) www.camsys.org - apparently our hosts never paid for it, and so InterNIC decided to take it down on the 24th. Those of you who still used our old address - camelot.warzone.com - were able to get into the site, but found that most of the site didn't really work. For all those of you who emailed me telling me to correct the links that had pointed to www.camsys.org to camelot.warzone.com, the links were always correct, but because the domain didn't work and your bookmarks were old, a lot of things didn't work. So you should all make sure that you're visiting www.camsys.org now rather than camelot.warzone.com, since the older url is merely a backup and redirects most things through the domain url.

In any case things have been restored now so that the domain works (and so should everything else). My apologies that this happened, but it was totally beyond my control.

In StarGraft news, a large # of you also asked me why StarGraft was never released last Friday. Well since the domain was down and I figured that just about all our viewers visited through that address, there wasn't any point to explaining what happened.

What did happen was that last week I just had too much else that was going on to actually work on SG at all. I didn't start work on it until today when I improved a few more things in the interface. I will hopefully add in support for adding new buttons and requirements sometime tonight or tomorrow. If it's not done by tomorrow, don't expect a release before Monday, because I'm not entirely sure the website will be available from Friday until then.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-23T17:44:00Z |title=Updates |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

First off, ICE Release 1.00 is comming up very shortly.  I've gotten a lot of new stuff programmed into it, a lot of the old bugs programmed out of it (Which I've probably replaced with some more updated bugs =)), and basically made it a much better program than it was before.  I'm in the final stages of programming, and I'm basically just tieing up loose ends before I get the thing out for release.

Secondly, I GOT MY NEW COMPUTER!  And it's about time!  We first went with Gateway (Parent's choice, not mine).  To make a long story short, they sent us the wrong computer 3 TIMES, and lied to us repeatedly over a period of about 3 weeks.  Needless to say, I was very irrate over that, and finally we just said screw it, and ordered a Dell.  In one week, we got a better system for a cheaper price.  The thing just arrive today, and man does it own =P.  Should make programming a lot less maddening, because I'll no longer have to wait 3 minutes while the damned thing compiles... So I guess we all benefit from this =).

And lastly, the bad news (for you anyway).  I just recently got a job over at the local movie theater, meaning I won't have as much time to concentrate on programming as I used to.  I'm hoping that you guys won't be able to notice the difference, but please understand if I get behind on some of my work.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-22T03:48:00Z |title=The return of an old project |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

All right, I've been delaying it, delaying it, over and over, again and again, and so on, and some wondered what happened to ScAIEdit. It's true that it has been a bit more than 5 months that the project has gone inactive, I'm really not proud of it, but I got involved in D3 softwares and related, so I put ScAIEdit on standby for a while to put my time on these.

But seeing how ScAIEdit still gets downloaded(average of 80 downloads per day), and the demands/wonders/wtfs I've received from many people to finish ScAIEdit, and how many times I said I would do it "sometime soon"(which is pretty unclear, but I like it :), well I finally decided to come back and finish it. Even more when I saw other CS staffs asking about it :) Since I want to finish that project that changed "the flow of my Internet life"(I don't like that expression, don't ask me why :) ), I have to do it now :)

In short, I'll rewrite(again) ScAIEdit from scratch, leading it to its third version. Seeing how buggy the second was, I don't even want to see that code again :) (I'm about to move the source folder to recycle bin, but I won't :) This time will be the right time(hopefully), meaning no major bugs and the like. Before starting officially the project, I'd like to hear your suggestions/ideas you would like to see in ScAIEdit III. This is the RIGHT time to do it, because once it'll be started, I'll probably only add features if they're easy to do. This will be more complex now that everyone is on the idea of replacing the SC units and adding commands, heavy thanks to StarGraft, which will complicate my life but hey, that's why I'm here, having fun coding thousands of lines of code :)

I already got a few ideas for ScAIEdit III, but I want to hear yours first before announcing anything. So please, [e-mail](mailto:dkknight@quebectel.com) me your suggestions, start a thread on our forums, do whatever you want so I can read it :) I really need it to make this final version the best possible. Thanks for your time!

Now, I've got to reinstall StarCraft I guess :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-21T16:49:00Z |title=StarGraft (and a bit of Arsenal III) news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

For all of you eagerly awaiting the StarGraft version that lets you add commands, requirements, etc, I'm going to try to get everything done for a Friday the 24th release (making it quite an Xmas present). I have released an internal beta to a few of the CS staff here that has 2 new command bars (but no provision to add anymore yet), and the testing has gone very well with that so far. It looks as though there will be an upper limit of around 4000 new buttons (not groups, just total # of buttons). This depends on the version of StarCraft that you're using, however.

I've read a few forum threads where people are complaining that the computer doesn't seem to use the new abilities that the human players have access to. All I can think of is that the AI Scripts probably need to be rewritten. When they were written before they were written with the understanding that the commands were in their original state, and they had no idea that you'd be able to train a civilian at a supply depot. I'm pretty sure it's the AI scripts that need to be changed - I can't see anywhere else in the EXE that it's storing that sort of data. I suggest that someone familiar/skilled with SCAIEdit try to play around with that sort of stuff. For example getting the computer to train the civilian that Ace explained how to do in his tutorial.

I'd also like to commend Noss for his excellent work on TileEdit. He's just as enthusiastic about it as I am about StarGraft, and I can assure you that some truly astounding things are possible if you use all of our tools together :)

I had thought that Merlin might have a release ready for the new Draft before he left on vacation but that's not going to happen because he's already left... I should mention that I'm going to start re-investigating CWAD support for Arsenal III sometime soon (probably next week). It turns out the code I had written for CWAD support had several major flaws in it that I recently spotted that hopefully were the only reason it wasn't working.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-20T17:44:00Z |title=TileEdit News Update. (not a release yet though) |author=Nossirahdrof |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, there's some good news and some bad news from the world of Tileset Editing. First, the bad news.

I have recently figured out (although I should have caught it earlier) that there is a limit on the amount of graphics that can be put into a Tileset. I won't go into great detail about why, mostly because the explanation would be long and probably more than most people would understand. So for now, the ability to import tiles is somewhat limiting. Which brings me to my next news bit, this time the good part. I have successfully completed the Exporting and Importing abilities of TileEdit. There are still a few minor bits and pieces to work out, but essentially it works.

So combining the bad news and the good news, we get the fact that Tiles can be imported to the Tileset, but once the limit is reached, that's it.

So, what shall be done? Well, in the next few days I'm going to try and create some kind of Tileset optimization to take away minitiles that have doubles or flipped doubles (probably the same technique Blizzard uses to reduce file size). This should help in lowering the size of the Tileset files once you've imported new graphics. Hopefully with this, the limit will be reached only in rare circumstances, but it will always be there.

So when can the next release be expected? Hopefully if all goes well, by Christmas I'll have a new version out, one way or another. If I find that the optimization will be more difficult than I am estimating, then I'll release a version without it, but hopefully, I'll get it put in so it'll be a much better release.

Finally, here's a little sample of something I've extracted from the Twilight Tileset. It took me a very short time to extract and assemble the tiles into this image of the Twilight Bridge. Here ya go.

{{img |file=/web/20000818043505im_/http://www.camsys.org/images/TwiBridge.jpg |title=Twilight Bridge}}
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-18T03:15:00Z |title=StarGraft Tutorial |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ace Calhoon (an MVP on our forums) has written up a very detailed tutorial on how to get a new unit to train. You can take a look at it [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043505/http://www.camsys.org/tutors/SGTutor.txt). Contact Ace if you have any questions about it (his email is all over the forum).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-17T05:45:00Z |title=StarGraft Updated (again) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

In any case, I've now got the bugfixed version completed, with a few new features: namely support for running Self-Executing CWAD's or for running normal StarDraft patches (run StarDraft, select your patch and start up SC, then run SG with your patch, or do it in the opposite order, won't matter). My apologies for the bugs, and thanks very much to everyone who helped me find them (Ace, DI, Ius, and Noss especially).

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-16T05:15:00Z |title=Greetings and TileEdit released |author=Nossirahdrof |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, this being my first news post here, I just want to say a big hello to everyone. But now down to business. I have been busily learning C++ while trying to make the program I've been wanting to make for the last year or so. Since a year ago is about when I first started researching the Tileset Files. So at long last I've completed the first release version of TileEdit. It is now a seperate program from Arsenal3 and has a few more features than the one in Arsenal3. Graphical editing is still in it's infancy but hopefully not for long. So I guess that's all, I hope you all like it and give me some feedback and report bugs if you find any.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-15T15:50:00Z |title=More StarGraft News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've decided to retract my earlier statement that the next vesion of StarGraft will only support Brood War 1.07. Initially that will still be true but I'm implimenting a more versatile system this time which will load all necessary data to patch a particular version from an ini file, which can then be edited by 3rd parties (or myself if I feel up to it) to support other/future versions of StarCraft. That way anyone can add support for new/other versions of StarCraft so that I don't have to worry about doing that stuff.

In fact the patch file itself will probably end up being an ini file as well, so that if people are unsatisfied with the Patch Creator I provide they can create their own and still use the Patch Loader, which is now the heart of the program.

Because I keep tinkering to add new features and better version handling, etc I may not have it ready for friday, but I certainly hope to have it done tonight (as odd a statement as that may be).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-14T17:16:00Z |title=StarGraft News Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sorry things have been quiet around here the last few days. I took a break this weekend from SG so that's why I haven't said much :P I started up work again yesterday and made a lot of progress. With any luck I may have it done for tonight, but it'll probably be done wednesday. This time I plan to send it to the rest of the CS staff before I release it publicly to hopefully clear up any bugs that I miss. So that means in all likelihood that you won't see it until Thursday or Friday but hey that's still not bad :)

I should note that you won't have to worry about backing up your StarCraft exe anymore because the new system will be all patch oriented. There will be a Patch Creator (the old StarGraft) and a Patch Loader (think StarDraft). Initially SG and SD are going to be incompatible (i.e. you won't be able to use both of them at once) but when Merlin finishes the new StarDraft that should no longer be an issue since they should be integrated... Although how far off that is I have no idea. What I may end up doing eventually is just providing you with a StarCraft.exe replacement that you can normally run to select what patch you want, and then when StarDraft starts it up it will run whatever patch you had selected. A hack to be sure, but it'll be better than nothing I suppose :)

I'll try and keep you all posted on what's going on.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-11T02:37:00Z |title=Bad news |author=Merlin |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I realize most of you don’t even know I exist anymore, but I thought I’d update you on what is going on :) I have begun work on a flat rewrite of StarDraft – to include support for SC/BW v1.07, WarCraft II Platinum, and possibly other games. The rewrite will take quite a bit of time because of both the sheer complexity of the app and the fact that I want to take my time to make this new update worth it, so please bear with me for a little while. An alpha will be released no earlier than a month from now and a patch for the current StarDraft (to add 1.07 compat) is scheduled for sometime in the next week and a half. If you are interested in the improvements in the new version, here is a tentative list:

* Support for WarCraft II Platinum
* New user interface
* Performance Fixes
* More reliable patching of SMK/WAV files (unreliable at the current time)
* Support StarGraft
* Greatly improved GRP editor

I will keep you all informed of my progress :)

-- Merlin
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-10T13:42:00Z |title=Bad news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I know I had promised to get a new release of StarGraft out today but as the hours tick away that's looking less and less likely. I don't really know what I was thinking when I expected to have it done by the end of today, what with me being so busy with other stuff. So I'm delaying the release until next friday. I don't have any exams at all next week (2 the week after tho) so hopefully I should have plenty of time available to get the program completed. I probably could have something done tonight but it wouldn't work very well. Sorry to keep everyone waiting but I feel bad after the last two bug ridden releases, so I want to take my time with this one instead of rushing it out.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-10T02:34:00Z |title=StarGraft Multiplayer news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

That makes things a whole lot easier on me because that's much less crap that I have to worry about keeping track of. So as of right now, SG will only support the most current version of SC/BW. I may not have SC 1.07 support ready for friday - it will probably just be BW 1.07. But I do plan to have the ability to add buttons in there if I can pull it off.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-08T16:11:00Z |title=N/A (most likely) for the rest of the week |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

My apologies but it looks like I'll be unavailable for the rest of the week. It's the last week of the semester so my professors are all expecting us to turn in assignments that they procrastinated giving us. Actually I do have most of that work done, but I spent about 8 hours last night on it, and 3 hours this morning, getting virtually no sleep. So tonight I'll be turning in early, and tomorrow I have to do another program that's due friday. That's fine when you're in the corporate world and don't have several other courses all expecting the same thing, but for school it's a little ridiculous. I.e. the assignment I worked on last night turned out to be 20 pages. Almost all of it was my own code that I wrote just last night. (plus I cheated and didn't put any comments in there).

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-06T23:41:00Z |title=Tragedy strikes... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm going to ask you all to take a few minutes of your time and read the article that I have pasted in below. It was sent to me by a classmate of mine, and when I read it I was enraged enough to see the need to post about it on this site, because frankly I'm not sure the true story will ever get out if I don't. This ghastly tale makes me ashamed to be an american citizen, and horrified that the people who really instituted this riot are members of the human race. I've been deluded for years in thinking that everything would work out alright if I just sat back and let other people do things for me. Well unfortunately it seems a lot of other people had the same opinion because that attitude has helped to make this atrocity become a reality.  I strongly urge you to read this article and to pass along its message to everyone you can. We're being decieved here by our government "of the people, by the people, and for hte people" and everyone deserves to know that.

For those of you who are upset with me taking up this space with non Camelot Systems news, I suggest you read the quotes at the bottom, particularly the one by Shaw.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-06T17:03:00Z |title=Multiplayer-capable StarGraft coming soon. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm very pleased to announce that after initial tests today attempting to log into Battle.net with a new StarGraft patching system, it appears that Battle.net will not disconnect me until I attempt to do something in a game that the other player can't do.

I've also thought more about the system that I'm working on now and I think that it should be possible to patch files based on what map you have, or even change the files during the middle of a map. That would enable you to "fake" upgrades by forcing it to load a new copy of arr\units.dat which has better stats or something... I just have to find how to detect trigger executions, and then it should be possible.

Right now the possibilities are quite staggering... For instance you could eventually have a trigger which acted sort of like a volcano and morphed the map completely. You could have it change tilesets, do whatever you want. It's pretty amazing what can be done, but given time pretty much anything should be possible.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-05T15:59:00Z |title=ICE |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'd just like to thank you guys for all feedback I've gotten about ICE.  Your thoughts are always welcome, though I think I've come to a decision on the future of the project - I will continue it, and I will get the advanced stuff worked into it.

Almost every email I recieved did ask for one thing, however - a tutorial =).  I guess I expected that you would all know as much about iscript as I did when making ICE, and I apologize for that assumption.  In light of this, I'll be taking a time-out from programming today to write up a comprehensive tutorial on ICE and iscript.bin.  Hopefully this will end most the confusion surrounding the program and its purpose.

Also, I had a number of people complaining of bugs in the program.  Unfortunately, none of them gave me any information on where the bug occured or on how to duplicate it.  Without this knowledge, I cannot correct my error.  So, please, if you do locate a bug, make sure to report it to me, and to tell me its causes and effects.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-05T06:35:00Z |title=Incredibly good news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've decided to delay the release of the next version of StarGraft until tomorrow. No that's not the good news. The good news is the reason for doing so: I've discovered the location in the exe of the data structures pertaining to units, sounds, images, etc. This means that with any luck I should be able to make it so that you can add as many things as you want to each of those sections :) But that means I'll have to update Arsenal III :P oh well...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-05T05:56:00Z |title=KA Rules ... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

.. I'm just surprised we're not getting chewed out for saying that CS would never make a command editor a while back =).

Anyway, if you'll all take a time-out from your EXE editing right now, I'd really like to hear some feedback on ICE.  Hell, I'd like to hear any feedback =).  Good?  Bad?  I dunno, none of you have told me =).  No one's given me their opinion of the project, so I'm not sure how/whether to continue.

This can go one of two ways, and I have no problem with either..  First, if you guys liked ICE, and think that it's a worth-while project to continue, I'll release another BETA within the next few days, which enables all of the basics, and I'll have a release version ready after that, that has all of the advanced options.  Otherwise, if you guys don't think it's worth it, or if you do like the program, but are going to be too busy using StarGraft, I'll do just the opposite - I'll finalize the code that I have now, and release it as the final release version of ICE.  Then I'll go play with SG just like the rest of you =).  Either way is fine with me, but I do need to hear from you guys.  [Drop me a line](mailto:kramerboy@warzone.com), and let me know what you guys think.

That is all, you may now continue your EXE hacking =).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-05T03:12:00Z |title=New version of StarGraft soon! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm very pleased by the huge outpouring of support and praise for StarGraft, and I want you all to know that I'm working on improving it right now to make it easier to use and capable of more. I hope to simplify the interface and add the patch feature for the next release. I also have fixed the support for all versions right now. Turns out that the reason that it wasn't supporting 1.04-1.06 was that it couldn't tell the difference between the Starcraft and Brood War versions. This error has been corrected now. I should mention that patches will have to be made in two seperate versions - one for after Brood War was released (that will be compatible with Brood War AND StarCraft) and one for pre 1.04 StarCraft. Although I would imagine that very few people still play StarCraft pre 1.04. I hope to have the new version available later tonight or tomorrow morning.

Oh and thank you to everyone who sent me in their 1.07 exe's. Even though they were all Brood War versions (I had asked for StarCraft only versions, I thought) it turns out it doesn't make a difference since I had already supported 1.07 SC anyways :P
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-03T02:36:00Z |title=Quick Request |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You may remember that I asked for all versions of StarCraft's EXE's about a month and a half ago - I had then said it was to fix StarDraft when it was in fact to help me add support for all StarCraft versions to StarGraft. My apologies for decieving you but I wanted it to work really well before I released it (oh yeah and there was the Blizzard approval thing - note that doesn't mean they authorize it. It just means they won't sue me for making it). In any case, I have support for all versions in there now except StarCraft (not Brood War) 1.07. So if someone who has SC 1.07 could send me their EXE I'll add that support in. Other than that all versions are supported. (And I will continue to add support for new versions as long as I can).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-02T23:53:00Z |title=StarGraft Released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

StarGraft (the secret util I promised earlier) is now available to the public. StarGraft is an EXE Editor which allows you to change unit commands, requirements for units to be built, technologies to be used/researched, and upgrades to be performed. It's really advanced and the tool is in its infancy so it's probably not terribly user friendly. You must save your changes over an existing EXE of the same version (basically make a backup of your exe and save changes to the copy). You won't be able to play Multiplayer if you make the changes - I hope to start implimenting a better patch interface than what is there now (basically nothing) so that Multiplayer is possible if you and everyone you're playing with all have the same patch. For now there is unfortunately no help file with it (because I haven't had time to write one) but it's a lot similar to WarHack - if you ever used that - and the manual for that is available on the WC Software page.

[Download StarGraft](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043505/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=sg) - [422kb] - Created by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-12-02T22:45:00Z |title=Excellent News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-29T22:33:00Z |title=Well, Not All The News is Bad... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Finally some good news to offset the problems that KA has been facing... The ICE Beta is now officially released (FINALLY!!!).  Though I know that it's long overdue, and that I should have done a much better job with all the time I've had, I am glad that it's finally out.  The program is about 80% completed on the basics, so I figured now is as good a time as any to give it to you guys to test.

For those of you who don't know, ICE is a program that edits iscript.bin - the file which stores the scripts that control StarCraft animations.  It is very powerful in the hands of a skilled user, though only those with extensive knowledge of HEX editing have been able to truly customize it... Until now =).  With the current ICE build, you can completely manipulate the headers and opcodes that the file uses, all using a very simple and effective interface.  ICE Beta includes full CWAD support, which eliminates the need to import and export files that you want to edit, and makes for a very convienient editing process....  Is it just me or does this sound like a really corny commercial? =)

Make sure to download the ReadMe/Demo file.  I would have added it to the installer if I had remembered.  And READ THE README!  It's really important that you follow the instructions it has in there, so that I can get ICE out of its Beta stages as quickly as possible.

The demo CWAD that I included is basically a brief demonstration of ICE's abilities.  At first I had something much more elaborate, but after playing it a few minutes, I realized that it wasn't all that fun to use.  So, instead, I made a MOD that can really be played.  All I did was make a few changes to iscript.bin, so that none of the blood animations disappear.  Ya know when a Marine dies, and it leaves that little blood mark on the ground that slowly fades?  Well, now it doesn't fade.  Makes for some really messy maps, and it just looks plain cool =).  Also, the Siege Tank might Siege up a little strangely;  I can't remember if I took that out =).

Now, on to the downloads:

[ICE v1.00 BETA](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043505/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=ice) [1.16MBs] - by KramerBoy

[ICE ReadMe and Demo](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043505/ftp://ftp.camsys.org/pub2/CamelotSystems/software/StarCraft/ICEStuff.zip) [17Kb] - By KramerBoy

Lastly, if you want to make suggestions or comments about ICE, use this [forum thread](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043505/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_gen/index.cgi?read=7365) to tell me what you think!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-29T16:42:00Z |title=Yet another setback... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well it looks like things definately are not looking too good. I thought that I had backed everything up via my Zip drive on friday but it appears that it never actually wrote any data to the disks... (yes I did use a sledge hammer on the drive - to no effect). So that means that I have lost all current source code for Arsenal III and several other projects I had been working on, most of which were unannounced. Fortunately I did make backups of my source on the 16th of November, so the source exists from then for all the tools. However that source is so old (now) that it doesn't include the War2 Upgrade Editor in Arsenal III or for that matter the Tileset Editor or the Graphic Editor that I had been working on and was hoping to release at the end of last week.

I did get my new HD today - and lucked out there. The new one is 27.3 gig, which is 11 gig larger than my old one. I guess I can't be completely upset :P Now I just have to go through the incredibly boring process of reinstalling everything. Ugh.

So in any case what does this mean for the future of Arsenal III? The prognosis is not good. I really don't want to redo the War2 Upgrade Editor or the Tileset Editor. I'd like to keep working on the latter, but without my old source that's impossible. So it looks like "that's all folks" for Arsenal III. Don't bother to email me and plead with me to keep going because I've had it.

I think that unless I can find a new and stimulating project soon, this looks like the end for me...
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-22T05:30:00Z |title=StarCraft Tileset Editor Completed |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A year ago I would have laughed at anyone who said I could pull off a Tileset Editor (meaning I thought it was impossible). Oops the joke is on me :) I'm very happy to announce that there's finally a (partial) Tileset Editor for StarCraft. As I get more time I will be adding features to it (such as Zoom levels, Gamma Correction, Graphical Editing, etc). I suggest that you read the documentation that is provided before you attempt to use it. I think that a lot of you will probably be lost until Ace writes up a tutorial for it (which I have asked him to hold off on until I get more features completed). For anyone who ever used the Tileset Editor that came with WarDraft, the use is somewhat similar. Or at least it is eventually intended to be :)

Two important notes: The Tileset Editor was made part of Arsenal III (so it's not an independent program, although I guess if you really wanted me to I could make it one). Also the Editor only works with the extracted Tileset Data files, which means that you have to extract them from the MPQ's yourself, and it neither writes to nor reads from bitmap/ini files as I had originally intended. The reason was that it was generating huge bitmaps and inis (several megs at least) which is pretty unreasonable. So I've just got it working directly with the data files. You should be able to import/export individual tile graphics in the next version. For now you can manipulate the existing tiles.

[Download Arsenal III](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224058/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=as3) [810kb] - by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-21T17:02:00Z |title=PUDDraft Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Great news: I heard back from Alex Cech, developer of the incredible War2 tool PUDDraft, and he's agreed to send us the source to update for War2 BNE. Merlin used to have the source but lost it when his computer got fragged, so this is some really great news, considering that we thought Alex was no longer online at all :) I'll be studying his code as much as I can because I think there's a lot that I can learn from it to make our current and future tools work better and faster :) In any case, Cech should send us the source in a few days, and at that point it shouldn't take too long to update for War2 BNE (my estimate would be 2 weeks at the outside). I won't be updating it - I don't have Delphi, and I don't really know Pascal all that well either :) It looks like Merlin will probably be updating it if I can convince him it's time well spent.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-21T01:51:00Z |title=The Return of TileEdit? |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've started preliminary work on what I hope to be the next version of TileEdit. Like Arsenal III I have completely rewritten this new TileEdit to be faster and more efficient than its predecessor. Actually TileEdit never did edit tiles. I certainly hope to change that with the new version :) I will be storing a great deal of the data in INI files this time in case someone feels that the editor I provide to work with the Tiles once they are converted from the SC format to a bitmap and accompanying INI file is not good enough.

Some of you may be wondering why I haven't posted my review of the new Bond movie, especially given my last 2 posts :) The answer is that I was persuaded to see something else by a girl I'm interested in. :) The two of us plan on seeing it sometime next week. I guess I can live a few more days without seeing Bond if it means that I might have a chance at a girlfriend :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-20T05:24:00Z |title=New Bond |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hehe, I figured I'd get the first review up, just to spite KA =P.  I'll try to keep it brief, because I'm sure KA will write up quite a bit once he gets around to it.  (Possible Spoilers, btw... )

Anyway, I personally thought the new Bond was the worst of the 3 I've seen (Golden Eye, Tomorrow Never Dies, and this one).  I dunno why exactly.  I think most of it's because the female hero of this movie just seems to.. pop into the story.  Like one minute you don't even know she exists, and the next minute she's off trying to defuse nukes.  You never find out anything about her.  She's just... there.  Poor writing it you ask me.  Besides that, the plot was pretty good though, and I don't regret paying for the ticket too much.  I just think that they could have done a much better job than they did.

I did love that last quote though:  "I thought Christmas only comes once a year."  ... Hehe =).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-20T03:37:00Z |title=D3CP Update |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

D3CP is now at 1.01, including new features I've got in feedbacks and bug fixes. Overall, there has been 14 new features and bugfixes in this version. I strongly suggest you to download it if you've already downloaded 1.0, for there is a lot of changes.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-18T01:09:00Z |title=One Post is Not Enough... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

More Bond stuff: Changed the boring old "Arsenal II vs Arsenal III" poll to something much more relevant to the site: choosing your favorite James Bond / Austin Powers girl. The choices were pretty much pre-determined by my top 5 favorites. Ok there may be one or two more Bond babes that I left out, but I can't remember their names :P Funny how the Powers ones are easier to remember :P

Also I managed to dig up some more movie links at [The Official James Bond Website](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224058/http://www.jamesbond.com/), specifically in [Mis Moneypenny's Rolodex](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224058/http://www.jamesbond.com/mmpr/mmpr_chooseformat.html). Is this all just a shameless advertisement for Bond 19? Probably. Hey give me a break. It's the only time the movie number will coincide with my age, thereby deluding me into some false assumption that I am anything like Connery, Brosnan, or Moore (note the two dirty rotten scoundrels I didn't mention - Dalton, who honestly isn't that bad, and Lazenby who is fucking aweful). I'm also doing this to try to make as many people go to see the movie as possible so that Brosnan won't get his wish to kill off Bond soon. (Yes he really does want to - normally I'd call him a pompous bastard but he's my favorite. Yes I like him better than Connery. Well I didn't grow up with *him* as Bond so why should I be supportive of him? :P
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-17T18:29:00Z |title=Bond 19 is almost here! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yes I have an unhealthy obsession with all things 007 (you would too if you had my last name (BOND)). For everyone looking forward to Bond 19 on Friday, I thought I would refer you to some highlights of his previous adventures just in case you need to brush up on your trivia, or need to fufill your desire to see Xenia in action :P BTW, Monty Python fans should note that John Cleese (Minister of Silly Walks) makes an appearance in The World Is Not Enough (Bond 19) as Q's Assistant (a role suited perfectly for him).

[Bond Movie Clips](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224058/http://entertainment.msn.com/special/bond/downloads.asp)

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-17T07:44:00Z |title=WarCraft 2 Upgrades Editor Complete |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The Arsenal III WarCraft 2 Updates Editor (an insanely long title) is now complete. I have tweaked a great deal of code to result in smaller, faster code. I think you should notice that the strange behavior of the TreeView's is now gone when you open a new file (or at least diminished significantly). I'm also proud to announce that this new version includes AUTO UPDATING! :) Actually it's not really "auto" updating. You still have to click a little link on the main page of Arsenal III that says "Auto Update" which will load up a webpage on our site that will let you know if there is an update available for that product, give a link where to download it, and display any changes that may have taken place. Hopefully that should make it easier for you all to keep up to date on our software, especially after you have downloaded it. Often times people come back to our site a month later and have no idea that software has been updated, hopefully this new feature will make things easier :) Thanks goes to Dark Knight for his work on the Download CGI which I uncerimoniously plundered for the Auto Update CGI (I can't write PERL from scratch :).

[Download Arsenal III](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224058/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/download.cgi?id=as3) [894kb] - by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-11T16:00:00Z |title=Stuff |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I've actually got a good excuse for not working over the past week or so.  My 6 year old monitor finally died out, leaving me with no way to use my computer.  I then had to get my parents to dish out money for a new one... To make a long story short, I just got the new one last night.  So, I apologize for the inconvenience, and I'll be back to work soon.

Speaking of work, ICE has hit its first major set-back... The old layout I had only looks good on 640x480 resolution; the resolution of my old monitor.  Anyone who has a monitor running higher than that (That should be everyone now... I think I was the last person on Earth at 640x480 =)) would see a layout that is completely off.  So, it looks like I'm going to have to dump the old, flashy layout in favor of something a bit more traditional and compatible.  Remaking it all will be somewhat of a challenge, since I already have the whole program layed out.  Shouldn't take too long though, so it's not much of a problem in that respect.  Still, I did really like that old layout =[.

So, still no projected release date for the project, but at least I'll be back to work on it now =).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-09T06:10:00Z |title=Arsenal III and SunForge News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've almost got the remaining Arsenal III editors (all the Advanced ones - Campaigns, Flingy, Images, Portraits, Sounds, and Sprites) completed. I think I should have everything ready to go sometime tomorrow afternoon. I also added in Copy/Paste functionality to all the editors (forgot to add that earlier). CWAD Support will have to wait for one more version - it's giving me some real problems so I decided to delay it in favor of getting a working Advanced Editor out there for people to use.

In SunForge news, I hope to have Beta 5 out by the end of the week. I have the Terrain Editor just about complete, I just need to add the Misc editor in and fix a few bugs resulting from editors sharing common files. If everything works, then that should be the last Beta of SunForge, and I'll pronounce that project completed (in case you're wondering what that means, it just means I take the Beta off the title and I only do bugfixes to it). Note that I won't ever be charging for the use of SunForge. It will always be completely free. (same goes for all CamSys software).
{{end-newsitem}}

Arsenal III now has the Unit, Weapons, Technology, and Upgrades Editors complete. I'm working on the CWAD support, currently that hasn't been implimented. I tried to test everything out as best as I could before I uploaded it, and I didn't notice anything wrong with anything I edited. Unfortunately none of my normal beta testers were online to give it a run through, so it may not be completely bug free. Please report any bugs that you find as soon as you spot em. {{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-03T06:24:00Z |title=StarDraft does not work with StarCraft/Brood War 1.07 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

From the initial tests that I've run tonight it looks like the StarCraft/Brood War patch 1.07 disables all StarDraft patch loading (regardless of StarDraft version). I've messaged Merlin about this and hopefully he can look into it soon so that we can get it working again. Until then I suggest that you either not get the patch, or re-install StarCraft if you already do have it and want to keep using StarDraft. I guess this was to be expected since they said that it is supposed to disable all hacks (even though StarDraft isn't considered one, to my knowledge, we may have used a method of patching that a hack used). I'm running the test on Win98 Brood War, but other Operating Systems may still work - I suggest that someone test it on Win95 and NT and let me know so I can post it.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-11-02T16:55:00Z |title=Good News, Bad News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Bad news first: I won't be working on Arsenal III much (or anything else CamSys related) this week, reason being that I have 2 tests this week (one tonight and another on thursday), and another next week (although that one I'm not so concerned about). For some odd reason I want to do well on them, so I will be pretty much abandoing work on everything this week in favor of studying.

Good news is that I have Arsenal III's Weapons Editor complete. I'm going to *try* and get the Technology, Upgrade, and Orders editors done sometime this week. After that's done, I'll make another Arsenal III release, and then work on the Misc Editors.
If Merlin gets his act together soon, I should be able to add CWAD support to Arsenal III sometime during November.... Anyways I'll probably have the new Arsenal III release ready on friday.
{{end-newsitem}}
