{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-29T06:15:00Z |title=New Poll, Arsenal III Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've created a new poll to try and help me figure out how I'm doing with Arsenal III vs. Arsenal II. Please take a few seconds and let me know your opinion so that I can work to try to adhere to your requests for Arsenal III. Also, please feel free to email me comments about the program, too. The results of last week's poll ended up with StarCraft winning with 40% of the vote, for game we should edit, and Tiberian Sun came in second with approximately 28% of the vote. Then War2BNE was tied with Age of Empires 2 for third (small percentage each), followed by Descent 3.

I also wanted to take this opportunity to show you the new main screen for Arsenal III :) As you can see, Joel's outdone himself this time :)

{{img |file=/web/20000919224103im_/http://camelot.warzone.com/images/as3v2.jpg |title=Arsenal III Main Screen}}
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-28T05:10:00Z |title=Arsenal III Released! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well all the Beta Testers have been offline for several hours now, so by the time I had all the bugs fixed I had nobody left to send it to :) But I ran it through the checklist I had of the bugs we had found, and all of them seem to have been fixed. As such I've deemed Arsenal III ready for public consumption. This is in no way the final version of Arsenal III - in fact as I stated earlier, only the Unit Editor is functional (the others should be a cinch now that the Unit Editor is working). You'll also notice that there is no artwork yet. That's because I just gave Joel that task tonight :) Unlike our previous software, this new program should be much easier to update on your part - once it's complete you shouldn't have to download the entire file again if bugfixes are necessary - since I've broken up the interface from the file reading, you should only have to download updates to one part or the other.

You'll also notice that the stats loaded aren't from BrooDat.mpq, they are from a file in the "arr" subdirectory of wherever you installed Arsenal III. Initially loading the data from there will be an option once the program is finished, I will let you choose whether to always load from the MPQ or from the hard drive (that way if you want to always load some default stats that are not present in the MPQ you can do so). Also, I intend to add support for editing all the DAT related strings into Arsenal III to simplify that even more. My ultimate goal is to make the interface resemble as closely as possible that of StarEdit without sacrificing the power that previous versions of Arsenal III have had.

In any case, you're probably all clamoring to get your hands on it (as I would guess from the results of the poll):

[Arsenal III](/web/20000919224103/http://camelot.warzone.com/cgi/download.cgi?id=as3) [747kb] - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-28T00:37:00Z |title=Arsenal III News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The release of Arsenal III with any luck should come a bit later this evening. I've sent it to a few beta testers to try and find any bugs I frequently miss. The exact time I release it depends on how rigorous they test it, how many bugs they find, and whatever last minute changes I make. Hopefully tonight should see the dawn of a new era in SC Editing since this version is much more versatile (or capable of being so) than all its predecessors (Arsenal, SCUnit, Arsenal II). Back to work for now though.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-27T21:07:00Z |title=Don't you just hate that? |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yeah, another "little" problem. All was right with the D3CP download, except that when I uploaded it, since I set the protocol with ASCII because I was working with the CGIs, I forgot to put it back to binary and uploaded the zip file in ascii format... So you couldn't unzip it. I just hate FTP sometimes ;) Anyways. I've reuploaded it in BINARY this time, so it should be openable... Geez, I'm tired of these kind of mistakes sometimes ;)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-27T01:58:00Z |title=Projects area online |author=SCX |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hey all, the projects area is finally back, and this time its going to be kept up to date. {{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-26T21:20:00Z |title=Another download problem... |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I thought the download CGI was working all fine now, but it is not. A file or two gets corrupted every day, and I thought it was due to a little something in the script, correcting it over each time, until yesterday, WarHack's database file got corrupted, I decided to put exclusive locks on every file access, and it didn't even worked any better... Worse, StarDraft's database file got corrupted today! This is unacceptable for now, and I'm unable to find anything wrong with the script itself. Looked over, over, and over, and it SHOULDN'T do that.

There seems to be a problem with file locking, and I'll look on that to know what is going on. Until then, the stats on the downloads have been **disabled** until I solve this problem. I'm really sorry but I can't take the risk to get any other files corrupted again, paralyzing the download until I notice it. However, you'll still be able to download as normal, but the stats won't be recorded. I wanted to release D3CP Beta tonight, but with this problem, I think I'll delay it a little until I find the problem. Again, I'm sorry for the problems caused with that CGI, but there is things that are unexpectable, and this is one of them... At least, when it will get solved, it will be for all the future CGIs ;)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-25T03:26:00Z |title=Arsenal II Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Seeing as how a large percentage of you (who voted, at least) are still interested in StarCraft software, I thought I would inform you of my work on the new Arsenal II. I started from scratch. I didn't want to try and weed out bugs from the older version that had a lot of unnecessary code making it very difficult to find what you wanted...  In any case, the interface will be completely revamped, in an attempt by me to make it more closely resemble StarEdit's interface (for the Unit Editor, at least). Hopefully this should lead to an easier editing experience since those of you familiar with StarEdit should be able to make a smooth transition. Also for simplicity's sake, I have moved the Weapons/Orders editing into two seperate editors of their own. I think that having them as part of the unit editor caused a lot of unecessary confusion because people thought they had to edit stuff that they didn't really have to sometimes. Plus if you just wanted to edit weapons, it's easier this way. I think having it integrated was a hold-over from the original SCUnit, and seperating it returns it to the way Arsenal used to have it. I'm about 50% done with the Unit Editor. I still have to add loading/saving code, but it shouldn't be too much longer on that. I think I will release the new Arsenal II when the Unit Editor is complete, get everyone's opinions on it, and then proceed from there on the rest of the editors.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-24T15:50:00Z |title=Wow, thank you. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I think it's about time I expressed my gratitude on behalf of everyone here at CamSys to you, our loyal fans. In the week that's elapsed since Dark Knight's download CGI went online, we've registered 10,000 downloads of our software. I never expected that number to be so incredibly high. According to our hitbox statistics, we now have an equally astounding 3500 visits per day. Two years ago when we started CamSys we were lucky to get 100 per day. So thank you to everyone for patronizing our website and for supporting our efforts by downloading our software. This is the kind of motivation that we need to keep producing software for you :)
Thanks so much!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-24T06:36:00Z |title=War2 UDTA Specs Complete |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The first of the WarCraft 2 format specs is complete: the [UDTA](/web/20000919224103/http://camelot.warzone.com/formats/WarCraft2/udta.htm) section is now online. This should pave the way for the new Unit Data TAbles editor to be written soon that will accompany the rest of our new BNE tools.

If I'm feeling up to it and ambitous enough, I will start work on fixing Arsenal II. What I'm probably going to end up doing is starting over from scratch, and then distributing it seperately for you to use with your current versions of StarDraft. All of the updates before were to Arsenal II, not StarDraft, but most of the bugs showed up in StarDraft, not Arsenal II. So I figure if I seperate the two, you can use an updated Arsenal II with whatever StarDraft you prefer.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-24T02:27:00Z |title=Time for democracy! |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As KA said in his last post, I've been working on a vote CGI and it's now ready to use! The poll subject is on what games we should focus on. So go vote and please only do so once! I think this poll lasts a week or two, don't really know about it ;) Please [e-mail me](mailto:dkknight@quebectel.com) if you run into any problems with the CGI!

I should now focus on D3CP or on another CGI that KA would like to have ;) I've done some progress on D3CP too and you should expect a beta of it to be released soon! ;)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-21T06:47:00Z |title=War2 BNE Utils Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Blizzard was kind enough to donate a copy of War2 BNE to me, which I should be getting shortly. My primary task with BNE is to get WarDraft II and WarHack ported (mostly the latter). I'll probably work with KramerBoy on the Unit Editor for BNE, too. Acutally depending on how things go I may do it on my own since he's got ICE to finish up (ahem). But I can't really yell at KB now because he's helping me out by making a program to automate the generation of the format pages, which will greatly increase the speed of development in that area. KB should be doing IScript.bin specs, and Dark Knight should be doing AIScript.bin specs as well as one or two D3 specs once the latest version of SCAIEdit is ready (I think).

Dark Knight's also working on another CGI script for us - this one will be our poll script so that I can determine more easily preferences you guys have on various aspects of our software, and the general direction of CamSys.

Anyways, as soon as I get BNE, I'm going to start work on a new (windows-based) WarHack (probably titled WarGraft to escape the negative "hack" connotation).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-20T23:31:00Z |title=Affiliate News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Usually I refrain from posting news about sites other than ours, simply because that's not the nature of our site, but since Campaign Creations is an affiliate in more ways than one, I'm doing so:

After a short downtime, Campaign Creations is back online with her new layout. ([http://www.campaigncreations.com/](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224103/http://www.campaigncreations.com/)) In addition to the new layout, Saturday will mark the release for a demonstration of a new project for StarCraft. Details to be revealed soon.

-Desler
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-19T06:52:00Z |title=StarDraft Request Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks to everyone who has responded to my request so far, I now have the stuff I need from StarCraft 1.00, and Brood War 1.04-1.06. If anyone else with a StarCraft-only installation of v1.01-1.06 would contact me, I'd appreciate it.

Also, according to our download script, there have been about 900 downloads of the latest version of StarDraft - the incredibly bug prone one... I reccomend that you all wait to download StarDraft until we get everything fixed. In preperation for working on the War2 BNE utils, Merlin is going to work on re-creating StarDraft to make porting it to War2BNE easier and faster. We may also redo some of the plugins, or at least redo how they work (which may lead to Arsenal II being available as a standalone program). What are your views on this? Do you think that Arsenal should remain a plugin only accessable through StarDraft, or should it also become a sepreate program?
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-17T20:58:00Z |title=War2 BNE |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Many people have been asking if we will be making new versions of our tools for WarCraft II so that they are compatible with Battle.net Edition. I'm pleased to announce that if all goes well, we should be able to do so. I don't have a copy of the game yet - hope to bum one off of Blizzard - and I have many projects going already. So I'm enlisting the help of Pendragon, KramerBoy, and Merlin to resurrect our War2 tools. I have heard reports that PUDs are supposedly validated... hopefully this is not the case. Otherwise we intend to get PUDDraft, UDTAHack, and WarHack back online, and start on WarDraft II (StarDraft for War2). So sometime in the next month or two you should start seeing new BNE-compatible versions of our War2 tools appearing on our webpage!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-17T20:47:00Z |title=Formats Section |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You may have noticed two new "menus" on the right hand side of the page: Online Help and the Formats Live Wire. The first will be links to the Online Help for any software released after September, Arsenal II included. Right now the Help is incomplete and as such is not online. I will post when it is online. The second section (formats) will be as complete a database as possible of all formats that we've determined exist in the games we edit. Right now the only format I have up is the StarCraft Units.dat format [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224103/http://www.camsys.org/formats/StarCraft/units.htm). Other formats will go online as soon as I can write them up.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-17T20:44:00Z |title=Request |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Could anyone who has a StarCraft version other than Brood War 1.04 or 1.06  please [email me](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com)? I only have 1.04 and 1.06 (both BW) and need to obtain data from the other versions in order to correct some problems with StarDraft. Please email me as soon as possible.
Thanks, KA
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-17T04:03:00Z |title=I hope it's the last one |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hehe, I doubt you've already guessed ;) Most of the download system at CS got paralyzed the whole day because of a little bug in the download CGI, which is fixed now. I'm really sorry for the inconvenience caused by that, I've tried to get online during all the day to see if everything was going all right with the CGI, but I've unfortunately not been able to until late in the night...

Thanks to everyone who sent me an e-mail telling me the download CGI was messing up, I really appreciate it(not that it messed up, the bug reports ;). As said in the title, I hope it's the last one. Oh yeah, I had to reset all the download stats because most of the database got corrupted, so it's now restarting from 23:56 from the October 16! And as a final word, I'd like to thank KA who improved the appearance of the CGI today ;)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-16T15:00:00Z |title=Download Problems |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It appears that something has gotten messed up and Dark Knight's download script is no longer working as it did yesterday... He and I will be taking a look at it today and hopefully we can have it fixed. So for now, sorry but all our software is down.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-16T06:05:00Z |title=Forum and Download Scripts updated... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I also updated the Forum and Download scripts today. To the Forum script I added icons for Campaign Creations members because most of them are fairly knowledgable about StarDraft and Arsenal and can usually help you out if you have a question. The Download script has been updated so that its layout matches the rest of the page.

Please note that many of the sections listed on the left in the menu are not online yet. Please don't email me about sections being missing until I make an announcement that says everything _should_ be online...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-16T06:00:00Z |title=Layout Update... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You will have noticed by now that the new post-box format has changed a bit. I got really sick to death of the old format, and I think that this one looks better, loads faster (no images) and takes up less space (both in the html code and on the page). It may get changed when Joel redoes the layout (hopefully next week). For now tho I like it better than the old... hope you do, too.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-15T21:27:00Z |title=I HATE that! |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I can't believe how we can do stupid mistakes sometimes... guess it's the common characteristic of every human ;) anyways... The download counter didn't worked yesterday, because I forgot to activate the write flag for the database... oh my god... what a mistake ;) anyways, just this to say that the download counter has started right now, not yesterday night ;) Sorry for the inconvenience... Funny thing though, as soon as I activated that flag, there have been 2 downloads of TS inis and StarDraft... hehe, pretty fast ;) Should add a stat page soon ;)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-15T02:25:00Z |title=New download system! |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As you may have noticed, there has been some changes in the CS website. KA tweaked a bit the layout, and I've made a download CGI which is now active. We'll now be able to track the number of downloads, as well as having a good database of our softwares. It shouldn't change much in your point of view, but it changes a lot for us ;) The design for the CGI is not really good, but it should be updated when the new design will be out! Enjoy!

As for my projects, I'm somewhat mad I haven't released anything for nearly a month and a half, so I'll concentrate most of my energies now in finishing a beta of D3CP so you can see what it will look like... That should be it!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-14T19:49:00Z |title=Forum Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I decided to update the forum code a little bit to add support for MVP and Staff "icons" to be shown after people's names on the forum. Hopefully this will help out people new to CamSys and our software, so that they'll be able to have an easier time getting help by easily finding people who should be able to help them. Hopefully this will help to eliminate the "KA HELP ME!" epidemic where everyone assumes I'm the only person who can help anyone out... which is definately not the case (nods to Ace and Galahad especially). If you feel that you deserve MVP status and I have neglected you, you can petition me to add your name to the list. However it's up to my sole discretion who gets MVP status and who doesn't... Right now the MVP's are Ace Calhoon, Sir Galahad, and Ius, and the staff members are me, KramerBoy, and Dark Knight... I didn't add any others because almost nobody else but the three of us posts (for staff that is). Anyways... hope this helps.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-14T01:13:00Z |title=ICE Break-through |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Long over due, I finally managed to create a working psuedo-execution module for ICE.  For those of you who don't know what that means, let's just say that ICE just became a thousand times more space efficient =).  It also means, that we've officially identifed every opcode that iscript uses.  We don't necessarily have descriptions of all of them, but at least we can edit them all, and we don't have to worry too much about bugs turning up from unknown ops anymore.

So, for those of you keeping track, I'm going to be cramming all of the code into a DLL, and making sure that all works.  Once that's been successfully completed, I can begin working on the guts of ICE.  It should be a lot faster this time around, because I'm a little better of a programmer and I've already done it once before, so I can skip the hard parts.  Not to mention that most of the interpreter coding is already done, so I can basically skip over that part this time around.  All in all, things are going very well for ICE2 =).

Note to self:  Try to use a word longer than 'conglomeration' in a post...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-13T02:31:00Z |title=Rant clarification... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After all of the CS staff (except Merlin, who I don't think reads the page anyways :) commented on the harshness of my last post (on StarDraft) I thought that I should make a clarification... All of what I said was from a programming standpoint. I think that StarDraft from the user's perspective is an awe-inspriing program, and I'd be damned if I could have come up with it on my own. Many thousand kudos go to Merlin for coming up with the way to make it work... From a programmer's standpoint, looking at the source code with 20/20 hindsight, I see a very unweildy conglomeration of many different programmer's efforts, coding styles, features, etc. and it just seems to be too much to handle (reasonably). Too often I find that my opinions get blurred when I both use and program something... My apologies if I implied something negative about Merlin that I shouldn't have. He's an amazing programmer with a knowledge of programming that far surpasses my own, and StarDraft is only possible because of him.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-12T16:41:00Z |title=I've just about had it... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You know, I've just about had it with StarDraft. I'm about ready to delete the whole fucking thing off of my computer. From a programming standpoint, it's the absolute worst piece of crap that I've ever worked on, and I am beginning to feel ashamed to have my name associated with it. Everything in it is such a kludge that it's no wonder that every new "bug fix" unleashes 10 more in its wake. I swear if I ever work on something like this again, I will NOT let Merlin fuck with my editors... his "performance improvements" may have provided an initial performance boost to Arsenal II, but in the long run it's made the program incredibly difficult to manage. One change in Arsenal II can effect the GRP Editor, TBL Editor, MPQ Viewer, etc, because they're all inside the same bloody program. ARGH! Never again... I'm so sick of the stupid thing I'm not touching it until tonight to fix it, and after that I won't touch it again. if it's broken, it's Merlin's problem from here on out. He made the stupid thing the bug-laden swamp that it is, he can fix it!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-12T14:46:00Z |title=Damn dirty bugs... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I thought everything went a little too smoothly last night. Turns out that StarDraft was - yet again - bug-laden because I changed one minor thing at the last minute that I didn't check... It's fixed now, as is SunForge - I included the wrong version of Art.ini with it, and as a result the Art Editor doesn't work. That's fixed now, sorry about that. Note: you must use the versions of Art.ini and Rules.ini that I distribute with the editor - they're custom versions made to optimize how SunForge works. No changes have been made, however, that will affect gameplay whatsoever.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-12T05:46:00Z |title=StarDraft Updated |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just posted the much-needed bug-fix for the latest release of StarDraft. I double checked everything and it looks to all be working perfectly, so I don't anticipate that anyone will have any problems with it. (*knocks on wood*).

[Download info...](software/scsoft.htm#sd)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-12T03:32:00Z |title=StarDraft News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

At this point most of you are probably wondering "where the hell is the bug-fixed StarDraft? Did KA forget about it?" The answer is that I'm going to try and get it working tonight, and yes I did forget about it. Although I had told KramerBoy that I would be fixing it, I forgot that I had done so and had assumed he would be uploading it :) Oops. :) Sorry, I'll try and get it uploaded tonight or tomorrow, depending on how things go.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-12T03:29:00Z |title=Future Projects (AOE2, etc) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I finally managed to pick up a copy of AOE2 late Saturday afternoon, and it feels like I've been playing ever since then (my weekend certainly disappeared in the blink of an eye). I must say I am very impressed by what I have played so far. It's superior to all competing games in its genre (which doesn't really include StarCraft, for those of you about to flame me). I enjoy playing it quite a bit, and will do so for quite some time, I should think. I also purchased Rogue Spear on Friday - this one I hadn't planned on, but my brother wanted to play it. I had an awesome time with this game. Brought back the many days this summer I spent playing Counter-Strike for Half-Life. An excellent game that I highly reccomend (same goes for AOE2). I've decided to cancel any projects that I had planned for Braveheart. I read the review of it in this month's PC Gamer and sadly, I did agree with them. I found that my initial feelings to the game were severly influenced by my love for the movie, and as a result I ignored the fact that the game was pretty poorly designed (and the combat interface sucked). As a result, I won't be authoring any custom tools for it (I have too much else to do, besides). My apologies to anyone who was waiting for me to do Braveheart utils.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-12T03:22:00Z |title=SunForge Beta 4 Release |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've released SunForge Beta 4 tonight, however it does not include the Misc. and Terrain editors as promised. Since the Tileset and Art editors took longer than expected to complete, and have a very large array of options available to edit, I rethought things through and decided it would be for the best if I released it as it is now, and work on the Misc. and Terrain editors for Beta 5 (hopefully the last version). I have no idea when Beta 5 will be done - I haven't done any work on it at all. Best guess I can offer right now is 2 weeks from now, as that seems to be the average time between SunForge releases. The Misc. and Terrain editors are significantly more complex than the others, especially the Misc. Editor, but if I have enough time, I should be able to get everything done by then. Between now and then I hope to get the rest of the Online Help written... please be patient.

Anyways... [to the download...](software/tssoft.htm#forge) (also available on the SLW to the right).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-10T14:52:00Z |title=Remember me? |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hehe... Been a while.  I know some of you may think I'm dead/quit/etc. and I apologize for giving you that impression.  I have actually posted a few times over the past month, but KA keeps overwriting my posts... I hope this one survives =).

So, what have I been up to?  Remaking ICE.  Basically, I was planning on releasing it a couple of weeks ago, when I looked at the code and realized just how poor a job I had done.  It just didn't feel right to release the program in that state, so I made the decision to re-create it from the ground up.  And so far, I've been very successful.  ICE has a completely new (and very cool, I might add) interface, and will have a number of new features included along with all the old ones I had planned.  Though it may take a little more time, it's definetly worth the wait... Trust me on that =).

Anyway, just thought I'd make sure you guys didn't think I had bailed on you.  ICE is still being worked on, and is going along well so far.  I guess everything's easier the second time around =).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-08T02:12:00Z |title=WTF is up with AOE2? |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've been waiting an entire week for a copy of AOE2 to show up at my local EB, but no they don't have any yet. WTF happened? Did they order like 2 copies and they all sold out last wednesday or what? I haven't ever heard of a game selling out so fast, and being so hard to find. I mean this is bloody ridiculous, you'd think it was the most hotly anticipated title of the season (that distinction belongs to either D2 or Q3). Well anyways, I guess it was meant to be delayed - and probably just as well. The two tests I had today went well since I had a bit more time to study as I wasn't hooked on a new game... Still, wish I had that game. All I can say is that it better be worth the wait...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-07T05:56:00Z |title=SunForge Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Again, please excuse my apparent lack of progress - in addition to the large amount of homework I had this week that I have already complained about, I also have three tests. One of them I took monday, two I take tomorrow (or rather later today (thursday)). As a result I haven't had much time to work on SunForge Beta 4, hence the failure on my part to release it yet. I made significant progress this afternoon - I now have both the Tileset and Art editors working, and hopefully I should also be able to get the Terrain Editor up and running by friday. The Miscellaneous editor may take a little longer, however, since it's the least structured of any of them. I also have in my creation of the two new editors so far fixed several bugs that plagued earlier versions of SunForge, and may have prevented some of you from playing the game correctly. Depending on how quickly things go with the Terrain Editor, I may release Beta 4 without the Misc. Editor, and instead just (ha! - just!!) the Art, Tileset, and Terrain editors. We shall see.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-05T04:46:00Z |title=Blasted Homework! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hmm... I had an extraordinarily evil Computer Science assignment that I was working on last night and tonight... it obviously took me much longer than I had anticipated... and therefore my apologies that I do not have Beta 4 of SunForge ready as promised. I will try and work on it as much as I can tomorrow, hopefully I can get it out before Wednesday. It does have a lot of new features - more than I have released to date, what with 4 new editors (Tileset, Terrain, Art, and Misc.), so I guess I probably underestimated how long it would take me. Besides I'd like to test it all myself before I release it to make sure everything really does work, and that will take a while now that there's so much to test. Thanks for your patience and understanding.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-03T20:55:00Z |title=SunForge Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The Art Editor for SunForge Beta 4 is now complete. I will be moving onto the Terrain and Tileset Editors next. I would expect that I should be ready to release Beta 4 to the public either later tonight or tomorrow.  Thank you to everyone who has supported the SunForge project, your comments/suggestions/bug reports have helped to make this a much better program. After Beta 4 is released, I will start work on the Map Editor. A week after Beta 4 is released, I will make all necessary bug fixes and then release the final build of SunForge (i.e. non-beta, not the last build).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-02T06:08:00Z |title=Software Updates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks in large part to SCX, we now have an accurate and complete software section which lists all of our software, a description of each program, and links to download it on the ftp and www servers. This may seem trivial, but until he started working on it, those pages had been in a pretty bad state of updating. Now they should all be current and have working links. Let me know if you find one that we missed. Also, I have updated the Software Live Wire a bit. I added back some of our War2 software in anticipation of War2 BNE, and I have moved some of the older StarCraft software off of the Live Wire and have instead placed a link to the rest of the software page. Also, each software section name will take you immediately to the software page for that game, so that you can read the descriptions for all of that game's software.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-02T03:27:00Z |title=SCX Joins the CamSys Staff |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A few weeks ago, SCX joined the CamSys staff but somehow I neglected to mention that he had joined. He works for SCLegacy in some of their sections, and when they didn't offer enough for him to do, he offered to join our staff and help me out with the many ancient sub-sections that I had let expire quite a while ago. Since then, he has gone to great lengths to update the entire site, and I am very pleased to have him on board to help us out here. Thanks SCX, and keep up the great work.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-02T03:05:00Z |title=Projects updates |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just hate it when I don't update at a regular rate. I wish nobody thinks I'm doing nothing because I don't update often... ;)

Ok, for the projects... the new forum CGI development is paused, I'm still waiting for the new design. I don't have any idea when it'll be done ;) I've also continued my new Descent 3 Project, called D3 Control Panel, which allow you to edit the table files of Descent 3. Table files contains information on Weapons, Textures, Sounds, Doors, Ships and Generics. I've posted a screenshot of what it will approximatively look like :

[Screenshot of D3CP main menu, in its development stages](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224103/http://www.camsys.org/software/d3cpbeta.jpg)

Sigh... if it wasn't of school, the project would have already been done. But well, real life is not that simple ;) I'll probably advance a lot this weekend.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-02T02:31:00Z |title=AOE2 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well according to my local EB, they won't be getting any more copies of Age of Empires 2: Age of Kings for 3-5 business days from today. They must have sold out incredibly fast, or ordered only like 3 copies... They're kind of inept so the latter wouldn't surprise me. Unfortunately there is no other close by software store that I can go to, so it looks like I'll have to wait until the middle of next week before I can even play the game, let alone customize it. Oh well that will give me a chance to finish up SunForge and get a good start on the Map Editor, I guess. Just in case I get hooked... :P
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-10-01T15:53:00Z |title=SunForge News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've been working on Beta 4 of SunForge this week and it's looking very promising :) I'm working on the all-new Art Editor right now, and after I complete that I will be working on the Terrain, Tileset, and Miscelaneous Editors. Once those have been completed, I will be moving on to the TS Map Editor, which thanks to the efforts of Mordred (working on SunDraft - the Archive Editor for Tiberian Sun), should be fairly simple to start work on (although the actual editor itself will undoubtedly prove to be quite a challenge. In any case, things are looking good, and I should have Beta 4 (probably the final beta) ready for release sometime this weekend.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=September 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-30T13:29:00Z |title=The new stardraft... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

First off - do not download the new StarDraft. As many of you already know, it's extremely bug-laden, and for this I apologize. Turns out that Merlin, KramerBoy and I all had different versions of the StarDraft source code, and my version happened to be not only incredibly old, but also incredibly non-functional it seems. I had planned to send my changes back to KramerBoy and have him release it, but he wasn't on and I wanted to get the software released... bad move :( I've now sent the source to him, and as soon as he gets a chance, he will be combining my Arsenal 2 source (which was up to date) with the rest of StarDraft, and then releasing it. Again, my apologies for this rather useless (for now) program. I meant well... :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-29T17:15:00Z |title=Age of Empires II: Age of Kings |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Oh my god I can't believe I haven't seen/heard anything about this game yet. I saw the screenshots at the official [Age of Kings website](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224108/http://www.ensemblestudios.com/aoeii/index.shtml) and I was simply amazed by what I saw. I love medieval based games (hence my nickname) and after seeing these screenshots, I know I must get this game. I'm going to get it as soon as possible, and with any luck start customizing it soon after that. If the gameplay is as good as the game looks, I'll be content for a long time :) I may even play this one from time to time, unlike the other games I customize :P
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-29T03:22:00Z |title=SunForge Beta 3 Released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-28T03:41:00Z |title=Tutorial Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just finished creating the outline for my new version of the Game Editor Programming tutorial (now called Game Editor Programming Unleashed - paying homage to my favorite series of books). As you can see [here](programming/gep2.htm) it's a pretty complete outline of all the topics that I consider worthy for study (I'm thinking of making just a C/C++ programming tutorial in general). It's also pretty ambitious. I'll be frank - it's going to take me a long time to complete this tutorial. After it is done, I will return to the 21 days tutorial, condensing what I have written for the Unleashed tutorial so that you get the same overall scope, just not the same depth that you get with the Unleashed series. I'll probably call in help with the Unleashed series, too because it has such a large scope, it would take me a very long time to write the entire thing myself. Plus there's some stuff on there that I just plain do not know yet. :) I will by the time I write it tho :P I guess that the outline is probably of more use to me than anyone else - it gives me a clear goal where I need to be going, what I need to concentrate on in each section, etc. In any case, it's up there, and I'll be going in order for the most part. I'll post when I have the first section of Chapter 1 completed.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-26T05:32:00Z |title=StarDraft and SunForge Update... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

KramerBoy's long awaited update to StarDraft is almost ready to be released. I just need to make a few last changes to it, and then it should be ready to distribute. Arsenal II has been changed a great deal, to support the many discoveries made by Campaign Creations, etc. I have also updated the Grapuical User Interface (GUI) to a SunForge-like style of interface. All that remains is to update the Misc Editor GUI (Unit and Upgrade editors have been upgraded to the new GUI already), and test out a few other updates to StarDraft and CWAD Manager. I would guess that it will be ready sometime tomorrow.

SunForge Beta 3 is about halfway done. My apologies that it has taken considerably longer than I originally thought - this week was quite busy and as such I had far less time to work on it than I had guessed I would need. I've also been working on a lot of Custom Controls so that I can have a great deal of experience to draw on when creating that particular section of the tutorials. You'll notice some of the custom controls appearing in SunForge (Betas 2 and 3) as well as the new Arsenal II.

For both Arsenal II and SunForge I plan to have someone work on an extensive online help system that will interface through your web browser. Essentially you would be able to click on the label for each particular stat and go to a webpage which would have the latest information on that stat, the values it can have, etc. I hope to get this up and online shortly after the release of both of these utils. As for the map editor - I haven't started on it yet - and won't for another week or so it now seems.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-24T04:24:00Z |title=Programming Tutorial Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It has come to my attention recently that a large number of you have become very interested in the Programming Tutorials that I have created so far. When I set out I intended to create 21-days' worth of tutorials that would eventually teach you enough so that I felt that when you finished, you'd have just about the same amount of knowledge as I did when creating them. Many of you have told me that you eagerly await the rest of the tutorials, and I am pleased to hear that, just as I was pleased to hear so many of you were reading them at all.

I have every intention of completing this tutorial series. I think that right now it's one of the most important tasks that I have set before me... In my opinion, although you can learn much of what the tutorials have to offer by majoring in Computer Science, I believe that most Computer Science programs are funamentally flawed, in that the software engineers the curriculum produces are ill equipped to tackle real-world problems. The reason seems to be, from observing my classmates, that they did not spend any great length of time on non-class related programming. It seemed that they all lacked a creative spark to propell their quest for knowledge, and to expand their abilities as a programmer. I think the Computer Science courses that they take have stripped away the magical aspect of programming, and its ultimate results, so that the end result is someone with a degree which doesn't really mean anything.

My goal with these tutorials is to try and provide as many of you with that creative spark as I can. Reading what I've written, doing the examples, etc is not enough in and of itself. You have to seek out projects to focus your knowledge on and produce something unique and challenging.

Well in any case, I did have the thought last night of re-structuring and re-ordering the tutorials, and removing the 21-day restriction. I think that such a restriction is artificial, and to imply that you can learn all that I have set forth in 21 days is absurd. Removing that restriction also enables me to add topics as I see fit and to re-arrange the existing topics should I find that one sequence is more successful than the rest. I also intend to compile a reference that you can turn to of examples of many of the things I have illustrated in the tutorials. Now that I have an idea of just how many people are interested in these tutorials, I feel that what I have already done is inadequate and below the standards I would have set if I had thought that there was to be such a wide-spread following for them. So I may try and rewrite the existing tutorials before I continue on. I will probably keep both versions up in case one format seems easier to understand than the other.

In any case, I thank you all for your feedback on the tutorials, I wanted to let you know where I was going with them, and to let you know that I am going to keep working on them, as soon as I am able to. I may start rewriting tomorrow if I have time.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-22T03:27:00Z |title=SunForge Maintenence Release |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-19T15:07:00Z |title=Bah... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-18T03:22:00Z |title=I guess it's my turn... |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It's going to be cool ;)

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-17T20:54:00Z |title=My Turn to Apologize... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'd just like to say sorry to all of you guys, and to KA and the rest of CS.  Ever since I was promoted to Lead Webmaster, I've gone into hiding =).  I've put off projects, and I've slacked in my responsiblities to this site.  Even worse, I don't have a reasonable excuse =).  I did just start my freshman year of high school, but I really should have been using my free time to work, instead of using school as an excuse to avoid it.  At any other job I would have been fired a while ago, so again, I apologize for being so lazy lately.

Now, as for StarDraft:  Should be out sometime later tonight or tomorrow.  I'm basically finished working on it, but I want to send it out to a few people first, to make sure I'm not screwing up anything important =).  Basically I just want to make sure that my the upgrades to Arsenal are correct.  The only other thing I have to do is de-pluginize ICE...  yes, you read correctly; I'm de-pluginizing it. Why?  Long story, but the short version is: if I didn't, SD wouldn't be out for another week.  So, with any luck, the next and final SD should be out very shortly.  I should apologize on this one too, because this is another project that I could have had ready much sooner.

I'm not really sure what's in store for me after SD is done.  I don't have any new projects in the works at this point.  All I can guarantee is that they won't be StarCraft related =).  I suppose I'll probably end up giving KA a break and putting in my share on SunForge, or the TS Map editor... If my help is needed =).  Otherwise I'll just... Slack =P.

So, for all you StarCraft fans, be on the lookout for SD4 tonight.  And, again, I apologize for being so unproductive lately, and I promise to do my best to stay useful for at least the next week =).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-17T03:43:00Z |title=Apology |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You all have my apologies - I feel like I've been less than productive this week since the Custom Combobox Control I have been working on for SunForge has eaten up a large chunk of what free time I had this week (which wasn't much - it's been pretty busy, very long, and extremly tiring). I can't promise that Beta 3 will be finished this weekend, but after tomorrow night I should be able to spend most if not all of my time on it. I also did not feel well at all today - either emotionally or physically - I think I have a stomach bug and a sore throat, plus I'm miserable on the social aspect of my life, so things are looking pretty bleak for me. In addition, my hopes for working for Blizzard part time during the year appear to have fallen through, so I'll have to find a job around here that I really don't want to do making money that I desperately need but don't really want to spend the time on earning it... oh well my apologies for venting my troubles... I do feel a little better from it, though, so thanks.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-16T04:37:00Z |title=Tiberian Sun Map Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As of this moment I haven't recieved a reply to an email I sent to Westwood last Friday asking them if they had any problems with me creating a Map Editor. I'm going to send one additional email, and if I still get no response by next week, then I will operate under the assumption that they have no problems with the Map Editor, so for all those of you eager to have one, as long as WW is unresponsive, it looks like you'll get your wish :) i'll keep you posted on what's happening in that area.

Also a number of you have reported an error in the Weapons Editor when adding a Weapon (and one said that they couldn't get it to save the changes - is this common, too?). The adding error produces a windows exception, and I have reproduced it and therefore confirmed it. I found where I made the mistake - an unfortunate typo on my part. I've fixed it and will release the bugfix with Beta 3 (due this weekend).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-15T00:56:00Z |title=SunForge Beta 3 and Tiberian Sun Map Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'd imagine that I'll have Beta 3 of SunForge ready to go by the end of the week. I am planning on adding an AI Editor and updating the interface of the Unit and Weapon editors to be still more user friendly (i.e. drop down lists of selections, rather than merely a text box where you have to remember what values mean). Also, I'm going to try and find someone to help me write up a set of Help files for the editor - I had to remove a lot of information about what each of the items changes for sake of screen space... Hopefully I'll be able to get most of that into a Help file that I can distribute with an upcoming version. After I have finished adding the ability to edit the entire file (probably Beta 4) I'll make sure everything's all running properly and then fix any remaining bugs and release it as a more or less final version (still free, by the way). Camelot Systems hasn't ever and doesn't plan to charge for our software.

Many of you have also asked for information on the Tiberian Sun Map Editor that we were planning to create - I had held off on starting that until after SunForge was complete (I want to integrate it into the Map Editor somewhat). After I had heard that someone else who had plans for a Map Editor had been threatened with Legal action on the part of Westwood, I decided to email someone at Westwood and try to determine what exactly was going on. That was friday (or so). I haven't heard anything since, but I'll keep you posted. I'm not going to get into a legal battle over this, so if it comes to WW not wanting us to make one, we won't. I'm not paying for any legal bills...
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-11T21:26:00Z |title=SunForge Beta 2 Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

First off, my apologies for missing the release date yesterday and not even announcing why. I got swamped with a lot of work last night that I hadn't expected, and as a result Beta 2 never got completed. Yesterday morning I had planned to do most of the work, but I had to try and find a jumper for the back of an IDE Zip drive that I just got which wasn't working. By the time I got the jumper and got home I had only about an hour before I had to leave for my campus, which was clearly not enough time to do all I had planned on. Last night like I said was just way too busy. This morning I had to work on some Java stuff for one of my courses this semester. After I finished that around 1pm I started work on SunForge again, the Weapon Editor mostly. I got a great deal done and have it loading all of the Weapon stuff. Just have to work on Adding/Deleting weapons. After that gets done, I'm going into a code freeze for SunForge and am shipping it off to Pendragon who will be fixing the undo/redo code that I broke (sorry!) :P When he's done we'll test it and make sure everything works now and then upload it. So possibly tonight, maybe tomorow for the new release date. Again, sorry for the delay but it couldn't be helped.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-10T03:46:00Z |title=SunForge Beta 2 to be released tomorrow! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'll be releasing SunForge Beta 2 sometime tomorrow (hopefully before 2pm EST). Beta 2 adds the ability to Add/Delete units (currently working!) as well as a full-featured Weapons Editor (almost done - the bulk of the work tomorrow will be on this part). I'll be putting a new main window up where you can choose between the Weapon and Unit editors (soon to be followed by other editors). {{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-04T20:01:00Z |title=StarCraft and Camelot Systems |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Recently a lot of people have emailed me complaining that we're not working on StarCraft software anymore. I wasn't speaking for everyone else when I said I'd be abandoning StarCraft to move on to other games. KramerBoy is working on an update for StarDraft that features his ICE program as a plugin, along with updates to Arsenal II's Misc Editor. Anyone who works here as a programmer has the freedom to choose what games that they work on utilities for. It wouldn't be fair to assign people games if they didn't want to work on them. So there is a possibility that some of the Camelot Systems programming staff will continue to produce StarCraft software. Also, we're not going to dump the software that we currently have available. Merlin plans to make updates to StarDraft to keep it compatible with StarCraft - he has no plans to add new features, however. I think it would be foolish on our part to have everyone concentrate only on StarCraft customization software. StarCraft is nearly dead - with it being dropped from the PGL recently and the announcement that there will be no more Battle.net tournaments, its not going to be too long until it will be a fading memory. I don't want to see Camelot Systems fold and go under because we kept working on tools for an old game that nobody plays anymore. By my expanding to work on other games, I hope to continue to see the success that we had with StarDraft and provide many more people with the customization possibilities that we had created for StarCraft. Please do not hassle me anymore about creating StarCraft software - I have no plans to do so right now.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-04T00:17:00Z |title=KramerBoy, _Dark_Knight_ and Merlin - MIA? |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

KramerBoy, contrary to popular belief, is not Missing in Action. In fact he's been helping me out with the Tiberian Sun tools, working on integrating the new Layouts into the site, etc.

_Dark_Knight_ has also been accounted for - he's been working on a new style of forum for our site. I was able to look over a preview edition of the forum software and it looks very promising. We'll be holding another vote to have our viewers choose which forum layout they prefer. Regardless of the vote, _Dark_Knight_ will be using the forum style he created on the upcoming Descent 3 subsite for CS. He'll be main webmaster for the subsite as he's the only one of us right now working on Descent 3.

Merlin had been swamped with school work for the last week and really was MIA until last night, really. Right now in between large amounts of homework, he has plans to make a StarDraft-like patching utility for Tiberian Sun MIX archives, whose format is in principle very similar to the MPQ archive format. Patching is much more preferable to a full archive editor, even though one is conceptually possible, due to the extremely large file size of the MIX files. No estimate yet on a release date for the patcher - the project is unnamed as yet (we'll probably end up calling it SunDraft).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-04T00:11:00Z |title=Tiberian Sun Customizing |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Although we're well on the way to developing tools to automate the editing of several files that control Tiberian Sun, I've decided to go ahead and release the INI files so that those of you hoping to get the customization done as soon as possible can get started. Just extract and place all the ini files in your Tiberian Sun directory, and open any one you want with a text editor. Thanks to Scott Walker for helping me to find all the Ini files!

[Tiberian Sun Ini's](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224108/ftp://ftp.camsys.org/pub2/CamelotSystems/TSunInis.zip)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-04T00:07:00Z |title=New Logo takes hold |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Due to the overwhelming outpouring of support in favor of our new logo, I've decided to go ahead and make it our official logo :) The same artist will hopefully be creating a complete redesign of the entire layout soon based on the same theme as the logo (I think). We have another layout replacement already completed by Wizard. Once the new layout is complete, I will set up a test page for each layout design and let everyone vote on the layout so that we can go with whichever layout the majority feels is the best.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-09-02T01:32:00Z |title=New Artists and Programmers |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm happy to report that I've had very good success in finding at least 2 very good artists and 3 programmers who all should be starting work at Camelot Systems very soon. Hopefully this will lighten my workload a bit and allow us to expand into even more games, too. Also the artists may be helping us out in the program design area, so that our programs are much easier to use, the layouts look better, etc. Hopefully they'll be working by the end of next week. I'll introduce everyone when we've got everything settiled. If you're worried that we've stopped accepting people to the site, that is absolutely not true! I'm always on the look-out for exceptional artists and programmers to help with our efforts. The more games we can customize, the better effects that we will have on the gaming community in general! I may even get some time to finish my programming tutorials sooner than I expected, so perhaps you could join us after reading them! :)

Note to self: I thought I resigned as lead webmaster... :)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{heading |level=2 |title=August 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-08-30T13:37:00Z |title=Tiberian Sun Map Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Great news about Tiberian Sun for those of you stressed over the lack of a Map Editor - the Map format for multiplayer maps looks to be incredibly simple AND robust. As soon as I get the chance, I'll be starting work on the map editor. Also expect to see within the next few weeks another customization utility that would work like Arsenal II did, only for Tiberian Sun. It will allow you to change unit properties, etc and a huge list of other things. With any luck we should have the first editors for Tiberian Sun available by the end of September, college schedule permitting. :)

Oh also, THE DOMAIN IS UP! Check us out at our new URL: [http://www.camsys.org/](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203190344/http://www.camsys.org/). Update bookmarks and links please, because we're going to be dropping the old address in a few weeks.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-08-26T20:57:00Z |title=Camelot Systems gets its own domain! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I figured that after a very successful second year for Camelot Systems, it was about time we had our own domain. So thanks to the generosity of BetaMax and the rest of the Warzone staff, we now have one! [www.camsys.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203190344/http://www.camsys.org/) will be our new address effective within the next day or two. Once the domain goes active I will place a redirection message here (at www.camsys.org). I will have Warzone keep the old address active for about 2 weeks, after which time we will be permanently situated at www.camsys.org. Why ".org" you ask? Well to be honest it sounds less intimidating than ".com" and describes us better in any case - we're definately a non-profit organization, not a real company, so the org seemed most appropriate.

In addition, now that we're going to have our own domain **and** be covering many additional games, I thought it was about time that we break up the site a bit. The main page will still be news about all of the games we customize, etc, but I will be splitting up the site based on the games we cover. In other words, StarDraft and the other StarCraft tools will be moved to something like starcraft.camsys.org and that particular webpage will cover only starcraft related stuff. That way it's easier for everyone to find news and files for the games they're interested in rather than having to wade through news and files for games you don't like.

So since we will be having a great many changes take place, please be patient with us and if portions of the site start malfunctioning, my apologies in advance. I will also be looking for assistant webmasters, graphics artists, etc to help with the page expansion. I'd like to give each special game page its own unique feel, etc.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-08-26T14:40:00Z |title=The Return (sort of) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm very pleased to be able to announce my return to working at Camelot Systems. The summer I spent working for Blizzard was the best time of my life, and I eagerly await next summer now that I've been invited back :) I do have plans to work outside of CamSys during my upcoming Sophomore year at college. With any luck I'll be able to do that and keep programming custom utilities for some of the best games out there.

Unfortunately a bit of bad news first: effective immediately I'm abandoning the Lead Webmaster position here in favor of programming productivity. As such, KramerBoy has been promoted to the Lead Webmaster position until such time as I decide I miss the job (not likely) or he decides he's had enough. I'll still be making posts now and again, but the bulk of the news you see should be from KramerBoy.

Also, as much as it might pain some of us to admit it, StarCraft is dying if not dead already. At least in the US. Korea it appears will keep it alive for a while longer, but for now all of us at CamSys realize that we have to move on to new games and new custom utilities. Therefore I've recently purchased copies of Braveheart, DarkStone and Tiberian Sun in hopes that I can create customization utilities for some if not all of these games. The reason for the selection of these games is fairly straightforeward:

* Braveheart - Loved that movie, have to try and customize it :)
* DarkStone - Took a look at this one during the summer. I already have an archive editor that I'll be releasing as soon as I put it through a few more tests and add a couple of features. No real personal interest in this game, just I saw how easy the archives would be to edit and couldn't resist :P
* Tiberian Sun - By popular demand I've purchased this game in hopes that I can customize it for the many people who asked me to try. Haven't ever played a CC; game before so I'm not quite sure how it will compare to other CC;'s and to SC.

Why the sudden change to non-Blizzard games? Basically because I've worked there and am not allowed to. Although Diablo II will be out soon, I don't anticipate a large demand for customization of that game, so I don't think we'd even bother anyways.

Anyways that's my agenda for now. I can't guarantee that if you request other games to customize I'll be able to get copies of them. I'll probably stick with these three for a while anywho. In any case, good to be back, and I'll keep you all up to date on my progress.

One final quick note: we may have a new layout up in a few days. KB and I will be trying to impliment it this week and see how it looks.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-08-13T20:36:00Z |title=Lotsa Stuff... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

"Maybe if I make a BIG post, they won't notice I haven't posted in nearly two weeks."

I apologize; I haven't been updating nearly as much as I should be.  But, there's been a lot going on lately, so I'll try to cover it all in one post =).

First of all, though I'm sad to say it, the Star Wars TC which we are hosting has officially called it quits.  It looked like a very promising project while it lasted, and I'm sad to see it go.  They have uploaded all the work they didn't get done for those of you who want to to check that out.  I definetly will =).  Don't worry, though, we'll keep the site on server until warzone tells us not to anymore.

Secondly....

> **[KramerBoy(CS)]** Mind if I mention your map site is opening up?
> **[Nossirahdrof]** ok. you can mention how great it will be :) and how you've helped me test a few maps that show just how great a map maker i am :) etc. etc. etc. just write a big long post about how great i am :)

Right, that's what I'll do =P.  Anyway, CS is proud to announce the opening of a brand new site we're hosting.  Our good friend Nossirahdrof, who was also a scummer for the SW TC, has opened up a website dedicated to StarCraft map-making, and whatever else he decides to put on it =).  Congrats to Noss for having the courage to open a website on Friday the 13th =).  Anyway, he's got some really cool trigger-powered maps that will be up there, so make sure to check it out [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203190344/http://www.camsys.org/noss/).

Thirdly, I'd just like to quickly address a subject I've been getting EMail about.  Yes, DK is going to be making some D3 software... No, DK isn't giving up ScAIEdit...Yes, I am going to continue working on my StarCraft projects.  There, now stop asking =P.  Also, stop mailing me about you IQ scores, I believe we've established that the test isn't accurate =P.

Fourth, I've run into a bit of a snag with ICE.  Apparently BCB doesn't seem to like me very much, and is giving me some very strange errors.  Hopefully I can have this all cleared up within a day or two, but I have no clue what's causing my problems at this point, so I don't know how to fix them.  Probably has something to do with me trying to program on Friday the 13th =[.

And finally, I've decided to save the best news for last (Yes, Noss, this news is better =P).  This will probably be my last post as the 'boss' of CS.  KA and Merlin will be returning from CA sometime within the next week, though I'm not going to mention exactly *when* in case they want a few days to take a break before you guys all start bugging them again =).  They'll also need some time to shower me with the gifts and souveniers that I know they're bringing back for me.  Anyway, given that I don't have my months mixed up/their plane isn't shot down/etc etc, they should be back here at CS sometime very soon.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-08-02T03:12:00Z |title=A new game added! |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Don't forget to [e-mail me](mailto:dkknight@quebectel.com) your comments!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-08-01T17:31:00Z |title=Updates |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yesterday was a very productive day for me.  I got the ICE Beta out to my testers (Most of them anyway), and so far there are no major bugs to report.  Once I start getting some more feedback from them I'm going to begin working on the script editing portion of the program, which should go fairly quickly if all goes well.

I also made considerable progress in updating Arsenal II.  Nearly all of the major unknowns have been ID'd, and support has been added for the sprites.dat file.  So today I'm going to go about getting updated entry lists, and then Arsenal will be done for the next planned release.

So, once A2's been updated completely, I'm just going to go all out on ICE until it's completed.  I'm not going to be releasing A2 until ICE is finished, and pluginzed and all.  Depending on whether or not DK's ready for ScAIEdit to be pluginized, we'll do that too, and then release SD =).  Mind you, I'm not sure how long this entire process will take; I'm just giving you guys an idea of what we'll be doing in the coming weeks.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=July 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-29T21:20:00Z |title=Stuff |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, ICE is going along *very* well so far.  The interpreter is completed to my satisfaction.  It's able to correctly identify and translate all of the opcodes we've identified so far, with one exception =).  I've completed the portion of the program that supports editing of the script headers and all that fun stuff... Now I'm working on editing the actual scripts themselves.  I believe I know of a method that should take most of the hassle out of the project, but I'm not sure how well it will work yet.  I'll be testing it out over the next few days, and I'll tell make sure to let you guys know how things go.  *If* I'm able to get things working, I plan on doing a closed beta test of the program (And, no, don't try to convince me to make it open =).  Then I go into the bug fixing stage of things, another closed beta, and then who knows... you guys might have an iscript editor.  Then again, you might not, at this point it's hard to say =).

And just one other thing you might enjoy.  I just decided to take an [IQTest](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204145125/http://www.iqtest.com/) off of a website that claims that "If the test's directions were accurately followed and the questions were answered as fast as possible, the test you just took usually yields scores that are within 5 points of the professional tests".  Sounds interesting.  Even more interesting: My score =).  I got a frickin 162 (Keep in mind... I'm only 14 years old)!! A 140 is "definetly genious level" according to them, so either I'm *really* smart or whoever created the test isn't =).  I dunno, but somehow that seems a little high to me... No, it seems WAY too high =).  I'll make Merlin take the test sometime and see what he gets.  Anyway, the test is only like 40 questions, and it took me 9 minutes to complete, so if you got some spare time, head on over and check it out =).

**Update**

I think I can say almost certainly now that the IQ test isn't accurate.  I've been getting numerous reports of people scoring in the 160's and 170's and that's WAY above average.  Still, it's kinda fun, and if you have some gullible friends, you may be able to mess with them using it =).  Just don't take it as an accurate method of judging your IQ.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-24T23:25:00Z |title=Updates |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Wow... Been a long time since I posted =).  Don't think that the past week has been unproductive though.  I've gotten quite a bit done.

The interpreter in ICE, our iscript editor, is coming along very nicely.  So far it's been able to identify and translate all of the opcodes that we know so far.  There are still some bugs to be worked out, but those should be fixed within a day or two.  I'll admit, though, that interpreting is the easy part of this.  It will be interesting to see how editing goes.  I'm not going to put any date for release yet, because I know how much I hate deadlines.  No point in setting one on myself =).  But I will say that things are looking good so far with the project.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-18T02:29:00Z |title=New Mirror/Affiliate |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-14T17:03:00Z |title=Various news |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I've returned, for the moment. Those of you that are very very old will remember my last post, those of you that aren't can look it up in the back of our old musty archives, if we have any. If you don't feel like looking, three or four weeks ago, I made a post (as you may have guessed). Pretty amazing, huh? Anyway, it basicly said I was having trouble with stardraft/starcraft/both, and that I'd be working on it for a while. I've worked on it. And worked on it. And worked on it. I've re-installed: SD and SC 6 times each, Windows once. That was accidental, though; the computer didn't like what I was doing I guess and crashed. It was fixed soon after, but still no luck with loading customs.

That was Friday. Saturday I got the flu (it's going arround, and it's really bad this time), and still had it Sunday morning, although not as bad. Monday I got the computer back and re-installed everything by 6:00 that night. Tuesday, yesterday, I tried to run customs again. No luck. So, to make a long story short (too late, I know), I've given up.

*So*, I thought, *Now what?*  A perfectly good question, deserving at least some kind of answer, but I couldn't think of anything right off the top of my head, so I gave it some thought..... Then some more thought..... Then some more..... Four chicken strips, a milkshake, and a side of fries later, I still had no answer. That was.... about 45 minutes ago (Noon, here). So, I've decided on a one-time-only solution:

[I](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204145125/http://dir.yahoo.com/Recreation/Games/Computer_Games/Internet_Games/MUDs__MUSHes__MOOs__etc_/MUDs/) [q](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204145125/telnet://luminous.wili.net:4000/)[u](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204145125/http://www-csag.cs.uiuc.edu/individual/pakin/complaint)[i](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204145125/http://www.angelfire.com/or/lordwoody/luminous.html)[t](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204145125/http://www.mtv.com/mtv/tubescan/trl99_new/), sorry everyone.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-10T21:38:00Z |title=GO USA!! =) |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-10T17:31:00Z |title=Small lie, New programs =) |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I know, I know.  I said I'd get back to my normal work schedule shortly.  But, really, I can explain! =).  With all the recent breakthoughs in iscript (Image script) information, I've decided to go ahead and begin an editor for it.  Hopefully I will have this ready in time for the next release of StarDraft (which will include ScAIEdit, among other things).  Dubbed ICE (IScript Customizer/Editor... I know... kinda lame =] ), this program will allow you to edit unit speeds, animation data, and some sound data.

I've also begun.. a... well... secret project =).  I'm witholding what it is because I'm not sure if it'll work yet.  But if it does, it'll sure be cool =).  I'm still testing it out, but I'll know whether it's doable or not by tomorrow or Monday.  If things look favorable, I'll tell you what I'm working on.

And lastly, I made a fun purchase yesterday.  I got myself an Imation 4x/4x/20x CD Recorder =D.  This thing rocks.  It actually runs better for reading than my original 24x did =).  So I've been playing around with that a lot lately.  It's cool to be able to play BW and a music CD at the same time =P.

So, yes, SCMDraft is on hold for a little while.  I know a number of you have been patienty waiting for a BETA, and I'm sorry to disappoint you.  Right now, my priority is working on ICE and getting it done in a resonable amount of time =).  So, sorry about the delay on SCMDraft, and expect some new program info shortly!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-07-05T03:14:00Z |title=Happy 4th =) |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It's genious really.  I see webmasters all over doing it.  They hide the fact that they have nothing of relevance to post about by writing up some 4th of July thing on their site.  Since all I really can tell you about is why I have nothing to tell you about, I think I'll join in.

This year's 4th weekend was less than exciting for me.  We drove 5 hours up to Minnesota to bike ride.  Seriously, the forest is beautiful up there, but after so many times of seeing the same tree, stuff like "I wonder how well these would burn" or "I bet that tree would make a nice couch" starts to go through your head, and it really ruins the effect.  30 miles of bike riding later ( yes, I actually made it that far... didn't enjoy it much though =[ ) we were back in the car for the 5 hour trip home.  This weekend's fireworks show consisted of a large oak tree with a lot of fireworks behind it that were out of my view, and the occasional "Holy crap that went high!" firework that made its way over the branches to the point where I could see it.  There's another one of those trees that I wish had burnt to the ground.  And if I hear "God Bless the USA", or whatever that song's called, one more time, I'm gonna snap, I swear it! =).

So, there's just an explanation at the lack of updates for a while.  Actually I did get a rather important part of SCMDraft working just a few minutes ago, so the past few days haven't been a complete waste.  I'll be back to my normal schedule shortly =).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-25T20:49:00Z |title=Stuff... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just a few updates...

The graphical portion of SCMDraft is coming along nicely, and I'm a few lines of code away from getting tileset displayed.  Units shouldn't come too long after that.  So far this is going a lot better than I had originally planned, though there are still some things that may slow me up, and I'll be hitting that area shortly.  So far, so good though.

The Warzone FTP server is back up and running, so I've moved all of our downloads over to it.  The links should be updated correctly, but I may have made an error.  If you find something, make sure to [complain](mailto:kramerboy@warzone.com) to me about it.

And finally there's been something bugging me lately.  In my recent cruises through the forums I've found a lot of troubling posts that I would like to address.... Please, bear with me....

THERE'S NO COMMAND EDITOR!!  THERE WILL NEVER BE ANY COMMAND EDITOR FROM CS.

There... Just had to get that out of my system.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-21T02:31:00Z |title=ScAIEdit II temporarily inactive... |author=Dark Knight |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}
