{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-19T20:03:00Z |title=Titles Suck :) |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I had a little chat with KA. Things are going pretty well for them over at Blizz, and they seem to be enjoying things over there.  Apparently Merlin's just a wierd as we all thought he was, but I haven't talked to him yet, so I don't know what to think of KA :).  KA was proud of me for not breaking anything important here on the site, and being fairly productive all by myself ;).  We'll see how long THAT lasts ;).

Speaking of productivity, I've finally gotten SCMDraft to generate it's own minimap images.  Ok..  I ripped the code straight out of TileEdit, so I guess *I* didn't really do it, but close enough ;).  Now comes the part where I see if I can make units show up, and if I can get the minimaps to refresh as the terrain is edited.  I'll probably want to get SCMDraft to display the terrain too while I'm at it...  So basically, I'm entering the (probably) hardest area of the program.  The next week or two will determine the outcome of SCMDraft, so we'll see what happens.  If I can't get things working the way I want to, I may leave the terrain editing part totally out of the program, at least for now.  I know, that would suck, but I do intend on getting a release version out sometime before I die, and if I can't get things working, I'll just have to leave them out.  We'll see though :).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-17T01:02:00Z |title=New program for ya... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I've decided to go ahead and release my program which allows square terrain in StarEdit; dubbed ISOM.  It's a very simple program, though it can be put to very good use once we found out more about what we're dealing with.  Essentially the key lies in the mysterious ISOM section of a Starcraft map.  This program will fill the section with a value you specify, so you can use some of the strange effects in StarEdit.  Nossirahdrof and I have made little progress in finding any pattern in this section, but I have included the little work we have documented in the zip.  I've got the source code all zipped up and ready to go, but, quite frankly it isn't my best work, and I'm not sure whether many people could gather much from it.  If people show that they want the source code, then I'll release it.

And this brings me to another point.  I still remember the trouble I caused in my original release of SDTutor, and I've tried my best to avoid the problem in this program... Now I am *almost* positive that people won't run in to the same problem as last time, but seeing as I don't have an effective way to test this, I really can't be so sure.  So, if it asks for VCL35x.bpl or whatever the hell it's called, just download the SDTutor runtime files for now, and let me know so that I know I'm wrong again :).

Also, if you can run ISOM, and you find some magical value that does something cool, or you think you have some information on ISOM's format and why it does what it does, [EMail](mailto:kramerboy@warzone.com) me with some info about it, so we can find out/document more of it's effects.

Oh yeah, for those of you wondering, this is the program that will allow perfectly square terrain in StarEdit...

Anyway, Enjoy!

[Download ISOM](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224124/http://www.camsys.org/software/ISOM.zip) - 310KB
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-15T17:18:00Z |title=Stuff... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, the recent days have certainly been productive.  First of all, my birthday was on Sunday (if anyone cares).  I turned 14, so now I can legally hold a job :).  I'm not sure whether I'll be looking for one soon or not, but it looks like I'll wait a while :).

And now down to business.  Over the past few days, Nossirahdrof and I have made some rather interesting discoveries about SC map editing, namely a way to make perfectly square terrain (IE that chess map), right in StarEdit without using a major hack :).  I've finished a little utility that will allow this type of editing, but I'm going withhold it's release until Noss and I can get a little more information on it's capabilities.  Right now, all we've really found is that it makes StarEdit go wacky :).  I'm going to do some more research, get some documentation on exactly what can be done, and then I'll go ahead and release the program.  Hopefully this will keep you guys occupied while I work more on SCMDraft ;).  Hehe...  I can just see all of you who were HEX editing to get this effect sighing in relief now that we found this :).

And, yes, KA and Merlin are now officially in Irvine working with Blizz now.  This means that now any email regarding CS should be directed to me or another available staff member, and away from them.  I can still harass them, you guys can't :P.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-12T15:12:00Z |title=Ok... STOP |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks to my friend Jorin for the suggestion that actually worked for me. Basically, it went like this:

> Lancelot: Hey Jorin  
> Jorin: Hey, saw yer post  
> Lance: Yeah? Any suggestions then?  
> Jorin: Yep.  
> [a long pause]  
> Lance: Well?  
> Jorin: Oh, you wanna hear it?  
> [This being in a MUD, I was able to whap Jorin on the back of the head at this point]  
> Lance: DUH!!  
> Jorin: Ok then, nothing, for the site.  
> [another dramatic pause]  
> Lance: I don't follow.  
> Jorin: heheh  
> Jorin: Do something, but not for the site. Then you'd have to meet everyone's demands, and everyone has different demands to meet.  
> Lance: Ahh... So what should I do then?  
> Jorin: Well, you have any free time this summer?  
> [bonk Jorin]  
> Lance: Umm.. yeah, duh  
> Jorin: Write a book, then.  
> Lance: Hmm... Good idea  
> Jorin: I thought so :-)

So, major thanks to Jorin. Thanks to your help, I'm naming the lead character after you.

Also, in other news: A new site, deserving mention for the LONGEST list of possible survey answers in a single survey I've seen yet: [http://home.adelphia.net/~cooldeath/](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224124/http://home.adelphia.net/~cooldeath/)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-11T15:34:00Z |title=Hello, again |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Good news: Finals are over.

Better news: So is school.

In other words, I'm here basically full time as of now.

Now the bad news: I have no idea what to do now that I'm here again.

I said I would be doing customs again, sure. I hope to start doing that by Monday. Here's the thing, though: That's not all I want to do. I'd like to do news, of course, but there's a problem with that. I forgot all the good site names (I haven't even been on Brood War OR StarCraft since Feb. or March), and even if I did remember them, I wouldn't visit them. Not to be mean, but I just really don't care that much about most of the news I see. That's where you come in.

Since I don't want to find the news myself, as I'm no longer a die-hard StarCraft player, I need those of you who still are to send in all the major news ya got. Don't flood everyone else's in-boxes, though. Send it in to me, unless, for whatever reason, you feel like telling someone else about it. For refrence, my e-mail adress is [silver_wolf3@hotmail.com](mailto:silver_wolf3@hotmail.com)

Yes, tell me about your new site. But don't necessarily expect me to post about it unless there's a lot of good, original work on it. For example, if your layout looks like a kindergardner threw up and started fingerpainting with his lunch, it's not goin up. Nor will it go up if it looks exactly like another site. Sorry, but that's the way it's gonna be.

Anyway, I was talking about wanting something else to do. I'd help out with programming, but.... I'd probly screw up the entire program within five minutes. I'd like to open the floodgates to my inbox here: Send in yer suggestions. To give you an idea of a few possible replies: Hmm... Good idea, I'll think about it; I know very little about tech support; I can't program.... at all; I don't have the time (even on vacation) to make SC/BW campagins for the site, at least not a good one; I'm already planning to start the Customs section up again; that's DISGUSTING, consider yourself blocked from my adress; isn't that illegal?; oh yeah, forgot I could do that; no, I don't think so.

On a final thought: I've gotten a couple e-mails asking me to divulge my identity on the MUD I play. Not gonna happen. And, from this point on, I will no longer even mention the name of said MUD. Although I will tell you one thing: Centaurs SERIOUSLY need to be played more

Well, I think I've made a long enough post, I'll make another one.... when there's something to post about :-)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-02T21:25:00Z |title=WOOOOOHOOOOOO!!! |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

School's out!! Yipppppeeeee!!! :).  Today was my final day in that hell known as Junior High ;).  I think for the next few days I'm going to sleep a lot, just because I can.  Then I'll do it some more.  Then I'll get back to work full time on SCMDraft.

Hopefully I'll have a BETA out sometime this month.  This basically depends on how easy terrain editing is.  In theory, I shouldn't have *too* much of a problem... of course everything's easier in theory, so we'll see.  Statistics editing was completed in my spare time during studying, and I've started to work in a few more map options.  Triggers aren't being started yet, because this was one of the major things that people suggested, and I got a lot of good ideas about the topic.  Now I'm trying to figure out how I'm going to put it all together.  It may be a rather complicated process, and that's why I'm going to be keeping it out of SCMDraft for a while.

Now the only other thing to worry about is any projects that KA and Merlin haven't told anyone about that I'm sure they'll dump on me :).  OK, so I don't know for sure that any of these exist, but I'll bet they created some just in case they needed to leave and wanted to laugh at me for doing their work :).

A few quick notes while I'm rambling... In the next few minutes I'm going to be fixing a number of broken links on the site, mainly on the software page.  So for all of you who are waiting to get your hands on our programs, you should be able to now.  If there's anything I forgot to fix, feel free to complain :).

And finally I'd just like to officially welcome Lancelot and Sir Galahad back to CS.  I doubt I'm going to need any new staff members after this, so those of you who ignored my request not to ask me to join the staff can now stop emailing me, because all the positions are filled.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-06-01T19:42:00Z |title=Look who's here! |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Guess who!

Hey everyone! I'm back!

For those who don't know: I used to work here back in January, but a suddenly heavy wave of schoolwork forced me to quit. Good news is: School ends at the end of next week. You might not see much of me until then, since that week is finals. Sorry :-)
Hey, major congrats to KA and Merlin! All that hard work paying off finally, huh?

Those that do remember me might want to know what I been doing the last five months or so. Not a whole not (other than school). In what spare time I had, I've been playing a particuarly addictive MUD, called Luminous Horizions. I won't tell you my name on there, I'd rather not be disturbed while I'm busy getting my text-based self killed. Sorry :-)

What am I doing back, you now ask. Well, unless you've been under a rock in a cave on Mars with your eyes closed and your fingers in your ears, KA and Merlin are heading off to Blizzard for the summer. Kramer asked if I wanted to come back. Well, of course I did. It'll be a little while before I get back into the full swing of things again, but here I am, none the less

That's not what I meant, you say then. Oh, what am I doing? Well, odds and ends stuff, mostly. I'm probly gonna go back to news and customs, but that's not set in stone as of yet. Don't start sending me your customs quite yet, but I probly will start taking them again eventually.

Now, you ask another question, as innevidable as any other: Am I glad to be back?

I answer with a reply as simple as the queston: Is the Pope Catholic?

In other words, yes, I'm very glad to be back

So, here I am. Hope to be all I was before and more, which should be easy since I wasn't much back in the beginning of the year :-)
The one and only promise that I will make at this point: I definately intend to make more than one post per week :-)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{heading |level=2 |title=May 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-30T04:18:00Z |title=This Summer |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

> [KramerBoy(CS)] If I were to say I'm working 3-4 hours a day, every day, all summer, for no pay, what would you call me?  
> [AnonymousFriend] An idiot.  
> [KramerBoy(CS)] ...Well you'll never believe this...

Hmm... this summer will certainly be... intersting :) (If you haven't a clue what I'm talking about, scroll down and read KA's most recent post).  All I'm thinking is 'Damn.. I didn't get invited :('.  Hehe... Not that I could legally do it anyway, but.... :).  Right now I'm positive I won't be able to handle the entire workload myself, and I'm looking into bringing on a few more staff members to help out.  This doesn't mean you should start emailing me about joining.  I already have some people in mind, and I'm not looking for any more than that.

As for Camelot's future.... It will probably be determined by early July.  By then I'll have added any new staff members I can, and I'll have gotten an idea of what work will be like.  And by then I'll have decided whether I can handle it all.  I'm going to do my best to be consistent, and I'm going to try to keep you from missing KA and Merlin too much :).  If things go perfectly you won't even notice they're gone.  If things go terribly, I'm not sure what will happen to CS.  I'm not saying this to scare you guys or anything, I'm just helping you get used to the possibility that there's a chance CS won't continue in their absence.  And I'm saying this in hopes that you guys will give me some breaks while I adjust to the new job.  I'll try to keep up, but please, cut me a little slack at first.

So don't panic yet, I still am confident that CS will survive through this.  Once finals are over and school's out (Last day is this coming Tuesday), I'll begin the process of getting used to the task ahead of me.  Though it appears daunting at first, I'm pretty sure I'll be able to manage fairly well :).  And for those of you who are wondering, I intend to continue work as normal with SCMDraft regardless of what happens to this site.  Whether it's running or not, I'll get SCMDraft finished.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-29T03:13:00Z |title=Incredible News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Camelot Systems is extremely proud and honored to announce that due to the fantastic public response to our software, specifically StarDraft and Arsenal II, Blizzard Entertainment will be borrowing the services of both Andy Bond (King Arthur) and Cameron Buschardt (Merlin). The positions at Blizzard are temporary; they will both spend approximately two months in Irvine, California during the summer working for Blizz. Hopefully this opportunity will lead to further association between Blizzard Entertainment and Camelot Systems.

Unfortunately due to the fact that both Andy and Cameron will be employed at Blizzard, they will be unable to discuss anything related to any projects that they work on while in Blizzard's employment, and will most likely be unable to independently release new programs which customize existing or future games. We hope to be able to continue our work on an official basis after the temporary employment is over, so that we can continue to provide the gaming community with the best customization opportunities for the best games.

Camelot Systems, therefore, will be left in the more than capable hands of KramerBoy, who will be responsible for the day to day upkeep of the site, and who will be continuing to produce independent customization utilities, possibly including any projects that either Andy or Cameron give to him that they were unable to finish before leaving.

Please do not ask either Andy, Cameron, or any of the Camelot Systems staff to divulge any additional information on this exciting opportunity. Both Andy and Cameron will be subject to the terms of a Non-Disclosure agreement, and will be unable to legally divulge any more information regarding this opportunity.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-25T01:59:00Z |title=Phantom Menace Review |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok actually I'm not going to do a full Phantom Menace review until I see the movie again... but I felt that it was important enough that I warn you all of one of the dangers of the movie; something so bad that it may spoil your enjoyment of the new chapter of Star Wars: Jar Jar Binks. Simply put, this is the lamest attempt at a side-kick that I've ever seen. His voice has got to be one of the most annoying things I've ever heard in my life. The accent, if you can believe it, is even worse. If you can, buy earplugs and/or blinders for when Jar Jar is on screen, because I think it may have ruined my impression of the movie. The worst part of it is, there's absolutely NO reason that he needed to be in the movie, aside from a lame attempt at appealing to 4 year old kids.

If you ask me, Jar Jar was the biggest villian in Episode I - he stole meaningful screentime from Darth Maul. Speaking of Maul, Jar Jar should have been the one to die, not Maul. I mean here's the equivalent of Darth Vader, and he gets killed off basically for the sole reason of appealing to the hollywood producers, IMO. I mean there was no reason for him to die in Episode one, except to allow Obi Wan to get something he desperately wants (you'll know when u see the movie what I mean). Jar Jar should have died, IMO, and since he didn't, I hope there's a special edition of the trilogy that either removes his character or dubs in a new voice over the existing one. Either that or he gets killed off in the first few seconds of Episode II. Hell I'd cheer my guts out if even the gigantic logo for Episode II happened to squash him as it moved off the screen!

Jar Jar aside, it was I think a worthy predecessor of the original trilogy.

Oh and if anyone else hates him as much as I do, take a gander [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://www.jarjarsucks.com/).

One last thing, feel free to spout off on Jar Jar :P I'll GLADLY post your comments. Direct them [here](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-22T20:46:00Z |title=Stuff |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I'm going to have to apologize for those of you anxious to see SCMDraft.  With finals around the corner, I've kinda put studying for those as a higher priority.  Work on SCMDraft continues, but nothing major has been done recently.  I'll get back to work more towards the end of this month.

And of course, if KA gets to do this, I guess I get to once in a while.

WARNING:  POSSIBLE SPOILERS AHEAD... (I think I took them all out though ;).

I got back from Phantom Menace a few minutes ago.  My thoughts:  Sorry Shlong.... the critics kinda did have a point.  At least the one's I've heard anyway.  Now, the special effects were amazing.  The lightsaber battles truly rocked... they make the original ones look like two kindergarteners playing pirate er something ;).  It lacked the great space battles, but the ones it had weren't really bad.  Obi-Wan and the Qui-Gon guy were truly awsome characters.  And the story was great, except for one thing... that kid.

He  did a great acting job, but they kinda put him in a lame position... I mean, c'mon, he jumps into a starfighter he's never flown before and he can't even turn on at first, flys the thing into a major space battle, and ends up, by pure chance, destroying a huge droid ship.  Bull :).  And what's he doing flirting with this girl all the time, huh?  It would have made more sense for her to be hitting on someone... my age ;)  Personally I think that his character hurt the movie a lot.  As an actor the kid did a great job, but his roled kinda blew.

So, to all of you who haven't seen it yet... Go.  Go twice, maybe three times... I probably will.  Just ignore all those parts with the kid in them :).  It's a truly great movie, and I'm looking forward to seeing how the next ones turn out.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-22T18:07:00Z |title=Map Generator Bugfix... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Apparently the bugfix I thought I had made hadn't actually happened in the last release, so I've gone thru the code again and found the problem. It now supports putting the minidat.mpq file in the same directory as the EXE, rather than the StarCraft directory, which is probably better in any case. I've uploaded two zip files - one with just the EXE and the other with all the required files. Sorry bout missing the problem again.

[Map Generator Upgrade](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://www.camsys.org/software/tileedit2.zip) - (427kb)

[Map Generator](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://www.camsys.org/software/tileedit.zip) - (1.02mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-22T04:52:00Z |title=Map Generator Clarification |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I feel a clarification of my post is necessary after Deathwing of Infoceptor posted about it and seemed to have mis-interpreted what I said yesterday. The minimap generator, one of several components of the program in question, still operates as before, generating small bitmap/gif/jpg images of around 18kb in size. The map generator, a brand new component, generates bitmap images of up to 64mb in size, simply because it's drawing an actual-size picture of the entire map. The Tileset Export component which saves a picture of all the tiles in a tileset generates bitmap files of approximately 8mb in size.

In other words, unlike what Deathwing presumed, I'm not forcing the minimaps that you were able to generate before into large real-size images that require 64mb of hard drive space apiece. The minimap images are still quite small and manageable for a website or hard drive of any size.

The map generator component that I introduced in the latest version is just an additonal tool available to webmasters who wish to try and generate maps that can resemble the images in Blizzard's StarCraft Compendium without much trouble.

I apologize for any confusion that yesterday's post may have inadvertantly caused; what Deathwing implied in his post is simply untrue, and he refused to post a correction when I requested one.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-20T23:42:00Z |title=Map Generator Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just finished up updating the Map Generator (formerly the Minimap Generator) with several bug fixes and adding several features, including support for generating pictures of the entire map, and for selecting a directory to place output files into. Right now, unfortunately, Doodads, Resources, and Units do not show up on either the minimap or map images. Hopefully I will be able to get them to display sometime soon. The main bugfix is one which allows you to place the minidat.mpq archive in the same directory as the TileEdit.exe file. Before it was required that you moved it into the StarCraft directory for it to work, contrary to what my documentation said.

Also note: for anyone downloading the bugfix, you will notice a non-fatal error message upon starting up TileEdit for the first time after you download the new version. This error message can be safely ignored, and will not appear again. There is no need to report it to me. The cause is simply the fact that the program is looking for new registry entries that won't exist until you exit the program again.

Please note that the map generation process is very complex, and requires a lot of your computer's resources. Because the files it generates are stored entirely on the hard drive (and never in RAM), large amounts of RAM are not required. A fast processor is of course reccommended, unless you want to stop all other activities while the map generation is taking place. For maps that are 256x256 in size, the approximate file size for the image generated is 64mb (that's because it is generated as a BMP). Automatic conversion to JPG or GIF format is not possible at this point, but hopefully I will be able to fix that shortly. Please note that the 64mb file size is generally reduced to 8mb when saving to JPG format, and that the 64mb file size is the maximum file size you will get, other map sizes require considerably less disk space. Nonetheless, watch your hard drive space carefully.

[Map Generator, v1.3](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://www.camsys.org/software/tileedit.zip) (1.2mb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-14T20:38:00Z |title=Download Problems? |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A lot of people recently have been telling me that they cannot download any of our programs properly. I guess that our FTP server is down more and more often, so what I've done is to ignore Warzone's request that we keep our files on the FTP server and have moved them back to the www server in hopes that you'll be able to get the files now. I'll keep them there until I have solid evidence from Warzone that the FTP server is back up for long enough to demonstrate that we're safe putting our files on there. I'm not going to put them on there just to keep them happy and make you guys all upset.

As an alternative, you can always visit the official CamSys alternate download site at [BWDynasty](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://www.bwdynasty.com/cam.shtml), which has all of our programs available for download, including the newly released Minimap Generator. Thanks for your patience, and I apologize for the extremely poor quality that our FTP server has had of late, but it is quite beyond my control.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-14T04:36:00Z |title=Minimap Generator Release |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've now completed work on the first public version of the Minimap Generator, of which the Tileset Editor is now part (although the Tileset Editor isn't complete yet). The loading speed for the Minimap Generator is about a billion times faster, and I also added an option so you can generate BMP's, JPG's, and GIF's (all or none)... it's worked great for me so far, and I hope that it works just as well for you. I also decided that there isn't going to be a seperate personal edition. Everyone will have to abide by the Webmaster License agreement - so if you publish material generated by this program, you will have to give us credit for it in some way.

Matrix fans: hope you enjoy my easter egg :P

[Minimap Generator and Tileset Editor v1.1](software\tileedit.zip) - (1.04mb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-13T21:47:00Z |title=Minimap Generator Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I did get the Minimap Generator to create the minimaps as JPG files now (I may add an option to do them in GIFs, too)... I've had several webmasters test it, and the only major complaint so far is the slow speed in reading the tilesets. Thankfully I'm SmartLoading the tilesets so that if you are converting a whole bunch of maps that have the same tileset, it's only loaded the first time it needs the tileset, and it stays in memory until the program closes. That has helped a lot, but it'd speed things up a great deal if I added support for storing average tile pixel values in seperate files and read those instead, cause that would be much faster :)

So I'll work on that tonight, and hopefully everything will be all set for a release tomorrow :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-11T22:30:00Z |title=ScAIEdit II Released |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Seeing as we're partial affiliates of it, I figure I'd better let you guys know.  ScAIEdit II, by Dark Knight, has reached a pre-release version, and is up for download.  It's really cool in my opinion :).  He does mention that it may still have some bugs in it, but if it's stable enough for release, it can't be that bad.  So what are you waiting for??  Head on over and pick it up!

[Official ScAIEdit Website](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://scai.infoceptor.com/)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-10T05:45:00Z |title=Minimap Generator Complete! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm pleased to announce the completion of the Minimap Generator. It doesn't generate the minimaps as jpg's yet - that will be in the works for the next version which I will start work on Wednesday night, after my last final. Until then, I'll be making the current version available to a few select webmasters to try out and see if it does everything they need it to :) If everything goes well, then the public release (hopefully with JPG support) will be towards the end of this week - it depends on how long it takes me to put in JPG support. The current version can batch process SCM's, SCX's, CHK's, and PUD's (yes, PUDs as in WarCraft II... Platinum? :) and is able to create minimaps and statistics for any of the map types. The statistics can be created as a text file or as an html file, it's up to the user to decide. All preferences can be saved upon exiting the program so that you can pick up where you left off last time.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-09T05:35:00Z |title=Minimap Generator Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've made great progress this weekend with the Minimap Generator. Not only did I finally fix the last lingering problem for non-square dimensioned StarCraft maps, but I've also added support for WarCraft II minimap generation. I am adding the ability to generate statistics files (in either text or html format - your choice) as well, and hope to be able to change the format for the saved images from Bitmaps to Jpeg before the final release. I plan on releasing two seperate versions - a private use one for SCM authors which has a few features disabled, and a webmaster useage one with additional features such as batch processing, and I may limit the HTML generation to the webmaster edition as well. Initially the webmaster release will be to a few sites of my own choosing. I will be allowing anyone to download the Webmaster edition as long as they agree to guidelines outlined in a special license agreement (which would basically state that you must give credit to me on each page of a map database if you've used my generator and you have either Server Side Includes or CGI access, or credit on the main page otherwise). I think that it's only fair to ask for the credit in return for the many hours spent on perfecting this program and making it easier for your users to get information on maps but saving you considerable work.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-08T18:56:00Z |title=Updates... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks for all those suggestions guys, and keep the comments rolling in.  I've heard a number of good ideas, and any more you have will be helpful.  Head over [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224129/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_dev/index.cgi?read=3631) to make your ideas heard.

SCMDraft has been put on a temporary hold.  Today and tomorrow are going to be spent writing up a section on the projects page for it.  I intend to have screenshots of the current layout (which will almost certainly change), some descriptions of what I plan to do, and other miscellaneous info on the program.  I'm also going to spend some more time testing some different things in maps... Mainly I'm going to see if a 12 player map is worth it :).  That and I've gotten back into playing SC after a long break, and it's getting fun again :).  I'll probably be back to work by Sunday or Monday :).

SCMDraft does now have the ability to batch edit the tiles in a map, and alter a map's tileset.  I was amazed at how easy it was to alter tilesets.  Blizz did a pretty good job of making them interchangeable.  I will be working on a 'smart converter' that will (hopefully) correct any mistakes in the map that pop up during the alteration ( Black tiles, misplaced units, etc..).  Still no date for a BETA release yet.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-07T03:30:00Z |title=Minimap Generator Coming Soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The Minimap Generator aspect of TileEdit is nearly complete. I have it working correctly for all square maps, and will be making it work with non-square maps as soon as possible. All tilesets are implimented, and batch conversion is possible. The image format for now is bitmaps, although I'll probably be changing it to JPG soon, if I can. I'll release a new beta when the minimap generator can work with all maps.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-06T20:20:00Z |title=SCMDraft Suggestions |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-03T20:13:00Z |title=SCMDraft |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Production with SCMDraft has gone very well over the recent days.  I must admit, I haven't been spending the amount of time on it that I should be, but things have still gone rather quickly.  I'm back up to the point that I was at before I destroyed the first version of it :).  I'll now be doing one of two things:

1. Doing more extensive work with the Statistics Editor, which will include more importation options, and setting up the editing portion of it.  So far it extracts and imports to all the maps I've tried, which is very good news for the time I've spent working on it.

2. I'll move on to the Trigger Editor.  Right now it looks like this will end up REALLY cool for those of you who like to make trigger heavy maps.  Plans include a mass trigger generator, which will do a LOT to simplify making those maps with long repetitive triggers that have only slight differences in each one.  I'll also see what I can do about some more advanced types of trigger editing, but I'll withhold details on that until I learn more.

Right now I'm leaning towards Trigger Editor.  I'll get the basics done with that, plus a few extras, then do some fun little layout options and release a BETA version for you all to take a look at.

And a couple people have asked me if I can tell them about this project that KA told about in his last post.  Well, no :).  I know what it is, but I'm not going to tell you :).  Trust me though, it's cool :).  I am prepared to take over the site for the summer, if the need arises, though I'm not totally sure what my exact plans would be program wise.  I'll keep you posted on that much as I decide what I would be doing.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-05-02T19:41:00Z |title=What's in store... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just for those of you who had been reading my tutorials - no I haven't forgotten about you. I've started my inital work for the 5th day's tutorial - pointers - and hope to get it finished in the next few days. Once I get that one out of the way, the next few should be easier to write cause they don't have as much.

TileEdit progress has been halted while I work on my DLL to simplify Tileset reading, etc. This is so that KramerBoy's development of SCMDraft can proceed more quickly cause he won't have to worry about duplicating my code, or trying to translate my code later. This way he'll just have a few functions to use to get the job done. Those of you interested in writing your own programs that use Tilesets, or even MPQ and CHK files will be able to have access to most if not all of the functions that I've created in the DLL. I'll be publishing the prototypes for the functions so that they can be used in your own apps.

As for what we're going to be up to this summer, KramerBoy will probably be left in charge of the majority of the CS site because Merlin and I have a rather extensive project that we may be working on. We will be available to update StarDraft if Blizzard makes a 1.06 patch, but aside from that, don't expect any new software from our end until the summer is over. I'm sorry, but it can't be helped. We'll have more details on the summer project once we know more.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=April 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-29T00:51:00Z |title=AI Editor Update (finally) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin was supposed to make this update ages ago, and when he finally decided that today we could update you all on the AI Editor, he asks me to do the update. (GRR!) Anyways the bottom line is this: Merlin has been working with Dark Knight, author of SCAIEdit, in hopes that their combined efforts can produce the optimal editor. Merlin basically gave up on the idea of a seperate camsys editor because it was simply too time consuming for him to do the entire thing on his own, even with all the research I had already done, and all the progress he had made. So he's given Dark Knight a lot of pointers and advice about how to do everything much more smoothly, and he's shared with him all the knowledge that we had pertaining to the AI's. So Dark Knight will be releasing the new AI Editor here, and it will be the second version that many of you may have heard about (and I must admit it looks very impressive/professional). Unfortunately this is Merlin and Dark Knight's project now, and I don't have any more info to share with you on the matter. Merlin's just informed me that he has about 4 weeks worth of heavy workloads for school, so it's doubtful that I'll find out much more from him for a while.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-29T00:34:00Z |title=Tileset Editor Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I finally got back to work last night. I'm in the midst of moving a lot of functions into a DLL so that other people can take advantage of easier ways to load MPQ's, create Bitmaps, etc. I'll make the DLL public, and release its import library for anyone creating a C++ program. Visual Basic users should also be able to take advantage of the DLL (I think)... I'll check and see which functions (if any) are incompatible.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-29T00:31:00Z |title=More Columbine Responses |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just thought I'd share with you a few emails:

(Names removed to prevent backlash)

> We've all heard of the Littleton tragedy, even in our remote
> (relatively) corner in Vladivostok. I can't say that's not typical. Such
> things, sadly, happen often. Now i can't exactly remember where and when
> the last such thing happened, but it does happen. As far as I know, it
> only happens in USA. Well, violence isn't unique to any given country.
> Several times we here had troubles, with recruit soldiers attacking
> their "comrades" that oppressed them, but... happening in schools?
> Never! Okay, our schoolchildren could fight, but guns - never! (And
> almost no one has guns on his own - only policemen or military...)
>
>> I don't think that "oppression" is the root of evil in that case.
> No. I think it is "too much freedom", uncontrolled "freedom" that turns
> into evil. People in your country think that they are the freest in the
> world and thus... can do everything they want. When I travelled to
> America and stayed in the host family, their son constantly told me that
> "America is a free country, and Russia is not", but he couldn't explain
> the meaning. He just believed in that, like some believe in God.
>
> That idea also can be seen in a very different - but very similar -
> situation. The Cosovo Crisis. USA and NATO think they are "the free" and
> therefore can tell the world what to do. Those who disobey and try to
> protect their "not free" way of thinking will be judged, and death
> penalties will be executed accordingly.
>
> I'm not goin' to post the whole lot I think about Cosovo and all the
> world, but I do say the way of thinking the American government was
> knocking into the heads of people since WWII, rooted and flowered. First
> generation was brought into Vietnam War, and the second - into Littleton
> and other numerous cases. And Cosovo, too. It is the way of thinking
> that the Americans are so proud of must be changed. it won't happen in a
> year, nor even in ten years. But if it is preserved, I've got a serious
> reason to seek escape from this planet.

------------

> Your post was right in many ways, but there are 2 kinds of tolerance, I
> think.  One is the one you mentioned, but the other one would be
> tolerating someone's stupidity (I can't think of a better way to put
> it).
>
> What I am about to say may sound a little immature, but for me, it's
> the dire truth.
>
> I have a math teacher.  Algebra, to be precise.  I'm in 8th grade (yes,
> quite young, I know), and up until this year I have never gotten
> anything below a C+ on my report card.  NEVER.  But all of that seemed
> to change this year.  The teacher that we have (I won't mention her
> name) is INCREDIBLY stupid (again, it may be immature, but it's the
> truth), and she cannot teach the class.  At first, I got a C on my
> report card when the first quarter was over.  'No biggie', I thought,
> 'I'll start doing better'.  But that didn't happen.  I got an F next
> quarter.  AN F!  The first one in my entire life, and it was because of
> my math teacher's incompetence!  And you know, I was getting A's and B's
> in EVERY other class!
>
> Okay, yeah, you may think it may be MY fault I got the F.  Well, to my
> knowledge, it wasn't.  40% of the class failed that quarter.  40%!  And
> she is still teaching???  Many parents have called to complain about
> it.  Many of them indeed.
>
> Also a new record this year:  number of times I have been called to the
> office.  And half, no, three-fourths, of the times I was called down
> were about Algebra.
>
> And yet she STILL has a job.  And I STILL have her for a teacher.
>
> It has gotten to the point where my parents do not care how well I do
> in her class.  My mom said (and I quote) "She's a twit".  Another one of
> my friends, named Bart, doesn't care so much that he doesn't do the work
> at all.  He finished a quiz in 2 minutes, whereas the rest of the class
> didn't have enough time to finish it by the end of class.  From what he
> says, his grade percentage is a 54% (probably lower).  Thats an F, and
> there is no chance in hell that he can raise that by the time report
> cards roll around.
>
> I have tolerated her for all of the school year, and many times I have
> been driven to the brink of insanity.  Well, I'm not sure where insanity
> is, but I think I've gotten pretty close.  Not so close as to go killing
> people, but pretty close.  I'm not sure what the purpose of this letter
> was, but I guess it was to prove there is another kind of tolerance.
> That, and I really needed to vent this.

-------------

> I have to agree with your's and slashdot's opinions about Columbine.
> While in my school it is not nearly as oppresive as some others, i'm
> stuck in a situation where the jocks are always being heralded (even by
> teachers), while everyone else is left in the dark.  One person in my
> school gets congratulated on his single goal in his hockey game, while
> i'm here with 2 best of my class math competition tests (yes, all +900
> of them in Council Rock, i forget the exact numbers), one being a tie
> between me and my friend .  AND WE NEVER GOT MENTIONED.  Not even on
> those dumb morning announcements where the jocks get all their glory.
> And now we aren't allowed to wear coats in school b/c the adminstrators
> are afraid of violence in school (meanwhile they also closed the school
> last Friday because of a stupid rumor a kid started to skip Report Card
> day).  Ugh.  Well, at least when we're adults, i'll be sure to laugh at
> all those dumb broke jocks while i work for a gaming company making a
> decent amount of money.

-------------

> I have to agree for the most part about what he is saying. our country is currently in a state of panic over what happened in the small "hic" town that no one has ever herd of. If something like that were to happen in an LA school, people would blame it on gang violence and be over it within a week.
>
> every school has its groups, mine is no exception. At South High School in Torrance California, the major factions are (in order of Dominance) the Tree Boys: named this because of the large pine tree that they hang ouy under at lunch. This group is composed entirely of surfers who dont care about much that happens around them. All they want is beer, sex, and waves. Harrassment is limited to verbal sexual harrassment of attractive "chicks". The second group would be the "in crowd". These are the people who everyone wants to be like. They are the popular ones. They generally play baseball or vollyball. Harrasment is non-existant here. Most popular people dont know they are popular and dont care. Next we come to the "Super Jocks". these are the dumb football players. They suck at their game and act like queers so no one fears them. Yes they are big and they think they are better than everyone else, but no bark and no one takes it seriously when someone gets punked by them. The Final Superpower is the "KP" crowd. KP is for Korean Pride. not all are korean, but who can tell so thats the general name. They arnt mean to anyone unless you piss them off. These people play basketball, book worm, or play tennis. There are the other groups including ones that listen to death-metal and wear trenchcoats, but they seem to isolate themselves when in groups so no one cares to notice. Marijuana is the drug of choice for a lot of people. Being a school that is very intigrated, Id guess we could be a model to schools with the classic problem of people getting picked on. everyone can find friends (just dont admit to being homosexual for another few years). Also, no one is afraid of computers because most of the school's students have one at home. One thing you will notice is those in team sports group together. This may be a way to solve  the problem in other schools (punish the team).
>
>     The bottom line is that i dont expect the littletown incident to be repeated in a big city. when kids have nothing to do, they find something to do. be it guns, sports or computers, everyone does something they enjoy.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-28T02:08:00Z |title=Columbine Outrage |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I can no longer stay silent on this issue: the littleton massacre. Originally I may have intended to post
a message condemning lack of gun control, etc for the murders, but a [post on Slashdot](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/http://slashdot.org/articles/99/04/25/1438249.shtml) that {{highlight |text=Tender Fury}} told me about. I ***highly*** reccomend this post if you consider yourself
an intellectual, a frequent computer user, or if you're depressed/oppressed in your school.

Responses:

----------

> Your (the slashdot) commentary is an excellent analysis of everything that is wrong with schools from the student's
> point of view - this is the exact type of document that the school administrators should be reading,
> not some burecratic nonsense drafted up by some 75 year old computer-illiterate. The fact of the
> matter seems to be that technology is causing a great divide in society between those that revel
> in it and those that are frightened by it and its users. I think that's why so many people all accross
> America are afraid of people who are high-frequency/skill computer users; they fear the unknown
> and since they've never experienced what they're trying to oppress for themselves, they cannot
> possibly fathom what they are doing - alienating us as well.
>
> I think it will take a lot of work on our (the computer user's) part to help educate our parents,
> friends, teachers, and school administrators that technology is a very good thing, it's here
> to stay, and at least the most basic concepts about it must be understood by everyone if we are
> going to live in any semblance of harmony.
>
> Shootings like the Colombine massacre are the result of years of oppression, ridicule, and pent
> up aggression. I strongly believe that any human being who was oppressed for any great
> length of time would lash out at their oppressors when there is no other choice left to them.
> People are struggling to find somewhere to place the blame. I think that we all need to learn to
> accept a small portion of the responsibility for these deaths, but mostly for the fact that we
> did not seek to help the assailants more quickly.
>
> As much as I detest saying it, I believe that we should be able to live in a society where people
> like the Trenchcoat Mafia can believe in whatever they want, even if it includes nazism. I am in
> no way condoning nazism, but I believe that if we live in a democracy, each citizen should
> have a right to believe in whatever system of beliefs they like, so long as they do not try to forcibly
> manipulate the idealogy of anyone else.
>
> I'd be foolish to think that any such could ever occur in my lifetime, but it's a goal that we can
> strive for; a country where any society is welcome to believe anything it wants and do anything
> it wants so long as it does not violate anyone else's human rights.
>
> Without tolerance and acceptance, there will undoubtedly be more explosions (both literal
> and figurative) of violence accross the country, and unfortunately they will undoubtedly only
> make the problem worse due to the sensationalistic viewpoint the media always takes.
> Persecuting people who sympathize with the columbine killers because of their long history
> of being oppressed seems like it's just as bad as what the killers themselves did, for it will
> serve to further alienate people who will most likely only lash out in the future.
> I've been on the recieving end of many taunts by my fellow class-mates because I pulled
> a few pranks and got suspended in middle school. I managed to live through that experience
> because I realized that what I did was irresponsible, but the thoughts behind what I did
> were not. Deciding to rebel against authority is nothing new. People have done it for
> thousands of years, and in less than a month, we'll see it happening again in a huge way
> on the big screen (I.e. Episode 1). Benjamin Franklin (I believe) once said that a truly
> successful democracy would be overthrown every few years to make sure that the people
> of the country would still be getting accomplished what they wanted. I believe that
> this belief, somewhat modified, should be the guiding principle behind what we strive
> to do in the next millenia: reach out to everyone and involve them all in some level,
> some form of a decision making process that most affects them.
>
> I know my ideas are really too optimistic and/or extreme to ever happen, but I think
> that some sort of compromise which encorporates their essense needs to be achieved,
> and quickly, too.
>
> Thanks for letting me rant.
> King Arthur

[Responses](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com) welcome and encouraged!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-27T20:25:00Z |title=SCMDraft Update |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Programming on SCMDraft was going very well up until last night... then I decided to scrap it all and remake it :).  Basically I decided to use a new method of editing, with which I am rather unfamiliar.  But from my own testing, and some example code, I have been able to get a good grasp on what is required to achieve a good SCM Editor.  One far better than it would have been had I used my previous methods, mostly because they were clumsy and annoying to work with.

So far, I am planning on definetly doing a statistical editor, and an advanced trigger compiler.  Things are looking good for a layout editor also, as KA's work with tilesets will help me a lot with my work.  I'll probably end up adding the Minimap Generator to SCMDraft if KA does one, but as I see it, it would be best if he released the generator separate, so you guys don't have to wait forever for it to come out with SCMDraft.  Other little things I hope to include are a Decorate map function, that will add Doodads, and things like that.  Generating 'Black Maps' ( Maps where the entire tileset is plain black... may not be very useful though, I need to do more research ), or making a map consist of all one tile ( looks really cool :).  I will also allow the changing of Tilesets and map sizes.  You will be able to change the race of a player without having to worry about StarEdit removing all its units, so you can have players of more than one race without having to use triggers.  Of course, I'll include all the other stuff that StarEdit does, except maybe sound importing.

I think I'll release a Beta once I finish the Statistical Editor, and probably the Trigger Compiler, and some of the map options.   Unless I find that I can complete most of it easily, in which case I'll simply do a full release.  I rather doubt Layout editing will come easy though :(.  But watch for a Beta coming up in the next week or two.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-26T03:36:00Z |title=Weekend gone already? |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well unfortunately the weekend is gone already, and I managed to get absolutely no work done on the Tileset Editor or my Tutorials. Big surprise, I guess. This week is my last week of classes. I've got a lot of stuff to finish up for my CompSci class on Tuesday, so I don't expect to be on much on Monday night. Tuesday night and the rest of the week I may be able to work on either the Editor or the tutorials. Sorry I didn't get to either this weekend. Yesterday my parents kicked me out of the house and made me go golfing (either that or mow the lawn), and today i was pretty sore cause I haven't done 18 holes in like 6 months so I just played X-Wing vs. Tie Fighter (just bought it). I tell you, one thing I'm pissed at LucasArts for is the fact that they took out the Tie Defender and the Missile Boat. GRR. All the ships suck, basically. I was going to buy X-Wing Alliance next, but if the ships in XvT are any indication of what's to come, I will pass that one on by for a while.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-21T04:13:00Z |title=Tileset Editor Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I'm very pleased to find out that so far pretty much everyone that's contacted me has enjoyed using the Tileset "Extractor" :) I worked on the load/save code some more last night and tonight, and it's much faster when saving the bitmap than it was before :) I am planning on adding two main features to the Tileset Editor: The ability to load a SCM/SCX and either generate a bitmap of the entire map, or a bitmap of the minimap. Doing the bitmap generation for the entire map will probably take forever, and possibly require up to 67megabytes per map file, which obviously is going to require serious compression (by saving it as a JPG) on the user's part, and probably shrinking it down, too.

In fact, I think I'm going to work on those aspects of the editor before I work on the editor itself, basically cause they can be finished quickly. I have been talking with Indoflaven of StarCraft.org, and I think I'm going to try to create a special version that will allow them to use it on their website to generate minimaps when scms are subtmitted (which would be _very_ cool if it happens).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-19T04:52:00Z |title=Tileset Editor Alpha |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've uploaded the Tileset Editor Alpha test, which is basically just an extractor for now. I'd appreciate it if as many people as possible could download it and test it out. Right now just click on the Tileset->Load menu option and follow the next few screens after that. It will create a bitmap file on your hard drive with all of the tiles in a tileset. It is very cool to see them all there :)

As I get more time this week and next, I'll be expanding the Editor's capabilities so that you can import graphics, too.

[Tile Editor (Alpha)](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/tileedit.zip) - (300kb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-17T21:46:00Z |title=BGE bugs fixed! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well my first (and last) venture into Baldur's Gate editing is now complete. I won't be doing any more research for the character files, item files or the spell files, this editor will be the last version I upload unless there are more undiscovered bugs to fix. Sorry I had to wait so long to upload it, but I decided I might as well just upload it and let the shit hit the fan.

[BGE](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/bge.zip) - (400kb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-17T18:21:00Z |title=Tilesets |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Today I decided I wanted to try and go through the Tileset files and find out which ones held the tile images... I managed to find them pretty quickly, and I'm in the process of figuring out what the rest of the files do. I've started to convert the beginning part of the tileset image file into bitmaps to look over later. The format I think is almost identical fo the WarCraft II tilesets. Unfortunately there are around 32000 minitiles in the file I'm looking at, and that's way more than Alex Cech's old Tileset Converter for WarCraft II can handle. I can't even make a single bitmap of the tilesets... I've got to break it up into may different smaller ones. I may work up a converter soon which will translate the image files into bitmaps... not sure yet tho. Looks promising so far :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-16T23:41:00Z |title=Good Thing That's Over With... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ahh well.  I think I got SDT working correctly, because I haven't been getting 20-30 emails a day telling me I screwed up again :).  Again, I apologize for all the trouble I caused.  Those of you who were reluctant to get SDT because it wasn't working needn't worry any longer, just make sure you download the system files before you complain that it won't run :).  I *still* need some more input from you guys, so EMail me about it RIGHT NOW!! :)

For the future, I think I'll be occupying myself with a SCM/SCX utility (SCMDraft).  So far,  I plan to have the ability to import/export statistical data (weapons, upgrades, units, special abilites), a trigger compiler, and probably the ability to export minimap screenshots.  Another handy feature of it will be that you can edit statistics for all 228 units, so customs makers can more effectively customize their levels.  I *may* do a layout editor, but those are fairly advanced, and right now I haven't done enough research to accomplish this task. If I do one, it will probably allow you to do a lot of the stuff that StarEdit won't let you.  Changing terrains, altering map sizes, and placing stuff where SE doesn't want you to ;).  A few people have suggested making a terrain editor, but at this point I have no plans of doing that.  If I do, it will be part of SCMDraft.

Unfortunately, for those of you waiting for SCUD, I am officially canceling the project.  Without specs on how units move, I cannot make an effective multi-unit dueler.  I could make a single unit dueler, but there are already programs that do this, so just making another one wouldn't be worth the effort.

So there should be new software coming in the future, but don't bug me about it yet :).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-14T20:54:00Z |title=ARGHH! |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok, I'm going to admit, for those of you who *still* can't get SDT to work, I haven't the slightest idea why.  I set the thing to build without runtime libraries, and according to every source I have, I did everything necessary to make this program work stand-alone.  But, apparently I'm still in error.

So, I'm just going to put up the files that you need to run it.  Keep in mind, that I have no effective way to actually test whether these are the only one's that you'll need.  The files can't be moved, deleted, or renamed on my comp, and I don't feel like doing anything more than that to try it.  So there may well be more, but hell...what's one more mistake?  I'm just plain pissed off at myself right now for all of this.  This release turned out to be a complete disaster, and I apparently still haven't corrected my errors...

Just put these in your windows\system directory.  Then I shouldn't have to give you any more when I release any other programs.  Hopefully this will be the last time I need to post about this, but like I said, I'm only 'Pretty Sure' that this is all you will need.

[Download Runtime Packages](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/runtime.zip) - 1.1 MB

Again, tell me if I'm making another mistake... And in the case that I'm actually right, I'd like to know too.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-14T02:07:00Z |title=Bah... I knew this would happen... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I apologize to you all for make mistakes at one point or another all throughout this project, and I realize that with the amount of time I took to complete this, I should not have made all the mistakes that I did.  This reflects badly on me and CS, and I regret causing all of this trouble.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-13T03:35:00Z |title=Programming Tutorial Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've been working on Day #4 of the Game Editor Programming Tutorial tonight. I wanted to get it done yesterday, but due to so many people talking to me yesterday I couldn't get both day 3 and day 4 done. I also changed the topic from Pointers to Functions because although I'd wanted to get functions introduced as early on as possible, I felt that people reading the tutorial wouldn't really see the use of them until after I introduced functions. So there you have it.

I'm finding that I'm going into a lot more detail with this day's worth of the tutorial than I did previously. I think that trend's going to continue because the stuff gets more complex and involved later on, requiring more examples, explanations, etc. (of course, right? :)

Anyways I plan on working on it for a little while longer before I call it a night. I've got a big CompSci test tomorrow and I don't want to totally fail it :) Hehe yeah in case you're wondering it's ***not*** on C++. Wish it was. I could use another really good grade in my stupid compsci class. But I digress.

If it's not done today, it should definately be done tomorrow. Day 5 will take a while cause I'll be covering more now that Functions are being dealt with before Pointers. So that one may not get done until Thursday. I'd say Wednesday, but unfortunately I have a physics test on Thursday that I really need to do well on... so I won't be on Wednesday at all basically.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-13T02:20:00Z |title=Finally!! SDT Released!! |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ahh... finally... It's compete.  SDTutor has now taken it's full form.

I am going to put it pretty bluntly though... This program is designed for the low to medium skill level SD user.  Those of you above that won't find much out of this one.  But it should help out those of you who are new to SD, and can't wait to start shooting out mods of your own.  It may do some good to have some of the others DL it, because, as you'll read below, I need some feedback from a diveresed group.  I plan on elaborating more on some of the tutorials in the future, and possibly adding more as our knowledge of SC increases.

For those of you wondering what I did about the bug I was working with... I just totally avoided the whole thing.  I redid the Unit tutorial as an HTML, and removed it from the program.  I regret taking this step, but I was going insane trying to get everything to work correctly, and I just decided the only other way to beat the bug was to remove the whole section.

I would like to note though, that towards the end of production I wasn't quite sure whether this was the best approach to take on such a task.  I began to wonder whether an HTML would be a better way to do it.  But I ended up being 50-50 on the whole thing, so I decided to go ahead and release it and find out what you thought.  After DLing SDT and taking a look around, I want to hear your ideas on how it was done.  This is the first program-based tutorial done by CS as far as I know, and any input you have would help dictate how our tutorials are done in the future, so your opinion does matter.  Just EMail me sometime and tell me what you think.  I'd like to hear from as many people as possible.  I won't necessarily be *replying* to everyone but I'll certainly read your response.

But enough of this chit-chat!

[Download SDTutor v1.0](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/SDTutor.zip) - 1.16 MB
{{end-newsitem}}

The following week didn't get much better, and I again, I got little work done. That weekend I had started to get somewhere, but I soon realized that there had been a saving error, and I lost a lot of work I had done. Spring Break came, and I pretty much refused to do any work for that week, including SDT. I had made up for my lost time by mid next week, and continued programming at a pretty good rate, until that Sunday, when my parents happened to screw up our comp...

...Following that, I have basically gone through the rather annoying task of debugging everything. I can't seem to get the orders part of SDT to work correctly with Arsenal and StarDraft. At least that's about all I have left to fix, and if I can't figure out what's going wrong within a matter of days, I'll change my plans to avoid the bug.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-11T01:12:00Z |title=SCMapCentral morphs into StarCraft Conclave |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After a year of work and being hosted by Camelot Systems, Moritz and Willow, formerly of SCMapCentral, have changed their URL, site title, and philosophy. The new site is called the [StarCraft Conclave](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/http://www.crystallized.com/conclave/), and is now concentrating on player-reviewed maps, rather than a staff of reviewers that analyze every user-submitted map. While I'm sorry to see them go, I wish them the best of luck in their new venture.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-06T00:46:00Z |title=The Matrix |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Some of you may be a little upset with me for this abnormal breach of what I like to think is professionalism, but I think that you'll forgive me after you see the movie I'm about to reccomend :)

Simply put, The Matrix is one of the best movies I have ever seen. My brother, a person who hasn't seen a sci-fi movie in his life that he liked absolutely loved this movie. The Matrix could be considered the Braveheart of the Sci-Fi world. In short, I very highly reccomend that you see this movie... again, and again, and again. I plan to see it at least 2 or 3 more times before it's out of the theaters (In the words of StarShack's Chaplan: "Yes it is *that* good").

Yeah, Star Wars: The Phantom Menace will probably blow the Matrix out of the water, but for now I am quite enchanted by it. Don't pass this one up :) Feel free to [email](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com) me about it, I'll be more than happy to discuss it. :)

For those of you who have already seen it (aside from seeing it again), I have good news for you: there are plans to make it into a trilogy (yes that means two more movies), plans which hinged on the financial success of the movie. Seeing as how it opened last weekend with the biggest box-office pull yet this year, that certainly looks possible :) And I cannot wait :)
{{end-newsitem}}
