{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-05T04:52:00Z |title=Status Updates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well Merlin was supposed to have posted about the AI Editor status today, but he didn't cause he was busy doing other stuff. I'll leave it to him to tell you the news because he wanted to do it himself. Hopefully this will provoke him into posting about it :)

Anyways, I have been talking at great length to members of another game-editing site, TeamBG, which develops Baldur's Gate utilities. Turns out they were upset by the fact that I made an editor in the first place, and that I will be releasing the source code, too. I can understand their complaints, especially because this is the first game they have edited, and right now their main goal is to establish a foot-hold in their own community much as we did (and continue to do). Even though BGE isn't as good as their editor (mainly because it's still incomplete), they still feel it will result in competition which could drive them "out of business". So towards preventing that end, I intend to try and work with them and also limit access to the source code/tutorials for BGE, probably on an email-for-password basis. I haven't completely made up my mind, however.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-02T01:32:00Z |title=Programming and HTML Resources |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Since we're going to be covering our own programming project, I thought I'd share with you links to some of my favorite programming websites. I've included links to C/C++, PERL, CGI, HTML, and Visual Basic resources, almost all of which I have visited. I hope that you find them as useful as I did.

[Programming Links](links/plinks.htm)

[Web Programming Links](links/weblinks.htm)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-01T05:46:00Z |title=Camelot Systems Search Engine Online |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just finished uploading and testing out the brand new Camelot Systems Search Engine. I added a small text box at the bottom of the menu bar on the left hand side where you can type in some search terms and hit enter or click on the search button. It won't search the entire site yet cause I haven't given it access to everything, but it'll find almost everything that you would want (hopefully). [Let me know](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com) if there's something wrong with the search engine... if you have a difficult time coming up with results, you should look at [Alta Vista](https://web.archive.org/web/20000818043606/http://www.altavista.digital.com/)'s search tips. The authors of this program used the same syntax as that search engine.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-04-01T04:06:00Z |title=More page upgrades... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Today I worked on expanding the Forum script a bit more so that our menu, SLW, etc appeared on every page when you click on a specific message. That should make navigation much easier, and the load time seems to be just as fast as the Server Side Includes on the rest of the pages, so hopefully it won't cause you too much delay. [Let me know](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com) if it does, or if something's not working correctly.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=March 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-31T22:54:00Z |title=Upcoming Projects |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Great news is in store for those of you interested in finding out how to program, specifically how to program game editors. Starting around the end of May, I plan to start an in-depth analyzation of how to make a BGE/Arsenal II like program. (or if Diablo II is out, I'll do one for that). But probably it will be BGE. That means I'm going to be tackling the entire problem I faced online, for everyone to see, and with completely open source code. However, this time around it's not going to be just any ordinary open source code. I will be doing a full in-depth analyzation of the problem, its solution, and the creation of the editor. I will be re-writing BGE as I write the webpages so that I can improve my now very silly code. I will start out from scratch and proceed through the entire project (if possible) eventually ending up with a completed program.

Merlin also plans to do the same thing (probably starting in June) by creating a basic CWAD Manager. This will be for the more advanced game-editing people, and will hopefully start a trend of using CWADs when patching other games (because they're much better than Baldur's Gate's Override directory, or the like). His project will also be open source and as completely documented as possible.

If Diablo II does come out towards the end of the summer, then we'll obviously try and gear our projects towards that game to try and solve two big projects at once.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-31T04:33:00Z |title=Page updates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I went through today and updated the vast majority of the pages to all use the same menu, ad/button bar, and slw files in the server side includes (before I had a seperate copy in subdirectories). As a result, this has speeded up (at least for me) the loading time of each page dramatically since it no longer re-loads images from the menu, etc every time you change directories on the server by navigating pages. Please [let me know](mailto:kingarthur@warzone.com) if it's gotten worse for you.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-30T02:17:00Z |title=AI Editor and FunCraft Status Report |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin's out of town until this Friday on vacation. Before he left on Saturday, he told me that he plans to finish up the AI Editor while he's gone (on the laptop) and he'll try and send it to me via email on Wednesday so I can post it. If he can't send it, then we should have it uploaded sometime this weekend.

I've got Friday off from college, and I think I'm going to work on some FunCraft stuff... try and get an Arthur CWAD completed (sounds and Tim's graphics) and uploaded.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-27T04:16:00Z |title=BGE Pre-release Beta |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've basically finished up just about everything with BGE now... there are a few bugs I am aware of that are still in the program, but nothing too serious (that you can't load more than one file right now, and that spell orders may be mixed up). Other than that, I thought that I'd upload the editor and let you all have a spin. Please let me know about the interface... I'm especially concerned about that. Basically I would like your comments on that, although anything else you want to report is fine, too.

[BGE 1.08 (Pre-Release)](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/bge.zip) - (408kb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-23T04:46:00Z |title=BGE |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I managed to finish all of the loading code this weekend... BGE (Baldur's Gate Editor) now properly loads any creature or character file. And as of yesterday it saved everything correctly, too. At least as long as you make no changes to the spells you know. Unfortunately for unknown reasons I can't get characters that I add spells to their knowledge base to function properly when imported into the game. My guess is that I'm missing some crucial detail for spells that is stored in the character file. I think what I'm going to do is go through and disable the code that allows changes to spells, and then release the editor until I can figure out what's going wrong. I should therefore have it ready for release by Wednesday (I hope) in its initial test version. Just in case you care, BGE is now up to 10,000 lines of C++ code. That's the most by far of any program I've worked on, except maybe SPUD Farmer (which was evil). Now more usually isn't better, but in this case its required to recognize inventory and spells, etc., and to make editing them easy on the user.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-21T22:54:00Z |title=Coast is Clear... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Pheww... that was close.  Apparently all my studying last week paid off, as I went from a D to a B in less than a week in reading :).  This means that I won't have to worry about getting kicked off the computer at all once report cards come out.  And now I'm actually starting to find some free time on my hands, so I should start getting some work done around here.

SDTutor is now in its final stages, and, if you ask me, is looking pretty cool.  I have some cleaning up to do in the weapons tutorial, a couple of things to add to the StarDraft tutorial, and have to test the stuff.  I see a release version being finished tomorrow, but with the AI Editor coming so well, I *think* I'll hold back until that's out, so I can include a tutorial on it.  This would actually speed up the thing, because then KA and Merlin wouldn't have to worry about writing up some complicated documentation on it.  If, for some reason, the Editor won't be out this week, I'll go ahead and release SDTutor anyway, and do a second release for the AI Editor.


After SDTutor, I'm basically gonna do whatever KA tells me to for a while, until I can get something of my own started... I have some plans, but at this point I'm not going to mention anything quite yet.  Just be on the watch for SDTutor sometime later this week.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-19T05:27:00Z |title=AI Editor and Baldur's Gate Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just wanted to keep you guys up to date on our two main projects:

Work on the AI Editor had been going well up until yesterday... I never heard from Merlin, and I haven't heard from him today, so I'm not quite sure where he is at right now. Last I saw it was reading all of the normal AI Scripts fine, and read almost all the Brood War scripts with only a few exceptions. I probably won't be around too much tomorrow night (last day of my spring break) so I won't see Merlin (probably) until Saturday. He's been pretty busy this week with tests, etc, so I'm not surpised I haven't seen too much of him. He may decide to make an update tomorrow while I'm gone to let you know what's going on.

The Baldur's Gate Editor had ok progress the last two days. I didn't spend all day on the editor like I did on Monday and Tuesday, so I didn't get as much done. I've added all the necessary code to manipulate/add/remove spells in a Creature's stats. I also added all the lists necessary for Inventory Editing. Tomorrow I'm going to work on the loading code for the Inventory. After that's done, then I do the Save code... hopefully the latter half of tomorrow and Saturday. After then I hope to have it mostly done, with only a few minor things to do on Sunday, cause I have other stuff I need to get done before the Vacation is over. Although the Editor will eventually support Spell and Item editing, I'm going to release an initial version of the Editor with only Character and Creature editing support.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-16T05:01:00Z |title=Baldur's Gate Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Work went very smoothly today on my Baldur's Gate Editor. I managed to get Character Spell lists working on load... I'll probably be posting screenshots from the program tomorrow if I can. The creature editor portion was getting so big I decided to just stick the whole thing into its own window. I'll make 2 more windows for the Item and Spell Editors. CodeLord lost two of his partitions and KramerBoy as you could see yesterday was slightly busy so I may end up researching the Item and Spell formats on my own after I finish the Character/Creature editor. I may release a beta of just the Character editor soon... which will be after I get Inventory reading added, and then of course the inevitable saving code :P Oh and this will have support for custom spells and items, of course :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-15T01:33:00Z |title=It's all a conspiracy.. |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I know, I know.  I said SDTutor would be out this weekend.    Of course, then I got slammed with homework, had two major tests to take in two classes, that would determine whether I get a 'B' on my report card or my parents behead me.  On top of all of this, Blizzard releases a new patch, meaning that I couldn't test out some aspects of the program until tonight, with a new SD version being needed.  Of course, then Merlin has to go and change the layout of CWAD Manager, meaning that I need to alter my CWAD Manager tutorial.  With the AI editor going as smoothly as it is, I would be releasing a new version in a matter of days anyway, to include support for it.  I think that my teachers, Merlin, my parents, and Blizzard are all working together to keep this program from being released :).  So all in all, a release tonight just didn't seem logical, or possible given the time frame that this new patch has put me in. Sorry about getting your hopes up, but it looks like I'll be releasing SDTutor when the next version of StarDraft, or the AI editor is released.  Oh well :).

Also, I'd just like to note that I've changed my EMail address to KramerBoy@warzone.com .  Those of you who have already sent stuff to the old address don't need to resend, but I would ask that all new EMail be directed to the warzone address.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-14T15:06:00Z |title=Blizzard Internal AI Scripts |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The last release of StarDraft was a nightmare :) I just fixed several major bugs. Thansk to Zeramid, I discoverd StarCraft didn't like my group files, that along with several CWAD manager bugs and 1 bug in PLOADER have been mended. In other news KA and I have been hard at work on the AI files, hopefully soon we will be putting out our full compiler/decompiler package, it seems to be coming along quickly, so stay tuned!

[StarDraft (for v1.05)](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.camsys.org/software/sdsetup.exe) - (2.3mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-14T15:06:00Z |title=Blizzard Internal AI Scripts |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just uploaded the new version (1.05 compatible) of the Blizzard Internal AI Scripts. It's a self-executing CWAD, so it doesn't require StarDraft to run. I also moved the Nuke Train AI Scripts towards the bottom of the list (near the other special AI's) and renamed them so they're easier to find. It's reverse compatible, so it shouldn't effect your older scm's.

[Blizzard Internal AI's](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.camsys.org/software/newais.zip) - (523kb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-14T14:19:00Z |title=StarDraft news... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Apparently Merlin disabled TBLEdit in the latest version of StarDraft that he uploaded yesterday (the 1.05 compatible one). As a temporary fix until he can get the problem solved, I've uploaded the latest version of my TBLEdit for you to use in the meantime.

[TBLEdit](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.camsys.org/software/tbledit.zip) - (15kb)

[Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.camsys.org/software/sysfiles.zip) - (1.5mb)

Remember you only need to download the Runtimes if you haven't done so before.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-14T04:48:00Z |title=StarDraft for 1.05 |author=Merlin |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just finished up getting StarDraft working with StarCraft/Brood War 1.05. I know that KA said that we'd wait to release the 1.05 version until our AI editor was ready, but we decided we'd be better off releasing SD now in case the AI editor took longer than expected. This version should have our GRP editor as a plugin in CWAD Manager.

[StarDraft (for v1.05)](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.camsys.org/software/sdsetup.exe) - (2.4mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-14T01:02:00Z |title=AI Editor gets some competition... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just to be fair to our competiton, I thought I'd post that _Dark_Knight_ has finished an initial version of his AI Editor. Right now it only supports creating Terran AI scripts (and you can only create one script per file), and can decompile only some Blizzard AI scripts. You can visit his [website](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://scaiedit.somewhere.net/) for more information. Apparently Infoceptor will be swallowing him up tho, chalking up yet another thing they hope to use to bring us at CS to our knees and worship Infoceptor as god of all things StarCraft.

On a related note, Merlin and I made great progress on our own AI editor, which some unauthorized people got a sneak peek at earlier this week. While the version they saw resembled _Dark_Knight_'s AI Editor, it was only an internal-release design. We plan to impliment a trigger-like interface as developed in StarEdit for creating your AI Scripts. We've also been spending a large amount of time in decoding as many AI Script instructions as we can. As it stands now, our decompiler for Blizzard's scripts can correctly interpret a large portion of the AI scripts, including almost all Terran and Protoss.

Finally, I managed to discover what I believe is the AI Script instruction that forces a specific unit to use its special ability. This should prove to be a very useful tool to campaign/customs developers who have been unable to force units to use their spells with any regularity (aside from using the AI Recall/Disruption Web scripts).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-12T03:31:00Z |title=Baldur's Gate Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The BGE, as I'm calling it, is proceeding along very well. I have completed all the initial parts of the character/creature editor. All I have to do is add inventory and spell book/scroll support and that part should be completed. After that's done, i will be moving onto tackle the Item editor and the Spell editor. I did discover quite a lot about the Character files, at least more than was previously known by everyone else, so they will be fairly customizeable. :) Hope the same is true of the Items and Spells when we get to them.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-12T03:29:00Z |title=AI Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just wanted to keep you up to date on the AI editor as well - Merlin finished the AI decompiler on Monday, but I have to find some more AI instructions before we can start work on the compiler. I should be able to work on the AI's all next week (which is spring break for me) and hopefully get all the info we need gathered by then.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-12T03:27:00Z |title=StarDraft for patch 1.05 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A lot of people have emailed KramerBoy and I asking when we will have out a version of StarDraft compatible with StarCraft/Brood war 1.05. I emailed Merlin Tuesday with the info that the new patch was out, and hopefully this weekend we'll have a 1.05 compatible version completed. That version should also contain the GRP editor.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-07T18:21:00Z |title=SDTutor Release Comming Soon... I Hope :) |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Things are continuing smoothly with SDTutor... I am now setting the release date to next Sunday.  I just need to complete the Unit tutorial, and a few other miscellaneous things, and get it fully tested.  After that it will be ready for a full release.

SCUD, unfortunately, is still on hold, and it's looking doubtful as to whether it will support group dueling at all.  Without specs on unit speeds, SCUD has to assume that all units are within the attack ranges of all other units, which kind of ruins the whole point of having groups.  1on1 dueling wil still work.  I think I'll have the user just tell SCUD how many shots Unit A gets off before Unit B starts attacking, or something to that affect.  I'm kind of disappointed though.  I had some BIG plans for the dueler.  Trust me the thing would have rocked :).  I'd still turn it into the program I had originally planned, but not without those specs.  I'm not sure when I'll be finished working with SCUD.

And for those of you who are still complaining and EMailing me about the cancelation of CE, please read [This](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_gen/index.cgi?read=5074) post.  Consider it my reply to anyone who EMails me, because I've given up on answering everyone individually.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-06T16:30:00Z |title=AI Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I suppose I should clairfy further: originally we were going to include an AI editor as part of the Command Editor. When we decided the Command Editor was simply too much to do, we decided we should still try and do an AI editor, mostly because it's much easier to do. I sent Merlin all of my notes last night for AI's, and today he's going to try and decipher some more opcodes. We hope to get at least an AI compiler done... we're not sure about an interpreter for existing AI's. We'll probably do the compiler first, and then worry about the existing ones.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-06T16:25:00Z |title=Command Editor Canceled |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

For those of you who may have missed the news buried in my post last night, we have decided to cancel the command editor, for several reasons. First off, we are very concerned about cheating/hacking as we have I hope made clear before. We would have had to make sure that the Command Editor patches were disabled in Multiplayer somehow so that cheating wouldn't take place. Doing this would be difficult at best, and I'm sure that the more resourceful hackers could find ways around what we had planned.

We were also having a hard time getting work done on it because simply put we don't know enough about the EXE. With WarCraft II, Merlin had a ton of help making WarHack thanks to the efforts of Mordraug. he spent about 14 hours a day for a year just looking at the EXE. Neither Merlin or I have that kind of time that really is required to do justice to an EXE editor.

It also would have been very difficult to use. At best it would have been like programming in C, and since most of our users can't do that, then it would have been useless for a lot of people.

So basically we're sorry to announce that we won't even be doing a Command Editor at all. It's a vast amount of time that we would have to invest to get it working, and it's time that neither of us have. Sorry to disappoint all you TC authors out there who were counting on the Command Editor for your TC's.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-06T00:49:00Z |title=Phew. Tha coast is clear for now. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Wow. Merlin and I now have a huge sigh of relief. We will be getting the official "cease and desist" email, but since it's a bulk email to all sites that offer software pertaininig to StarCraft, we have to be covered. As long as we don't ever offer cheats/hacks for StarCraft, then we can safely ignore the email. Whelp, that makes me happy. Unfortunately that probably means that the Command Editor and its like will never see the light of public release. It's simply too close to a cheating utility to risk anything.

In other news, KramerBoy, CodeLord, and I are all working on Baldur's Gate data files now, in hopes of getting an editor for them released soon. No it won't be a cheat program, just one that lets you create new spells/items/creatures to extend the game.

Thanks should go to the many people who emailed me with their support for our plight, as well as the people who posted on the forums in our defense. Merlin and I are very impressed that you guys would all stick up for us if the worst had come true.

Finally I must extend apologies to Blizzard for assuming the worst, although it's my nature to do so, and I'm glad to find that once again you've proved to me that you're just as good a gaming company as I always thought :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-05T04:38:00Z |title=Sigh |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I don't know what to think anymore.

I was hoping that this wouldn't happen, but today I got that "official" email I'd been dreading since Tuesday. Things do not bode well for the future of StarDraft. Nothing specific has been stated yet, but the mere fact that I got an email kind of puts me on edge. If the sender of the email is reading this, my apologies for talking about the email, I didn't realize it was supposed to be secret.

All I know is that I'll be calling up Blizzard sometime tomorrow to hopefully settle this issue. I have had many people email me and offer their support (which we are greatful for), and a few people offer to host StarDraft on their sites. While it may seem a generous gesture, it is in fact probably not a good idea, especially if things go as badly as I fear. So I must request that if we're asked to remove StarDraft from our site, that no one attempt to put the file on their page and make it available to download. Doing so will only get you (and possibly us) into trouble..

Again, this is all assuming the worst, but I'd rather assume the worst and hope for the best than the other way around :P
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-04T01:24:00Z |title=New VIV Wizard for NFS3 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SuperFly has really done a great job with his great looking and easy to use [VIV Wizard](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/vivwiz06.exe). Now in its 6th version, it lets you edit pretty much any aspect of a car that you could want to, with the exception of the architecture of the car itself, which by the way, you can do with the CarCad utility also available at Camelot Systems (on the right).

[VIV Wizard](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/ftp://ftp.warzone.com/pub2/CamelotSystems/vivwiz06.exe) - (564kb) - Designed by SuperFly
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-04T00:33:00Z |title=Good and Very Bad News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'll give you the good news first:

Warzone finally got their FTP server back up, I'm told it can accomodate 1000 anonymous users at any one time. This evening I will be changing all of the file locations to point over to the FTP server. That should help to speed up our website a great deal since the www server won't have to handle file transfers and the webpages.

And now the bad news:

[Blizzard Entertainment](https://web.archive.org/web/20000205010418/http://www.blizzard.com/) has started a very agressive anti-cheating campaign. This in itself I considered to be great news, at least until I heard that they may be turning their sights on us. I haven't gotten an email from them yet asking me to remove StarDraft yet, but if it does come, I'll put up a fight only to make sure they understand everything about it, and then of course back down once they threaten to sue my ass. Hey I have ambitions to work for the company when I'm done with College... or at least I used to... I dunno now.

I was going to discuss what this would mean for Camelot Systems if they do decide to "ban" StarDraft, but any dicussion of what they'd do is pointless until they actually do something.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-03-01T04:58:00Z |title=Updates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Tim the Enchanter's got most of the work done on one of the Castle GFX for the FunCraft Total Conversion. We should have a preview up on the page within a few days.

Turns out the FTP server was really being bogged down by one of our hosted sites - [Hacked Speed 3](hs3/) - they're getting like 100,000 visits per day. Thats 100x what we get, and almost everyone who goes there is downloading custom cars. (General Kane - see people do want to see NFS3 customs. Have you ever tried playing with new cars in NFS3? :)

On the AI script front, I made great progress this weekend. Chaplan was able to beat the AI script after a few attack waves, so I still have some tweaking to do to make it a little more tough. And on second thought, I'm going to be integrating the Campaign/Custom versions into one version that can handle both cases. Only thing it won't do is to expand to another base. But it's a Rush script - it's designed to kill the player quickly... and if that fails, let's just say I have some seriously heavy duty attack waves scheduled after the 3rd or 4th wave. (ex. Wave 6 or 7 has an attack force of 10 Reavers, 60 Dragoons, and 20 Dark Templars).

I did some more Baldur's Gate editing today. I'm almost done with the Character format. I will be doing a Character editor that will allow you to save as a creature or a playable character.

For those of you wondering about our vitality (General Kane especially) have no fear. As soon as we get the FTP server back up, we'll be releasing the PC Gamer version of StarDraft on the website - with a GRP editor and the more advanced Self-Executing CWAD generator.

I had planned on doing the BG editor as a multiple file format editor like Arsenal (i.e. it would edit Items, Spells, Characters), but I think I'll end up doing one first, releasing it, then adding the others just so that I don't end up waiting forever to release it.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=February 1999}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-28T05:09:00Z |title=Protoss Rush AI Script Test |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok I decided to cave in and upload a beta test of the Protoss Rush AI Script. I'm not exactly sure of the composition of the Attack Waves in this version - it's an older one, and the last one that Chaplan played. I figured I'd let you guys download it, and tell me where I need to improve the attacks waves, etc. if they don't kill you right away. They killed me, but that's no challenge. My only requirement was that they did kill me... if they didn't then I knew it didn't work.

[Protoss Rush AI Script](software/zrush.zip) - (512kb) - Designed by King Arthur and Chaplan
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-26T22:29:00Z |title=Command Editor: A Request |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm only going to say this once: please stop emailing me about the Command Editor.

1. Merlin hasn't even finished it yet, so answering any questions about its availability is pointless.
2. Don't ask me to put you on a list to get it emailed to you. For most of you asking this, I realize that you probably have a legitimate interest in the Command Editor, but to be honest there's no way for me to tell the difference between you and Alyssa P. Hacker.
3. Please don't ask me how cheating would be possible. If we do finish the Command Editor, the answers to that question will only make things worse. Also, it's nearly impossible to predict all the methods of cheating that are available through a utility that isn't even completed yet.
4. If you have questions/concerns/comments, please use the forum. My email address is not a discussion forum, so don't use it as one. We have 3 free forums for you to post any comments you want on there with no limits except profanity, so please, share your comments with the forums. That way hopefully the massive number of redundant emails I've been getting that all ask the same question can be answered in one (or three in this case) location.
5. The reason that we're concerned about cheating is that we don't want StarCraft to be destroyed like Diablo's multiplayer aspect was when people cheated there.
6. Don't bribe me. First off, even if we did want the money, we couldn't sell our programs cause then Blizzard would sue our asses into the ground. And I'm sure that *most* of you don't want that to happen.

Any more emails that I get about the Command Editor will probably be ignored/deleted, so if you don't get a response to your message, don't be too suprised.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-26T03:37:00Z |title=FunCraft Site Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks to the hard work of Tim the Enchanter, FunCraft: Deride and Plunder now has real [website](FuncraftSite/news.html). Although there's not much there right now, you may want to take a look at the units page which has graphical previews of about 5 or 6 of the new units that will be appearing in FunCraft, and most of them are brand new. Tim and I will be working on the site layout and content over the next few days to get everything up to speed with the TC progress/storyline, etc.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-26T02:05:00Z |title=About the Command Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I got a *lot* of email today about the Command Editor. A lot of people have problems seeing how the Command Editor could be used for cheating, or would rather have it released even if it does allow cheating just to make sure that Total Conversions have access to using it.

I think I owe you an explanation. The Command Editor is capable of a lot more than its name suggests (which isn't going to be its final name anyways). While creating cheats is of course not its main goal, it could probably be used to do that by people if they wanted to. One solution is of course to limit the release of the Command Editor to only people who email me for it, or a selected group of insiders, etc. That's obviously not going to be a very popular move on our part, and it probably won't be very effective either, cause there's no way of us making sure that it doesn't leak out to the public. I'm sure this is the kind of thing that sites like (*name removed to protect the guilty*) would just love to get their hands on and then put it up on their site where we can do nothing to take it down.

It's quite possible that the Command Editor will never even get finished. Regardless of what happens, please keep one thing in mind: there are reasons for pretty much everything we do, including trying to prevent cheating with the Command Editor. Most of them have to do with preserving the integrity of a game like StarCraft for as long as possible. We certainly don't want to be responsible for the total collapse of Battle.net due to large amounts of cheating. So please bear with us and at least respect our decisions, even if you don't like them. After all, I'll say it once again just for those of you who might have eye problems, our software is free. We'll do what we want with it and there's not a whole heck of a lot you can do to change our minds. Not until you're paying our bills at least.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-25T04:33:00Z |title=Command Editor Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Whew one big day of updates. I finally got sick of the malasie that had overtaken the page (ok mostly just me) and decided to do something for a change just to make things exciting. After discussing this at length with Merlin over the last couple of days, I have determined the reason that he hasn't released the Command Editor yet. Essentially he's very concerned about cheating, specifically how easy it can be to cheat in Multiplayer with the type of utility that we intend to provide. I hope that we can find a way to prvent multiplayer usage of edited command structures, etc. so that we can release it as a tool geared towards advanced single player Total Conversions. If we can't figure out how to control cheating, we may halt development, or keep it a product intended for internal Camelot Systems (and affiliate) use only.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-25T04:29:00Z |title=FunCraft update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Tim the Enchanter should be working on the new webpage for the Total Conversion, which will outline the graphics already covered and reveal some details about the plot that until this point has remained a mystery. Yes FunCraft actually is going to have a plot this time around. I think I'm going to base it loosely on the movie, possibly have it take place after the original events which transpired took place. If I get a chance, I should be writing up the preliminary storyline this weekend so that Tim has something to go on. Again, many thanks go to Tim for his continued involvement with Camelot Systems and helping us out in a great deal of places while Thrak tries to decide what the future has in store for him.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-25T04:26:00Z |title=StarDraft and PC Gamer |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well the good news is that yesterday Merlin successfully got everything completed on time that he wanted to finish before sending StarDraft off to PC Gamer for inclusion in the May issue CD-ROM. The coolest new features are the additional abilities added to the Self-Executing CWAD Generator (now allows for custom TC titles, logos, and descriptions) and the addition of a GRP (Graphics) editor. As soon as I get a chance to move it over to the FTP server with the rest of the files (probably tomorrow night) then I'll update the software pages with the new URL's of everything.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-25T04:22:00Z |title=Good news: AI progress |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well finally I have had some more time to work on the AI scripts, and tonight I had significant progress. I actually got to the point where the computer was beating me all the time with the Rush script. The script is by no means finished yet, I just had it test out about the first 5 attack waves. Unlike the current AI scripts, they should constantly be building up to attack or attacking. Essentially no units will be left at the base unless it proves to be a problem. The assumption will be that the rush should eliminate the player or cripple him enough that an undefended base won't matter too much. I may throw in a few dark templars and Photon cannons to guard the base, but essentially that'll be it. They also won't expand. They'll stay at the same mineral deposit location until killed or it runs out, and at that point I think the AI will just build until it runs out of money and then stop.

I passed a version on again to Chaplan, and he managed to get it working, unlike last time. He hasn't given me any specific feedback about the attack wave composition, timing, predictability, etc yet so that's why I haven't released it yet. Once he does, I should be able to get the Campaign version finished, and after that the Custom Map version (only difference is that the custom one assumes it starts with nothing and adjusts the build order accordingly to produce the required buildings, etc)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-21T22:41:00Z |title=File move |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

BetaMax, the admin guy for Warzone, our hosts, emailed me today and told me that we need to move our files over to the FTP server because they're bogging down the server quite a bit. So if in the next few days you find that the links on the software page aren't working, please be aware that we may be moving the files during that period. Hopefully if everything goes as planned, you won't even notice anything at all... but I'm just letting you know this in hopes that I don't get 50 emails telling me the files are gone :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-21T09:51:00Z |title=Come ON! |author=Tim the Enchanter |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Alright, i'm not *technically* a poster here...i'm sure several people recognize my name, i'm the one in charge of the Funcraft project, and i've been doing some graphics around here lately. Everyone, make some noise! Things are going on, dont get discouraged! I'm working on funcraft, and hopefully, i'll be able to make a page for it this week, so you all can see what we're doing. Its looking great! I'll grant you, Baldur's Gate is excellent (I bought it this week) but SC is not DEAD. On a semi-news issue, I've just come into possession of a fantastic new modeller to use with TS4, so I'm expecting to be able to crank out some new Funcraft graphics (i'm thinking rabbit...=D) soon so dont get discouraged!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-21T06:15:00Z |title=Hmmm.. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well apparently everyone around here is dead. Merlin's off at some Robotics convention, and so hasn't been working on anything for a while. Who knows about the status of the Command Editor at this point. All I know is that we've got a deadline of Feb 23rd to get PC Gamer our latest version of StarDraft so it can go on the *MAY* edition of their CD Rom - which I'm told will be available in April. Anyways, I'm still waiting for Chap's opinions on the AI. He hasn't been able to get it working properly. I sent him a sample map and the map has gas on it, but when he plays it there's no gas anywhere, so the AI just breaks down and cries.

UGH. So apparently nobody around here is doing anything anymore. Merlin wanted me to fix up the Self-Executing CWAD generation interface, so that it would support the use of Custom Logos, etc, but I don't really see the use until we have something more useful in additon to that.

If you want my honest opinion, StarCraft's pretty much dead. At least for me. Well it's been dead for me for a while, but now I've really lost all motivation. Thrakerzog told me the other day that he's basically lost all motivation to be on the internet anymore.

No I'm not saying I'm quitting or anything, I'm just venting a little steam. It's just that I guess that we had all gotten used to updates quite often to our programs and the page, and well there's not enough changes yet in the program to warrant a new release, my AI's are acting strange, and we're all losing motivation.I just knew that if we stuck with StarCraft too long it'd be bad. We've already missed a lot of other good games that we could have customized that are now dead while we worked on StarDraft.

Oh well. I keep wishing that we will do something to Baldur's Gate, but the sad truth appears to be that by the time Merlin's ready to start work on Baldur's Gate with me, it'll probably be a dead game that nobody plays. I can't win.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-17T22:58:00Z |title=AI Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I sent a Campaign version of the Zealot Rush AI script to Chaplan to test out today to see if he liked the Build Order I programmed in. I would guess that he'll be done testing it out in a day or two. I haven't finished it by any means - this is just an inital test. I decided to ditch the one I was working on before and try it from a new approach since I couldn't get the computer to build any troops. This version has worked much better for me so far. I plan to finish up the Campaign version (which assumes that you have started the computer player out with a Gateway, Forge, and Citadel of Adun) and then start work on the Custom version, which will build everything it requires. The Campaign version will probably be more viscious in that it attacks faster, so you may want to plan accordingly in your maps. So yes, I will be releasing **both** a Campaign and a Custom AI version.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-17T22:54:00Z |title=New CamSys Logo |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just wanted to credit and thank Tim the Enchanter for our new logo which you can see near the top of the main page. Best one we've had yet. In fact it's the first that we have had in quite a while. Tim seemed to suggest that it may become animated at some point... who knows? All I know is I really was impressed by it :)

We were going to go for a silly theme ala Monty Python/FunCraft, but we thought better of it and went for *slightly* silly instead :) Much more subtle that way.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-12T04:45:00Z |title=Protoss Rush AI update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I apologize for leading everyone to the conlcusion that the Protoss Rush AI was ready the other day to upload and use. Things simply weren't working as expected and as they had in previous AI tests. I had designed the AI from scratch, but for some reason it wasn't working properly at all. I'm going to take a look at another approach to the script and see if there is some other way of making it from scratch. If that's not possible, I'll just base it on an existing AI and modify it where necessary to act differently.

A note about the AI: when it is available, it will require that you run StarDraft to load StarEdit/StarCraft when you're editing/playing a map with the new AI in it. Otherwise StarEdit/StarCraft will crash since they won't be able to find the AI script that I will be *adding*. Yes you read that right I said adding. That way you don't have to worry about incompatibility with existing AI's or having any of them being over-written by my AI. I also should make clear that for at least this AI, Brood war is not required, but the AI file I'll be modifying contains some StarCraft 1.04-only triggers. That means that you'll probably need to make sure that you have the latest version of StarCraft (1.04) or just Brood War (which is already 1.04).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-12T04:39:00Z |title=Baldur's Gate Editing |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After checking out a few websites, I determined that a Character editor was already available. Upon further thought I decided that it'd be a pretty silly thing to program because it's essentially a cheat program, not a custom program. So after we get done with StarCraft, we'll be looking into the possibilites of making a Baldur's Gate version of StarDraft - basically to see if we can get it to patch files, etc.

We may also look into the format of the archives and see if we can work up an extractor/viewer type thing so that the "levels" can be edited, too.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-12T04:35:00Z |title=StarDraft and Command/AI Editor News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin has more or less completed the Command/AI compiler at this point - don't bother emailing me and asking me for a copy of it - it's still being tested thorougly. He hasn't even added patching features to StarDraft yet, so even if you did have it, it would be pretty much useless. Originally the Command/AI language was going to be Macro-based, but now it'll probably resemble C/C++ more, at least according to what I last heard from him.

And you did read that correctly - it will be a Command ***and*** an AI compiler, assuming that I can incude enough info in the compiler to make it worthwhile. Our plan is to stick it in there anyways...

Merlin's also finished work on a GRP editor that will act as a CWAD Manager plugin, and will also allow us to possibly display unit graphics in Arsenal II to make some aspects of editing easier to cope with :)

Again, none of this stuff has a release date yet, so don't email me asking for the files. Some of it is just in the planning stages, while the rest of it is still being tested.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-10T00:42:00Z |title=Protoss Rush AI |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I should be finished implimenting the Protoss Rush AI script very soon - Chaplan just sent me the first copy of the Build Order, now all I have to do is translate it into a valid script format and whammo I've got a new AI script. Course we have to test it some, but I'll put it online asap. Finally a useful AI :) Thanks to Chap of course :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-07T16:15:00Z |title=Where have I been? The land of Baldur's Gate. :) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You've probably been wondering where is King Arthur this weekend? :) I've been playing a lot of Baldur's Gate :) Boy is this game addictive. It reminds me a lot of Diablo's gameplay, just with better graphics and a lot more places to go. True none of the levels are randomly generating, but it's still a cool game. I'm working on a Character Editor right now for Baldur's Gate, and I'll let you know as soon as I've finished it. Right now I'm just in the research process, so don't bother emailing me asking for a copy.

Does this mean we're deserting Blizzard games? Absolutely not. They're still my favorite game publisher, it's just that until Diablo II comes out and Merlin finishes the Command Editor for StarCraft, there's really not much Blizzard related stuff we can do. Oh and of course Chaplan's Zealot Rush AI should be done soon (I hope).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-04T21:28:00Z |title=I'm Leaving... |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hey people. Remember that post I made a while back? The one where I said I'd be on less than usual, because I had a lot going on in my life? Well, things haven't eased up much recently. In fact, it's very much packed in. For that reason, I'll be leaving Camelot Systems, at least for a while, to persue other interests. I may come back, if the world is one day taken off my shoulders long enough to get some real work done. See you guys later.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-03T23:08:00Z |title=Updates... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just as an update for you guys on what I've been doing lately...

It's now official; I'm going to be writing SC Unit Dueler (SCUD) that will be an Arsenal II component.  I got the Borland Compiler and everything ( which I must say, KILLS Visual C++), now all I need is a day or two to learn the new environment, and I'll begin working on the Dueler.  I'm not sure if SCUD will end up being a higher priority than SDTutor ( yes I've changed the name ), but please feel free to drop me an [email](mailto:kramrboy@execpc.com) suggesting which programs you'd like to see finished first.  Ultimately though, the decision will depend on whatever I feel like doing =).

Speaking of SDTutor, I'm probably going to end up re-writing it in Borland also.  Although this will probably end up slowing things down a little, it will prove to be VERY beneficial in the long run, because Borland is soooo much easier to use for the stuff I plan on doing.  So I apologize for the delay, but as KA has said... our programs are free, so deal with it =).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-03T02:26:00Z |title=Baldur's Gate Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Due to the advice of other ppl who have been gyped by Electronics Boutique (because of the large discrepency between their advertised price on the website and the one in the store) I went over and had to haggle with them for like 10 minutes before the two guys there caved. Sheesh I was older than they were and knew more than they did. I should have used my Psycho-telekinetic powers to grab a copy, slam it at 40 miles an hour into the side of their heads and then walk off with the cash register. {{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-03T02:23:00Z |title=Site news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-01T04:04:00Z |title=Baldur's Gate Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I was going to go over and buy the game today - I checked EBWorld to see how much I needed to bring with me, and I had just enough - they listed it for 39.99 and I had like 45 bucks which should have covered it. Well turns out when I got over to my Electronics Boutique they were selling it for 49.99. I was like WTF? So I asked the clerk about the price discrepency and he pretty much didn't have anything to say except "too bad" That really sucked cause besides them there is no other place I can get it (EB has had it for like a month, and Media Play hasn't even gotten a single copy of it yet, even though they have like 4 quadrillion copies of Big Game Hunter II). So I was going to buy a couple of DVD's but I decided, since the response to us editing Baldur's Gate has been very good (I have gotten a couple of emails a day over the last week or so encouraging us), that I had better save my money until later this week (Thursday) when I get paid.

Even if I do get it on Thursday it may be a few weeks before I can get around to editing it. I have to find the time to play thru the game a little bit first, then I have to see if it's ediitable. All this is *after* we get the StarCraft Command Editor done.... so please be patient with us :) We are pretty much guaranteed to try working on it... just we have to wait until the SC stuff is pretty much wrapped up.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-02-01T03:48:00Z |title=Server Problems |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

They're just going to upgrade the server tomorrow morning (so the site may be down for a few hours) and then hopefully if everything goes well the site speed should be back to normal.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-31T17:38:00Z |title=AI Script Progress |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just finished making a new AI script - I may or may not release it - it's a modification of the Hardcore Templar AI script and is a Hardcore Dragoon script. Basically it produces around 60 dragoons, and attacks with parties of 30 every couple thousand milliseconds or something. I forget the exact delay. In any case over this weekend I've managed to learn quite a bit for the AI scripts, and I have deciphered about 10-15 completely. I *may* work with Chaplan of [StarShack](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://www.starshack.com/) to get some new AI Scripts written based on rushes. Or I may do some for Ground Attacks and Air Attack AI's. I haven't decided yet.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-31T17:35:00Z |title=Command Editor progress |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin's made pretty good progress with the Command Editor so far. He thinks that he'll have an internal-alpha test ready by Monday. If it does work ok then we'll be spending a few days writing up tutotirals, etc so that when it's released, you aren't all stumped by it. Which brings up an important issue: this Command Editor utility isn't intended for everyone to be using. I _may_ require that people email me to get access to it because simply put it is a very advanced utility that not everyone should try to use. In fact I'd say if you're not familiar with StarDraft/ Arsenal II already, or haven't used the StarEdit utility, don't even bother downloading the Command Editor when it's done. Sure it'll be able to do pretty much anything you want, but it is going to be very difficult for the average user to figure out how to use. That's why we may restrict its release.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-28T03:49:00Z |title=Arsenal II News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just in case anyone cares, I discovered the meaning of the "Group" variable on the "AI/Orders" page of the Units editor in Arsenal II - it corresponds to the number of a technology required in order for the AI/Order to be carried out. A value of 44 signifies that no technology is required. Any other value corresponds to the technologies in the order they are listed in the technology editor. A new release of Arsenal II will add a listbox for this item so that you don't have to guess on the number required here.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-27T01:33:00Z |title=Arsenal II Tutorial Updated |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You can take a look at the new version of the tutorial [at his page](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://members.xoom.com/JQpanG/arsenalt/arsenalt.html).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-24T23:21:00Z |title=Baldur's Gate |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A lot of people have told me that Baldur's Gate is an excellent game, and when I went to Electronics Boutique this weekend I had to agree - the game certainly looks very impressive. It's supposed to be a lot like, if not better than, Diablo. In any case, when I do buy the game I'm definately going to try and determine if it's worth creating custom-enabling utilities for (like StarDraft/Arsenal II). If the demand for such utilities is pretty good, then you can expect Baldur's Gate to be one of our next projects.

Feel free to [email me](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com) and share your opinions on Baldur's Gate, our intentions, etc.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-24T23:17:00Z |title=Commands and a possible editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

All day yesterday and today I've been studying the command structure for StarCraft - stuff embedded in the StarCraft exe file. I've made lots of notes, and Merlin thinks that we have enough to start work on a command editor which will create files that you would place in a CWAD and then patch the EXE while it's running (so you don't make permanent changes to it). This should allow us to add buttons and even new spells to the game. I was able to successfully make the Marine for instance into a Transport this morning - previous patches with CWAD's had allowed the marine to pick up but never release any units. By changing the commands anr other stats I was able to make the marine act like the Dropship, etc. Basically this means that soon you will be able to edit pretty much everything outside of AI scripts and Image scripts (which I'm also working on in hopes of a future editor for them).

[Marine Transport Picture](images/marinetrans.jpg) - (96kb)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-22T01:45:00Z |title=NEW StarDraft/Arsenal II/StatPrinter UPLOADED! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After extensive in-house beta testing, we have decided to release what should be the last major version of StarDraft/Arsenal II/StatPrinter that you will likely see. That doesn't include updates for bug fixes and StarCraft version changes. Basically what it means is this is the last version of StarDraft/Arsenal II/StatPrinter that we will probably completely overhaul and add lots of new features.

New to this version:

* First and foremost: Arsenal II has been tested thoroughly and should now be bug-free. All previously caught bugs have been fixed, so you should be able to run Arsenal II without having to worry about it corrupting your data files/changes.
* Should Arsenal II have any remaining bugs, you can now decide whether or not you want to save the changes that you made during your Arsenal II session when you shut down Arsenal II. Yipee! :) Sorry it took so long on this one :)
* One word: StatPrinter. The new version in my opinion (and several other people including Shlonglor) just plain rocks. There are so many cool new features and fixes that you'd swear this was re-started from scratch (it practically was).
* StatPrinter now supports grouping by race, unit, building, etc. It also supports hiding Sub-units (so that the Goliaths and Tanks have proper attack stats) as well as custom names for your units (which you can change by editing the "arsenal\unit_list.txt" file in your CWAD
* Arsenal II has several new listboxes, a modified and less crowded layout, and a brand new help system which explains what everything that we know about does in Arsenal II.
* TBLEdit will now save changes made to your TBL files. The previous version had a saving problem which has now been fixed.
* The InstallShield version has been fixed so that the StarDraft icon no longer points to ploader.exe but instead to Stardraft.exe

[StarDraft SE (InstallShield Setup)](software/sdsesetup.exe) - (2.7mb)

[StarDraft SE (Zip file only)](software/sdse.zip) - (1.7mb)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-19T05:18:00Z |title=Arsenal II / StatPrinter Progress Report |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well folks things are going really well for the new Arsenal II / StatPrinter. I've got lots of cool new features in StatPrinter. You can now have StatPrinter choose units, buildings, and heroes by race, and you can sort the output so that groups of units that share the same type are printed together. You can take a look at what the finished output looks like [here](software/testpage.zip). Also, I finally got the Notes section of StatPrinter working.

So basically you can generate your own tables now and have them end up looking almost exactly like the ones on Shlonglor's StarCraft Compendium. One final note is that since StatPrinter is now integrated with Arsenal II, it will support custom unit names. This should be great news for customs/TC Authors because you can now have a quick and accurate way of generating stat tables for your units with the names you gave them :)

The new hints have all been added into Arsenal II - when you click on something, hint text appears in a box below to describe what the variable changes.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-17T22:15:00Z |title=Camelot Systems is expanding! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Starting today we're going to be adding programs and content to the Camelot Systems page for non-Blizzard games. The reason we're doing this is that we feel we need to expand and differentiate ourselves to cover new games.

The first game that we're going to be concentrating on is Need for Speed III. Some of you may have noticed that I've already added one program to our Software Live Wire - VIV Wizard - a file editor for Need for Speed III, which was programmed by our new staff programmer, SuperFly.

In addition, sometime this week we will begin hosting Hacked Speed 3, a website dedicated to providing custom cars, tracks, and editors for Need for Speed III. I'll post when the URL is active.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-17T22:07:00Z |title=New version of Arsenal II on the way |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm working on a new version of Arsenal II which fixes all the bugs that have been found so far, adds several additonal lists (instead of the random numbers you would type in before), makes the layout a bit easier to work with, and adds a considerable amount of hints to the editor. I will hopefully be adding StatPrinter to Arsenal II later tonight, and if all goes well I will release it when that's done (which may not necessarily be tonight). I may just ship it out to the Beta testers.

Also, Merlin's out of town for a couple of days, so I haven't been able to get the bug in TBLEdit fixed (it wasn't saving the changes to the files in the last version). I may or may not wait for that bug to be fixed before I release the new Arsenal II. Depends on if I can get ahold of Merlin to find out where the problem can be fixed.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-17T16:25:00Z |title=A little news about me |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, depending on your perspective, this could be good or bad news.

First of all, I can't do customs for a while. Whenever I load them, the game crashes. I'm going to try a few things to see if I can fix this problem, but until I get that done, it's not gonna work.

Now the worse news (for me). I love working here. It's great. I like the people, the programs, and, of course, the fame (even as little of it as I get). Sadly, though, there are other things in my life that I need some time to handle. I'm not quitting, and I'm not retiring. I'm just going to spend slightly less time working here than I usually do. I already spend very little time here, so don't be suprised if I go a little while without posting or somehow making my presence known. After all, I do have a life beyond cyber space.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-16T16:28:00Z |title=New Software |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I've finally gotten my first piece of software uploaded.  Sorry everyone... it isn't SCTutor.  It's WMakeDef.  And before I go any further, I'm going to warn you that this one will probably be best used by TC authors.  It generates default SC .dat files from StarCraft or Brood War, and allows you to have it 'learn' your own custom .dat's.  So unless you're a hard core SC hacker, or a TC maker, this piece of software probably isn't going to do you a whole lot of good.

[Download WMakeDef](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://www.camsys.org/software/WMakeDef.zip) - 70kb

{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-16T05:34:00Z |title=StarDraft/Arsenal II update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We realize that there are several bugs remaining in the latest release of StarDraft and (mostly) Arsenal II. We hope to have those fixed within the next few weeks. We intend that fixed version to be the final major version of StarDraft that we will make, aside from various updates to the program when Blizzard changes version numbers. Other than that, we are kinda growing tired of making updates to an already very advanced program. Every version we fix bugs AND introduce new features. Well the next version will just be bug fixes and adding a lot of help, but that's about it.

Looking toward the future, we may work on other editors for StarCraft depending on our motivation and the difficulty of the editing. We're also planning to continue to expand our coverage to other games as we purchase them and begin editing them, with our goal always remaining one of customizeability.

Also, although I will be discontinuing my tech support, other people, should they want to offer their services, may email me and request to join the Camelot Systems team as tech support personnel. Basically the job would require a thorough understanding of the current software, and an ability to adequately explore new releases. Good writing/communication skills are also probably a good thing. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-15T03:33:00Z |title=Sheesh... calm down people. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hehe I make a post saying I'm thinking of leaving and everyone emails me assuming the worst - that I'm leaving and that Camelot Systems will go to shit when I leave... neither of which is true (for now). Last night Merlin, Thrakerzog and I worked out our new plan for this year... and basically it amounted to no tech support. At least on our parts. I won't make you guys go cold turkey... I'll answer questions on anything related to our programs up until next Monday, but after that I'm calling it quits in the Tech Support department. If someone wants to get help, you'll have to use our forums, that's what they are there for. That way you can find a resource where there are lots of answers in the open, and lots of knowledgeable people to answer them. When questions do get answered, lots of people can see those answers, so if they have the same question, that kills multiple birds with one stone.

Merlin, Thrak, and I are still gonna be working here - with the rest of the gang and some newcomers (hopefully) but for right now the "Big 3" are toning it down a notch. Basically we realized that we spent enough time working on Camsys stuff to equate us all working at full time jobs *in addition* to our normal school responsibilities. That's a bit much for anyone to handle.

Spending less time on tech support should help me to make sure our programs are better made, and to help keep us all less stressed out and not so prone to burn out (like I almost did).

So people, I appreciate your concern very much... I'm really touched (no not like that), and I'm glad to know that you all care about me. :) Which is a big change from about a year and a half ago :P
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-13T01:58:00Z |title=Here's the deal... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok by now you're probably wondering... WTF? where is KA? He's been back from Florida for 3 days now and he hasn't updated the site at all. What is going on here? Well truth be told I'm sick of doing this job. I may or may not continue to work at Camelot Systems... I really find it all very boring now... I'm no longer having any fun doing anything, website stuff especially.

I don't know what's going to happen, to be honest. Merlin's probably going to kill me, considering that this post is the first he's heard of my... reluctance to work. Right now I'm having a good time working on some Need for Speed III stuff - who knows, maybe I'll just work on software for that for Camelot Systems... but right now I'm kinda sick of SC and pretty much the internet in general. Who knows what the future has in store for me and Camelot Systems. This isn't my swan song... so don't worry you'll hear from me at least one more time :) Please don't flame me about this... on this issue the only person who's got an opinion I care about hearing is me.
{{end-newsitem}}
