{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-07T14:04:00Z |title=Custom Graphics Tutorial |author=Tender Fury |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[SC.Org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://www.starcraft.org/) has a new tutorial up on how to make [Custom Graphics for SC](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://www.starcraft.org/customs/tutorial1.shtml). Should come in handy for all you  TC people out there. It's written by [Acolyte57](mailto:Acolyte57@hotmail.com) who, I understand is a regular in the forums here and co-creator of the [Betrayal at Geedon V: Starcraft to StarWars Total Conversion](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://www.camsys.org/StarWars/). It's a very well written tutorial that is geared towards the newbie and has links to all the software you'll need. Give it a read [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://www.starcraft.org/customs/tutorial1.shtml).

Oh and King Arthur said I should introduce myself or something, so here goes. I run a Starcraft site and I'm friends with King Arthur and Merlin. Arthur asked if I'd help out with posting while he was on vacation and I foolishly said yes. I didn't know he had a whole custom prog to do this. eh, programers :p hehehe Anyway I'll try not to delete the news section and if I do,  well ... I'll just blame it on Merlin. *wink*
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-06T22:53:00Z |title=AI Fix |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've had some reports that people couldn't get KA's new hardcore templar AI to work, and I had the same problem, so I wrote a fix for it. The problem didn't have anything to do with the AI, so you don't have to worry about downloading a gigantic Zip file or anything.  I just updated the level a bit. If the AI worked for you before, then there's no need to download this. I uploaded it onto my server, since I didn't know what the guidelines are here for files :).

DISCLAIMER:  I can't guarentee this will work for everyone, but it worked for me 3 times in a row.  I assume KA's worked for him too though....

[Download Protoss8b.zip](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224149/http://execpc.com/~kramrboy/ai_fix.zip) - 75k

Use 'Operation CWAL' and 'Show me the money' cheats to make it go faster.  And it should run in the Brown town in the bottom left.

And just as an update for everyone...

I said something I think in my first post about WMakeDef, a program the generated default data files for SC, and could learn your own data files, so it could generate those too.  Well, I've gotten by my biggest challenge with writing it, and now there's just a few bugs to work out and a few things to add before it's ready for release.  I expect that to be sometime either today or tomorrow, or when KA gets back.

And in my last post I said something about Florida being 70 degrees... I've recieved quite a few EMails telling me that it is in fact in the forties and fifties there.  I apologize for being inaccurate, and will do my best not to make that mistake again :).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-05T18:02:00Z |title=KA's gone folks... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I had like 3 EMails today ask me when King Arthur's leaving on his vacation.  Well everyone, he left this morning.  So while he's enjoying 70 degree (don't hold me to that guess) Florida I'm freezing my butt of in 20 below Illinois.  And although me and my mail server will probably regret this, you can send any questions you would normally send him to [me](mailto:kramrboy@execpc.com) and I'll either do my best to answer them, or pass them along to him when he gets back.  We wouldn't want him to have to download 120 MBs of EMail when he could be working on programming would we =)?
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-05T02:32:00Z |title=New Staff Member, New Programs... |author=KramerBoy |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hey everyone... I'm your brand new Camelot Systems programmer/news guy whenever I get bored =).  The main program I'm doing is SCTutor.  It'll teach you on how to do hacks like adding weapons to stuff like Defilers, enabling new units, and other neat stuff like that.  And it will teach you how to use all these comfusing programs that KA and Merlin make =).  Actually now that I think about it, it'll need a new name, because I intend to expand it for Diablo Hacking once some of those come out, and my creative juices are sapped, so [EMail](mailto:kramrboy@execpc.com) me if you have a cool suggestion.  And I want to hear what you guys want for Tutorials, so I know what to write up =).  I'm also doing WMakeDef.  This is a program that generates default data files for SC, and (hopefully) will allow you to have it 'learn' new data files for TC makers.  And finally I'm starting work on ASH - Advanced SCuM Hacker.  Don't hold your breath for this one though =).  I'm still working on decoding the CHK format for BW maps, and I have only done the visual design of it, but it should be cool when it's done.  So if you happen to have anything (USEFUL) that you'd like to suggest, [EMail](mailto:kramrboy@execpc.com) me, or whisper me on BNet (Psionic4LiFe).  I figure I'll be making another post in the near future telling you all to stop bugging me but oh well =).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-05T02:00:00Z |title=Brand spanking new AI Script! (Hardcore Templars) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well I decided that before I go on a 5-day vacation, I ought to wet your appetites a little bit for what I will be working on when I get back :)

I'm uploading a new Self-Executing CWAD that contains a new AI Script I have written - Hardcore Templars. Admittedly not a very useful AI but it does prove that new AI's can be written. In the ZIP file I have included a sample SCM with which to use this AI - I am not sure if it will work with other SCM's or not. This SCM (and the AI) was based on Protoss Level 8 of the StarCraft Campaign. However both the script and the SCM require that you have Brood War.

[Hardcore Templars](software/hctemplar.zip) - (570kb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-04T05:50:00Z |title=Brood War MPQ ID File Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Even though I included an updated version of the MPQ ID file with the newest version of StarDraft, I have recieved about 25 requests so far for the new MPQ ID file to be seperate from the StarDraft package so that people could use it with other MPQ Viewers.

[Brood War MPQ ID File](software/bw_data.zip) - (19kb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-04T04:59:00Z |title=New AI Scripts available |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

These aren't the AI Scripts that I am working on - these are AI Scripts that Blizzard created to use in the Brood War campaigns but did not give users access to. They include such AI Scripts as "Make Units Patrol", "AI Nuke Here", "AI Disruption Web Here", "AI Recall Here", etc.

The new AI Scripts are available when you run this Self-Executing CWAD (does not require StarDraft to run):

[New AI Scripts](software/newais.zip) - (508kb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-04T04:52:00Z |title=Self-Executing CWAD Problem fixed. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sorry about the problem with the Self-Executing CWAD's not working - they work fine now without any problems.

[StarDraft SE InstallShield Setup](software/sdsesetup.exe) - (2.6mb)

[StarDraft SE](software/sdse.zip) - (1.6mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-03T22:26:00Z |title=Self-Executing CWAD Problems |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin just went offline and I haven't had a chance to contact him yet, but in the latest version of StarDraft, the Self-Executing CWADs are not working as they had in earlier versions. When I find out what the problem is I will post about it and let you know.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-03T20:47:00Z |title=StarDraft SE/Arsenal II Released! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

StarDraft SE/Arsenal II now finally support StarCraft 1.04/Brood War 1.04. Unfortunately due to significant format changes, Arsenal II is no longer compatible with StarCraft 1.03 or earlier. We have uploaded two versions of StarDraft SE for you to choose from. One has the slick InstallShield interface, the other is simply the program and readme zipped up.

[StarDraft SE (with InstallShield)](software/sdsesetup.exe) - (2.6mb)

[StarDraft SE (without InstallShield)](software/sdse.zip) - (1.6mb)

I reccomend that new StarDraft users download the version with InstallShield, while other users download the regular version.

One final note: Virtual Directory support was removed at the last minute in CWAD Manager, and although the documentation mentions it, it is not available.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-03T04:10:00Z |title=StarDraft/ Arsenal II news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well with any luck, the new version of StarDraft/Arsenal II will be uploaded tonight or tomorrow. We want to express our sincere apologies that it has taken us so long to add support for StarCraft 1.04 and for Brood War - we are trying to work out all the bugs that have been found so far as well as add a lot of new and very cool new features.

Just to wet your appetite, here's what is in store for the new version of StarDraft/Arsenal II:

* Self-Executing CWADs which DO NOT require the use of StarDraft
* StarEdit Enhancer - a utility which will allow you to import any file into a SCM. This should enable you to "patch" most files into a SCM file without the use of StarDraft - which means that people can download the SCM and be able to play it with your changes.
* StarDraft in *one* file. Yep, no more runtime libraries or multiple plugin files floating about your hard drive. StarDraft is now self-contained in one simple file.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1999-01-02T02:32:00Z |title=Arsenal II bugs... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Turns out there are a few bugs in Arsenal II that we never noticed before but have just caught. We're not sure what's causing them at all... so it may take longer than expected before the new version is released. We will release it as soon as possible, but we're not going to knowingly release a bug-laden version of our software. Please be patient with us as we try to perfect our software so that you have an easier and more pleasant time using it.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=December 1998}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-31T18:06:00Z |title=StatPrinter news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I will be starting work on a new version of StatPrinter that is Brood War/1.04 comatible as soon as we have distributed StarDraft and Arsenal II's new versions. The new version of StatPrinter may have some other interface changes as well to make it easier for TC authors to produce tables of stats for their new units.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-31T15:53:00Z |title=StarDraft/Arsenal II update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Everything with Arsenal II is ready for distribution - only reason I'm holding it back is because I want to release it with the new version of StarDraft that Merlin should be finished with very shortly. I did get a version from him yesterday - and it does work with 1.04 and Brood War, so that's the good news. He's adding some extras that I think you'll appreciate - such as Self-Executing patches, etc. :) Very cool. Arsenal II also has a few suprises in store for you, too. Stuff that Total Conversion authors especially should like. :)
{{end-newsitem}}

Star Spike, the webmaster in charge of the Starship Troopers TC site has completed the move from his old URL to the new one at Camelot Systems (which you can find [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/StarshipTroopers/)).

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-29T21:42:00Z |title=Camelot Systems to host the Starship Troopers TC |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

This is the third Total Conversion site that Camelot Systems is hosting, and I welcome any other Total Conversion authors with the progress, ambition, and great graphics of these Total Conversions to join their fellow authors at Camelot Systems (following my review of your application of course :)

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-29T16:04:00Z |title=StarDraft/Arsenal II Update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok last night I got a considerable amount of work done on Arsenal II, and I'm very pleased with the results. It is now possible for you all to customize Arsenal II to your heart's content. This is especially useful for Total Conversion authors who want to keep track of their own units by their own names, rather than using the default ones that used to come with Arsenal II and SCUnit. I've given anyone the ability to customize almost all of Arsenal II's lists by generating text files in each CWAD that Arsenal II is used in. By ediitng the text files, you can change the text that appears in Arsenal II. It is very cool :)

Merlin's decided (as have I with Arsenal II) that the new version of StarDraft will probably only support StarCraft 1.04 and Brood War. The reason for this is because it's a lot easier for both programs to support just the newest version of StarCraft. The newest version of StarCraft changed a lot of the formats for the dat files, as well as changing the dll's somewhat. So I believe we will keep StarDraft SE Beta available for any version before 1.04, and release a new version of StarDraft (and Arsenal II) for 1.04 (and after if necessary).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-26T19:09:00Z |title=StarDraft 1.04 support status |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Many people have emailed me asking when we are going to have a version of StarDraft out that supports Brood War and StarCraft v1.04. The answer is very soon. Last night I asked Merlin if he could take a bit of time off from a project he's been working on in order to update the StarDraft engine for the new versions of StarCraft, and he agreed. So his part should be done shortly. My part involves getting Arsenal II to support the new versions and formats for the *.dat files. The Units.dat file I just added support for in Arsenal II, but unfortunately the StarEdit Availablility listbox is getting screwed up again, so I'm looking into that problem right now. Aside from that, I'm just about done with my part. So really, support for 1.04 and Brood War should be coming very soon. I plan to release the Brood War MPQ ID file at the same time that we release the new StarDraft - I'll release it seperately and include it with our own MPQ Viewer plugin for the CWAD Manager.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-26T19:02:00Z |title=StarCraft Music requested... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A number of people have emailed me requesting that since I posted the Brood War music, they want me to post the StarCraft music as well. As soon as I can make sure that we have ample space for it, I'll be uploading the music to the site for whomever wants to download it can.
{{end-newsitem}}

Wolverine and Guy, webmasters and creators of the Star Trek TC for StarCraft have asked me to announce that their website is now open to the public, and you can begin to obtain information, etc regarding the TC from there. [Star Trek TC](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/StarTrek/)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-23T20:30:00Z |title=Brood War Music available. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just extracted, converted, and compressed the four new music files from the Brood War Expansion Set. Three of them are additions to the beginning of the first music file for each of the three races. The fourth is the bonus Zerg song that you obtain by typing "Radio Free Zerg" when playing that race. They are compressed in Microsoft ADPCM format at 22khz-4bit stereo sound. You can get them from the links below:

* [Terran Music](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/terranmusic.zip) - (4.3mb)
* [Protoss Music](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/protossmusic.zip) - (4.7mb)
* [Zerg Music](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/zergmusic.zip) - (4.4mb)
* [Zerg Bonus Track](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/radiozerg.zip) - (3.3mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-23T02:41:00Z |title=StarDraft, 1.04 support, and the MPQ Data file. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm posting about this again because I have recieved like 30 emails today asking about when we will have StarDraft support for version 1.04 of StarCraft and for Brood War. People, I just got done with my exams like 2 hours ago... so have patience, please. Also keep in mind that we, unlike the Hack authors apparently, have RL issues to deal with once and a while. Merlin's sick right now and is working on other projects besides StarDraft - we all get sick of doing the same thing all the time - and I had to study up until today for like... most of the last few days.

The only time I wasn't studying was when I was working on the new MPQ Data Identification file which I will release AS SOON AS I BELIEVE EVERYONE WHO WANTS BROOD WAR HAS IT. That means POSSIBLY as early as a few days after Christmas, when most people will be getting Brood War. That gives the people who want to play it a few days to play the Single Player Campaigns without it being spoiled by releasing the MPQ File... I will IGNORE ANY EMAILS COMPLAINING ABOUT THIS.

I feel rather strongly about keeping the plot of the single-player Brood War Campaign intact. This is because I feel it is FAR superior to the plot of StarCraft and is therefore worth keeping under wraps as long as possible. I also realise that it's quite possible for someone else to make the MPQ ID file and release it before I do, but people please keep in mind the REASONS that I'm not releasing it yet. That's all I am saying about ANY of the above topics until I upload the new StarDraft or the MPQ ID file.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-20T20:30:00Z |title=Brood War MPQ View Data file... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A couple of people have asked me for a Brood War version of the MPQ View Text file that will reveal to you all the new files inside the Broodat.mpq and Install.exe files. I have created such a monster, but I have decided to hold off for a while on releasing it until either I feel most people already have played through the Single Player game, or StarDraft will support Brood War. The reason I am holding it back is because the Single Player campaign is simply spectacular... I know because I've been through all 3 of them, not in actual gameplay but in finding all the filenames... and I think that it's better than the original game's plot... So I strongly urge you to play the single player game now before too many spoilers get out there releasing information, etc. Please don't email me asking for the file, I'm not releasing it until I feel it is needed. I mean, geez people the game just came out! You had to wait like 3-5 months after StarCraft came out before StarDraft started working. Now that BW is out you expect immediate hacking for it? Are you buying these games for fun or to screw around with?
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-19T19:14:00Z |title=Customs Section |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The customs section is up, but the button link isn't. There are three up so far, so check it out [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.camsys.org/customs/customs.htm). By the way, when I said that I would be reviewing them, it wasn't my intention to make you think that yours wouldn't be put in. All I need is something original, and not too badly done or stupid. I really would like to put some more up, but you guys aren't sending them to me. I won't be able to put any up until we have the new StarDraft version out so that I can review them, but if you have one that you'd like to see up here, send it in.

By the way, I don't know who designed the ones that I have up. If you do, let me know so that I can give them credit.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-19T16:25:00Z |title=New SC site |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I don't usually post this kind of thing, but there's a new StarCraft site up. The layout looks good, and the graphics aren't too shabby, either. You can check it out [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://scrafter.virtualave.net/).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-17T21:02:00Z |title=Something new... |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Someone brought some information to my attention recently: We came out with StarDraft, but we're about the only site that doesn't have an archive of customs from the people that use it. A good point, don't you think? Well, I totally agree. That's why we're starting one up. Now, before you all start flooding my inbox with anything you've come up with, I should warn you that I'll be reviewing them before they get put up. I'm just one of those people that doesn't find what's fascinating about swiching a unit's weapon (like the FireBat's flame thrower) with the weapon from that same unit's hero (Like Guy Montang). Also, when you send them to me, be sure to incude the changes you made, so that I know what to look for when I'm reviewing it.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-17T16:23:00Z |title=StarDraft and the 1.04 Patch |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Honestly I think that half of the StarEdit changes were influenced by people using StarDraft... anyways, StarDraft will **not** work with version 1.04 of StarCraft until we get a chance to add support for it to StarDraft SE. So at this point you can download the patch (of course) but don't expect it to work with StarDraft until we can get a new version of SD out. Just figures they'd do this to us. :) Oh well I'm not complaining too much cause it has some nice changes in it (mostly the StarEdit ones).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-16T22:19:00Z |title=General news... |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I had some net troubles yesterday, and there's some of news I missed for ya'll:

[Brood War](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.blizzard.com/broodwar/) has finally gone gold! **Shlonglor** sez that it could be out as early as this weekend in [this post](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.battle.net/forums/starcraft-broodwar/posts/dj/48909.htm). Check out this [pic](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://images.blizzard.com/PIX/BWGoldfull.jpg/) that [Blizz](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.blizzard.com/) put up:)

In another of **Shlonglor**'s posts ([here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.battle.net/forums/starcraft-broodwar/posts/yc/49424.htm)), he tells all about some probable changes in the next [Battle.net](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.battle.net/) patch. Now, don't get your hopes up if you really like them; none of these are set in stone. Nothing is until it's released, and even then, things can change.

That's all I know about, but if you know anything, feel free to [let me know](mailto:silver_wolf3@hotmail.com).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-14T00:11:00Z |title=Brood War Beta is Over!! |author=Lancelot |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, as you've probably heard one way or another, the Brood War Battle.net Beta is over as of midnight (Pacific Time, USA) tonight. It hasn't gone gold, at least not yet. Here's [Blizzard](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.blizzard.com/)'s [official announcement](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224154/http://www.battle.net/forums/announcements/posts/ac/52.htm):

> Sunday, December 13th at 12:00 midnight PST will be the official closing of the Starcraft: Brood War Battle.net beta. We cannot express our thanks enough for the stalwart and heroic efforts of our Beta Squadron of testers. Your feedback on the balance and feel of the game has been invaluable and greatly helped make Brood War the game that it has become.
>
> We have some real surprises in store within the single-player Campaigns that we anticipate are going to knock your socks off. This, together with some incredible cinematic sequences and an even more solid multi-player balance, is destined to dominate your Christmas and take you well into the New Year.
>
> See you on Battle.net!
> The Brood War Development Team

With any luck, it'll be in stores just in time for all us last-minute shoppers:)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-13T20:57:00Z |title=StarDraft SE Beta Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I decided that since I had some free time today, I might as well try and fix a few of the major bugs that were pointed out to us after the StarDraft SE Alpha release...

These bugs were fixed:

* The Marine's stats are no longer screwed up upon program load
* The StarEdit Availability listbox is now compatible with older versions of units.dat
* The Plugins.ini file has had the "G:\..." paths removed from it.
* CWAD Archives will now be registered to open with CWAD Manager when you double click on them.
* The Upgrades Editor now has the "Repeat" value that was removed from Arsenal and SCUnit.
* The Upgrades Editor in Arsenal IInow has the upgraded interface of the rest of Arsenal II.
* The Shortcut folder placed in the Start Menu now works properly so you can select from CWAD Manager, StarDraft, or the Documentation Viewer

That's all the bugs I could remember that had to be fixed. You can download it below or from one of the SLW links:

* [StarDraft SE Beta](software/sdsebeta.exe) - (2.62mb) - Designed by Merlin.

If you have downloaded StarDraft SE before, you can just download Arsenal II (does not include the CWAD Registration fix, the Shortcut fix, or the Plugins.ini fix):

* [Arsenal II](software/arsenal2.zip) - (263kb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-12T02:49:00Z |title=Review: Insurrection |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Warning: This review may or may not contain spoilers, depending on how much you care :)

I went into the theater expecting something quite different from First Contact, the last Star Trek movie featuring the Next Generation cast. Surely the writiers couldn't come up with an enemy worse than the Borg, right? At first glance, they appear to have done just that - by finding aspects of the Federation which seem to be at least as ruthless if not more so than the Borg. Slimier creatures you will not often see...

In any case, the movie began rather strangely... at first I thought that perhaps I had slept through the credits and missed something, because all of a sudden Data comes careening onto the screen running away from some red-suited pursuers. What I can't understand at that time (and still don't) is what exactly was he doing there? I mean what could he have done to aid the Federation's covert operation there that the personnel stationed there couldn't do?

Well in any case if he wasn't there, then there wouldn't have been much of a movie. I was getting worried that the producers and writers would keep straying away from Gene Roddenberry's original vision of the Enterprise and its crew, and their mission: to explore... Picard later remarks on just that subject, that he's not the explorer he once set out to be.

It's a sad moment when this occurs because you almost feel sorry for Patrick Stewart, the actor portraying Picard. Here is a guy who has acted with the Royal Shakespearean group in England, and yet is stuck in this rut of ever more increasing action and ever decreasing plot.

The plot itself seemed to be stretched as much as the face of the main "villain." It seems like they combined several plots from years 1-3 into one big 2 hour "best of" episode. Except it's not even that - it's not the "best of" It's more like stuff I wish they had left alone. Well in any case, to make a long story short if you've seen Symbiosis, Captain's Holiday, Descent, and several other episodes, then you can feel confident that going into the movie, the only suprise you will see will be some woman besides Crusher interested in Picard.

That kind of nags at me, too, cause what's up with the two of them? She's just like his bitch or something and is around whenever he can't get none from the Archeologist of the week (remember Vash?)

I mean what happened to the 7th season romance between the two of them, and the episode where they were joined at the hip, so to speak? Oh well, it's not all bad - there are some scenes that I did enjoy, namely ones not involving Data. Don't get me wrong, i think that Brent Spiner is a great actor, but they're just not giving him anything new to do at all. It's the same old "What's Data's malfunction-of-the-week?" spinoff episode... Data must be using the 24th century equivalent of Windows 98...

All in all it's not the greatest of the movies, but it was admittedly better than all of the "original" series movies. My only wish was that they stop focusing so much on Picard and Data, and instead on the other cast members... I mean hello, there are other people in the cast! The guest stars all have more lines than the other cast members now... It's a shame, really. All they did with Geordi was give him some new eyeballs (sound familiar anyone?). It was a good scene, but I'm depressed that they don't do more with him... especially cause he's the only actor besides Stewart that has done anything outside of Star Trek these last few years.

I'll probably see it again in the theaters before it gets kicked out by other schlock, but if you're not already a fan of the series, don't plan on starting with this movie...the only insurrection I saw was fans storming theaters to get in.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-07T00:53:00Z |title=HellDraft and StarDraft update: |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just wanted to let you all know of a few updates that Merlin informed me of today:

HellDraft is complete right now, but for some reason, patch 1.07 is not cooperating at all. HellDraft patches ok, but then the patches are instantly over-written somehow. Blizzard may have done some strange things with 1.07 that we weren't aware of. In any case, Merlin says that it should take him about a week to fix the problem (due to schoolwork, etc).

StarDraft Special Edition Alpha was a smashing success except for one or two small bugs that we are now working on fixing. Merlin estimates that a new version of StarDraft SE will be available immediately after HellDraft, which is now our primary software concern.

Some of you may have noticed that I removed a significant portion of our software from the Software Live Wire and from the Software pages. This is because all the programs I removed are dupicated in StarDraft Special Edition, and I felt it was kinda silly to offer 2 versions of almost the same thing.

Another note to be aware of is that those programs I just mentioned are plugins for StarDraft Special Edition, and require that you rung them from CWAD Manager, not seperately as some people have attempted.

In one of the upcoming releases of StarDraft and/or HellDraft, Merlin will be adding the ability for users to register CWAD files to open up in CWAD Manager, and perhaps even StarDraft. This should save some time for those of you creating or running lots of customs files.

Phew, that was a long post. Hehe, last few days have had a lot of long posts. :) Oops. :) Oh well... deal with it.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-04T05:23:00Z |title=More HellDraft news... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just thought I'd clear up a few things before tomorrow's target release date for the public version of HellDraft:

* HellDraft v1.0 Public will allow users to patch any files except WAVs and SMKs into the game.
* Patching is done using a patch loader which reads from a CWAD Archive that either the player can create or download from somewhere.
* CWADs are created with the CWAD Manager, using the same interface that was created for StarDraft. CWAD Manager will come with the v1.0 Public version of HellDraft.
* CWAD Manager contains no plugins right now *except* an MPQ VIewer which allows you to extract files from the Diablo MPQ Archives
* Battle.net play has not been tested with HellDraft. However, it is assumed that any non-graphical or textual changes will result in a desync/disconnect from the game *if* both players are not using the same patch - basically it should act like StarDraft would.
* No changes made in a patch are permanent. You can have as many different CWAD files in your HellDraft directory as you want, and each can contain its own Total Conversion which won't interfere with any of the others.

I think that sums up most of the common questions so far. If you still need some answers, use the email link above (just click on my name beside the post title).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-03T00:11:00Z |title=Partial apology for yesterday's rant... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm posting this to try and apologize to some of my affiliates that were disturbed by the Rant I posted yesterday. With StarCraft.org, for example, I had sent the email to Mutant, who according to Mr. Plow is away for 3 weeks, this being the 3rd week. When Mutant left, I understood him to be gone for 2 weeks, not 3, and since I saw him the other day online, I figured that he was back. Apparently I was wrong. StarCraft Legacy I neglected to include in the email list because although I sent it to Cleric, I did so not realizing that the email list I was using was outdated, and so never reached anyone there.

Blizzardgames.com I did manually submit the news to them using the form on their site - I requested a reply but haven't heard from them.

Starcraft Infinity I sent the news to thru Dioxide, and since he's been offline/busy the last couple of days, the news also didn't get posted. Again, I thought perhaps he was just getting online at different hours, not that he was offline completely.

I don't have the emails of too many other webmasters from the newer sites - StarCraft Tarsonis just to name one that quickly comes to mind, and when i get the chance I'll try to find one or two.

I did send Lanhdanan and Tender Fury emails but since they're CS Beta testers they might have thought it was supposed to be confidential and also didn't post.

So I'm sorry for assuming that every site is like mine and has one person dedicated to doing all the news updates and webmastering.... it does make it easier to know who to contact/blame, but harder to get a reply from in the long run.

So again, sorry if I offended anyone who had "speshul" circumstances with the email. I guess I'm kinda glad I was wrong and that I can still count on some sites to post my news :) I realize that some of them might be discouraged cause I don't post news from other sites (generally) but that's a general rule that I like to follow because I don't consider myself a news site. I consider myself a news provider, like Blizzard would be (no I'm not comparing our software to Blizzard) because we have a goal in common that is to release software, and releasing software generates news that other sites post about....

Anyways, sorry to waste more of your time if you're not interested.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-12-02T04:21:00Z |title=Warning: Rant |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok this kind of pisses me off... sunday afternoon when we posted the HellDraft Beta for our testers, I sent out a mass email to what I consider the "Big" sites - sites that I used to send news to, and sites that would normally post anything I sent them (within reason of course). Now perhaps my email was a bit confusing? If so, I'd appreciate it if they let me know...

All I asked basically (from my perspective) was that they post that we had just released the HellDraft Beta to our testers, and that we were going to be releasing it to the public on Friday.

I consider that to be fairly newsworthy for a couple of reasons:

1. We're not some lame-ass backwater site that nobody's heard of that is hosted by Geoshitties. We're what I would consider one of the high-traffic StarCraft sites...
2. After the StarDraft sensation, a Diablo follow-up has to merit at least a post. Hey, I mean they post like every time EmperorZileas breathes... and he's not even speshul. Any old person given lots of free time and a reasonably competant mind can comquer the StarCraft Ladder - Beta or not - there aren't too many people who can practically single-handedly hack into a game format that's been around since Diablo and lord knows how many hardcore-cheater programmers trying to artificially improve their records.
3. I don't post a lot of news, I'll grant you that. I try to weed out the boring ass stuff like "I just figured out how to queue units"... The news I do post, however, I consider to be important. Heck, if I took the time to post it, it's probably worth most people's time to read it, ya know?
4. StarCraft's dead until Brood War comes out - and there was no news this weekend due to Thanksgiving and all, so what's the deal here, really?

Now guys, I don't have anything against you all... I just wish I knew why you (and a lot of other people) are starting to ignore me all of a sudden. I've emailed everyone to my complete boredom about various things, and often times I get no responses... that's bad form, and it generally causes shit like this to happen. I mean I get some really silly (notice I didn't say stupid) emails about StarDraft... but I try to answer them all, cause I realize that we have an obligation to support our software, even though it's free. Heck, if you think about it, their news is kind of like their software, and I think they should operate on the same kind of moral code that I do.

But hell we all know that's a little too pessimistic.

5 bucks says that it'll turn out that my email program fucked everything up and never even sends out half of my goddamned email messages. So guys, go easy on me, huh? :)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-29T20:15:00Z |title=HellDraft in Beta, Public release forthcoming... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well today thanks to Merlin's hard work and my prodding (or blackmail if you prefer), HellDraft has made it into Beta testing. By the time you read this post, the Beta testers will have it in their hands and can start doing what they want with it :)

Anyways, all Beta Testers - if you haven't gotten the email yet from me, please send me an [email](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com), and I'll send along the address where you can download it.

HellDraft Beta testing should be completed by midnight on Thursday, and we will impliment the necessary bug changes on Friday to get hopefully a release done for Friday or Saturday.

Right now there are no plugins aside from an MPQ Viewer (like the one that came with StarDraft's CWAD Manager). CWAD Manager also will be in HellDraft for you to use.

Now you may be wondering... what does this mean for Camelot Systems... well it means we are differentiating, basically. We are no longer going to be just a StarCraft-based website. I won't necessarily be covering Diablo news or anything, but our focus will no longer be just on StarCraft.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-28T15:50:00Z |title=AI script update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just in case any of you were wondering, my work with the AI Scripts has been pretty good so far... I was able to change one of the Protoss Campaign's AI's to produce ONLY Templars... and lots of them... for those of you wondering... can't I just do that with Create Unit triggers... that's a different story. I'm talking about the AI scripts used with the Run AI Script trigger action. So anyways, I've got some more experimenting to do today... but that doesn't mean I'll be done today... :) I'll try to write up a new AI Script sometime soon so that you can all have fun playing around with it. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-27T16:14:00Z |title=Miscellaneous updates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just wanted to keep you all informed of what's going on behind the scenes here at Camelot Systems:

* Merlin is working on StarDraft Special Edition (SE). Right now it's in Alpha testing and will be shipping to our Beta Testers soon - don't even waste your time emailing me to ask to Beta Test... I'm ignoring all further requests. Sorry if that seems rude.
* I'm working on a couple of other projects - as you've probably noticed. Stuff like the Allied General Editor is just stuff I've wanted to do for a while but haven't gotten a chance to do until now. This is because I'm about at the end of the StarCraft files that I can edit without problems.
* Speaking of files I'm editing... I'm working on decoding the Aiscript.bin file so hopefully we can get some new AIs working - and better yet an AI Editor, too.
* Arsenal and TBLEdit are soon going to disappear from the Camelot Systems software pages. No, we're not going to start charging for them or anything. Merlin's taking code that I added to SCUnit (code for the remaining Arsenal files that SCUnit didn't edit) and is adding it to CWAD Manager in the form of Arsenal II. This is being done in an attempt to simplify the unit editing and CWAD Making processes.

I think that's about it... no exact release dates on any of this stuff.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-24T05:14:00Z |title=AGE (Allied General Editor) Released. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yeah, I know it's an old game, but I love it... I dug it out recently because I'm taking this really cool WW2 class in college, and I hadn't played it in a while... I kinda sucked to begin with, so I ahem wanted to make a program that would edit the equpiment statistics to give the Allies more of a chance. :) Or at least those Allied forces under my command. :)

Anyways, I've uploaded the editor to a new page in the software section called "General" Series Software. Basically I intend to release editors for all of the games in the "General" series from Strategic Simulations, Inc. - which include Panzer General, Panzer General II, Pacific General, People's General, Star General, and Fantasy General. Actually AGE will work fine without any modifications with Panzer General's Equipment files, too. :) But the rest I will be making new editors for as I get the games themselves.

* [Allied General Editor (AGE)](software/Age.zip) - 170kb - Programmed by King Arthur
* [Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime Libraries](software/sysfiles.zip) - (1.7mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-23T03:12:00Z |title=StatPrinter Released! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, it's been over a week since I emailed Shlonglor my deadline for friday as the last day he could prevent me from releasing StatPrinter to the public... and I didn't get an email yet, so here I go - it's released. And I made a pre-emptive strike at the [QuickStart](qstart.htm) Tutorials page... adding what I included as the Readme for StatPrinter to it in a new section. I will try to do that for all new program releases if possible. So some of you are probably wondering what StatPrinter does, and should I download it?

First of all, it's not an editing program... no this is a different kind of coolness. :) This program generates HTML Tables of statistics for whatever units you want, based on whatever statistics you want, in the colors and format you want. :) Basically you can generate a 280k+ file in around... 2 seconds with all the stats for all the units in StarCraft... compared to the normal oh... 4 hours with anything else? :)

* [StatPrinter v1.0](software/statprint.zip) - (234kb) - Designed by King Arthur using GFrazier (Shlonglor)'s HTML structure on the StarCraft Compendium page.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-21T04:22:00Z |title=Funcraft III: Deride and Plunder, StarCraft Edition news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yeah, I know it's a long title. Deal with it. :) Tonight I posted a unit conversion chart which I intend to use in creating the StarCraft Edition of FunCraft III: Deride and Plunder. Thanks to the advances in StarCraft, much more will be possible in this edition than was in the WarCraft edition. Also, thanks to the help of my very talented friend Thrakerzog, we will have completely new graphics for the units. :) He won't necessarily be doing all of them, as long as I can find some other people to help out with them. Sound conversions and storylines will all be handled by me, and I've already got a superb former-PUD maker, Duriel, to help with the scums. FunCraft will not be like playing StarCraft - it will be sort of a combination between StarCraft and Diablo - half RTS half RPG...

Check out the [FunCraft III: Deride and Plunder, SC Edition Unit List](Funcraft/fc3sc.htm)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-17T04:50:00Z |title=New forums coming soon... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I decided that the General Discussion forum was getting a little too crowded for some people to find information that they were looking for to help them out - so I'm going to be adding two new web forums to the page sometime this week hopefully. One will concentrate on Tech Support, while another will center on Custom Development. Both will go online at about the same time, and the General Discussion forum will remain open as usual. I'd obviously prefer if people stuck to the general topic of the forum (i.e. post messages on the appropriate forum), but I won't be policing it...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-16T06:27:00Z |title=StatPrinter due out soon... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I haven't recieved word back from Shlonglor yet, but if I don't hear from him by the end of the week I will go ahead and release StatPrinter anyways. Version 1.0 will definately support all StarCraft versions - and possibly Brood War if I can figure out the last few remaining bugs with it. Merlin also wants to add a unit-comparator to it (a feature that predicts the results of a battle between two given units).

Future versions of StatPrinter will probably have support for SCuM stat generating, among other things. Work on Arsenal II went well this weekend - I managed to program in support for all files now except for sprites.dat (YIPEE!) - so no more switching beween SCUnit and Arsenal - it's all in one big program now. :) As soon as Brood War support is added to Arsenal II, I think we should be set to release it, too.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-13T22:22:00Z |title=Funcraft uploaded. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well this should make all you FunCraft fans happy... finally after about 6 months of FunCraft being missing from the Camelot Systems page, I have decided to give into popular demand and re-instate it as part of the site. After all, it was the reason that this site exists in the first place, so I guess I kinda owe it to the TC. :) Anyways, just to be clear, this is for *WarCraft II* - not StarCraft... yet. :)

*[Funcraft III: Deride and Plunder](Funcraft/funcraft.zip) - (4.5 mb) - Designed by King Arthur
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-13T06:43:00Z |title=Stat Printer screenshots. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I realize that Lan of StarShack released Screenshots yesterday from StatPrinter... but they weren't of great quality, and so I wanted to release ones that better showed Stat Printer and its features off. This is also the screenshot from the new beta version (4) that my inside testers are probably firing up as I write this. :)

* [sp1.jpg](images/sp1.jpg)
* [sp2.jpg](images/sp2.jpg)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-13T01:40:00Z |title=SCM2BMP back from the dead... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A few months ago before StarDraft or anything of the sort was out, I had talked about a program I intended to create called SCM2JPG or SCM2GIF... well now it's being resurrected (I had dropped the project in favor of other things) in the form of SCM2BMP... all you webmasters out there will for at least the first version be forced into batch file conversions of the generated minimaps. This program is no longer being created by me, however. Brainiac (soon to be a regular CS staff member if he finishes this :) is working on it. I may be helping him out some, but it will primarily be his project from now on.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-12T04:54:00Z |title=Total Conversion section up... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, right now I've only got a link to one Total Conversion page, so please send me some more URL's to add to the page. The page is accessable from the "Top Sites" link on the left, and you can also see it [here](links/tclinks.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-12T02:25:00Z |title=Now my interview's up for real. :) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[StarCraft Abundance](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224158/http://starcraft.bwdynasty.com/) had trouble with their FTP server the other day, and so they couldn't upload their interview with me like I thought they were going to... According to BallooGa it's up there now (I did see it there, too :)... go check it out [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224158/http://starcraft.bwdynasty.com/).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-11T20:21:00Z |title=Custom submission request... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

*KA takes shelter in bunker*

Realizing that this may prove to be a rather time consuming venture, I'm asking for authors of TC's to start submitting URL's for their Total Conversions, and possibly even TC's or custom files themselves that would be hosted by Camelot Systems. Please do not submit any Brood War-to-StarCraft, StarCraft-to-Brood War, or WarCraft-to-StarCraft conversions. We would like the content to be as original as possible. I also suppose this is as good a time as any to announce the return of FunCraft III (for WarCraft II)... I will be posting it on the site (hopefully) tonight or tomorrow since so many people have asked for it. If anyone's interested in doing a StarCraft version of FunCraft III, feel free to email me, and I'll get you up to speed on what I want to do with it in StarCraft.

The reason I'm doing this is because there's no really good database / listing of Total Conversions on the net right now for SC... and what better place to host that list then the place that gave you the tools to do the Total Conversions in the first place?

So if you can, please send me an email with your TC's name, website, background storyline if necessary, and staff list...

This should be interesting... :)

I may even do a "Community News" section where you can report on the status of your TC, etc... dunno yet.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-11T19:42:00Z |title=Arsenal II v1.0 due out soon... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Right now I'm in the process of working on Arsenal II v1.0 - formerly known as SCUnit. I'm trying to make some aspects of SCUnit more closely associated with the counterpart in Arsenal... and I will be adding support for editing of all the files that the original Arsnenal has. This does not mean that work is done on Arsenal I - I won't be done with it until the 4.0 Beta test is complete.

I'm also proud to announce a brand-new feature for SCUnit and the Arsenal line in general - the Stat Printer. With the assitance of Lan from StarShack and Geoff Frazier (Shlonglor), I have created a Unit Stat printer which generates an HTML table of stats that you request for units that you want to see them for. Right now the format is almost exactly duplicated from Geoff's work on Blizzard's SC Compendium - on purpose. It was originally created with him (and SC Compendium) in mind... so that he could work on the Zerg and Protoss equivalent of the Terran Unit Info page... Depending on what Geoff wants me to do, I may or may not be releasing it to the public... or if I do it may be without some features available to him. Lan is helping me to think up stat features, etc to add to it, and I thank him very much for his help with the Stat Printer... it really looks awesome when it works. :) Anyways, please do not email Lan or Geoff asking for a copy of the Stat Printer or Arsenal II. They have both been instructed to give it out to no one else. Same thing goes for emailing me and asking me for a copy... I'm hand picking the Beta testers for this one... in fact I can only think of one or two more people that I want testing it, and those people already know who they are. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-09T22:50:00Z |title=WarCraft II Total Conversion annoucement... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A good friend of mine, Shaundog, wanted me to announce that he's put a page up with information on a WarCraft II Total Conversion that he's creating. I must say that from the 4 screenshot's he's got up now, it looks very promising... especially the new Gnomish Flying Machine... he's also got the ability now to have units go *inside* buildings... very cool. :)

* [WarCraft II TC](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224158/http://208.132.73.2/users/shaun/_warcraft2.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-09T05:17:00Z |title=Arsenal bug fixes... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I had a few problems earlier today with my copy of Arsenal 4.0 Beta 1, so I took a look at the code and found some old bugs that nobody had reported yet - specifically about the Default sfxdata.dat... I had included a corrupted version. So if you are downloading the new Beta (2) that I've just uploaded, be sure you re-extract the sfxdata.dat to your Defaults directory. There are a few more bug-fixes - mostly correcting file-loading procedures and errors.

* [Arsenal 4.0 Beta 2](software/ar40beta.zip) - (206kb) - Designed by King Arthur
* [Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime Libraries](software/sysfiles.zip) - (1.7mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-08T18:50:00Z |title=King Arthur Interview at SC Abundance... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yeah, so I took a break for a few minutes the other day and spewed out a great deal of information about myself, Camelot Systems, and my goals... BallooGa at our affiliate [SC Abundance](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224158/http://starcraft.bwdynasty.com/) managed to catch most of it in a chat we had. Actually it started out as a chat, but I had to leave so he emailed me the rest of the questions... hence why he's pretty silent for great stretches of time. :) Go check it out if you want leverage in poking fun at me. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-08T18:43:00Z |title=Arsenal quick fix... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

There was a problem last night because I was so tired I forgot to update the text files in the zip :) oops. Anyways, I've re-uploaded the beta... so if you were getting an "Error (62): Input past end of file" error that's most likely what caused it. :) Sorry for the goof up. :) Also last night's version didn't have enough space to store some of the Beta files like it should have. :) I fixed that too.

* [Arsenal 4.0 Beta 1](software/ar40beta.zip) - (233kb) - Designed by King Arthur
* [Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime Libraries](software/sysfiles.zip) - (1.2mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-08T05:49:00Z |title=Arsenal 4.0 Beta 1 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just completed Arsenal 4.0 Beta 1, and have uploaded it to the server. This version has support for Brood War data files. Actually, as long as Blizzard keeps the number of bytes per weapon, etc the same, I will never need to update Arsenal after this version goes out of Beta - it will automatically recognize the file length, and open it accordingly.

Note that I have not replaced the previous version of Arsenal - I merely added this beta test. This is a beta open to everyone... I welcome all your bug reports (I'll have a page for them later to make sure that you don't send in any duplicates), but be aware that Tech Support with this beta will be very limited... if any at all. I encourage you to test it as thoroughly as possible, because I am trying to get it as refined as possible before I release it officially. Thanks for your help.

* [Arsenal 4.0 Beta 1](software/ar40beta.zip) - (233kb) - Created by King Arthur
* [Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime Libraries](software/sdruntime.zip) - (1.7mb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-07T22:49:00Z |title=New poll available |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've changed our poll of the week up at the top of the page to ask what you want to see more of from Camelot Systems. I'd appreciate your input so we can help to make y'all more satisfied. :) Thanks.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-07T22:41:00Z |title=Poll results |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The latest poll (in which people voted on how they thought the page looked) had 63% of the people say they liked the layout the way it was, 21% who didn't like it at all, and around 16% who weren't sure. So I guess that means I'll keep most of the page the same, but try to spruce it up here and there when I get the chance.

Thanks for your opinions on that. I'll be posting a new poll in a few mins.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-07T22:30:00Z |title=HellDraft Beta "signup" closed. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We now have plenty of people who will be testing the HellDraft Beta when it is completed, so I am now ending the Beta "signup" period. Please do not send me any more screenshots or requests to get into the Beta. For those of you who didn't make it, thanks for trying - and don't worry. That was the *only* time I will be doing that form of Beta "signup"... The next one (probably StarDraft - a new edition, will have a regular Beta Signup where I will choose people at random (or however random I can get :)

Also - for those of you who didn't get in the Beta test for HellDraft - don't fret - we'll be releasing a HellDraft DEMO just like we did with StarDraft, so you can still use that. It should be released around the same time as the Beta. The Demo however, will be without Tech Support. In other words, you can send me your problems, but don't expect a reply until the full version is out.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-07T22:27:00Z |title=SCMod Updated |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Today I worked on improving certain areas in the [SCMod](SCMod/) section in hopes that you would find them more useful. Contrary to what was previously thought, I did *not* take down all of my Brood War Unit, Hero, and Building info/gfx. In fact, today I added the hero portraits to the heroes page for the Brood War Info section. I also added 3 new pages with stats for all of the units in the game - stats you normally never see (i.e. sight range, attack range, attack rate, damage type, and point value).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-05T05:33:00Z |title=Camelot Systems Software QuickStarts available |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just published the first 3 in a series of who knows how many QuickStart Tutorials. Right now I have two up for StarDraft (each covers a different common problem/issue), and another up for TBLEdit (on how to change unit names). You can check them out [here](qstart.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-05T02:37:00Z |title=Forum back online! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, it's about darn tootin' time that this stinking forum is back! :) For those of you who bookmarked the old url, you will need to update the bookmarks to the new address: [http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_gen/index.cgi](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224158/http://www.camsys.org/cgi/wwwboard_gen/index.cgi). I would have kept it at the old location, but the CGI wasn't executing properly from the old location. So what are you waiting for? Go check out the forum via the button on the left hand side of the page!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-04T04:31:00Z |title=StarDraft for NT and 98 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Before HellDraft is released, Merlin will be working on getting StarDraft working for Windows NT/98. About 50% of the users of StarDraft who run 98 haven't had any problems, but 100% of the NT users have. Hopefully the same problem that disables StarDraft in NT will fix the remainder of the 98 problems... It should be out within the next week or so.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-04T04:05:00Z |title=More HellDraft Beta Stuff |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Note: officially the Beta test registering is not done. I think I'll wait until I get around 10-15 beta testers (around 135,000 hits to the site). - Also, a lot of webmasters of both prominent and unknown sites have asked me to be part of the HellDraft Beta - much like webmasters got a free beta with Blizzard's Brood War Beta. I'm not going to get into that. Everyone should have a fair chance to test it out. Besides, that means I have to cater to around 30+ other webmasters and make sure they all get the Beta, etc. Frankly, we don't really need all that many to test it out.

My plan for distribution is this: Beta testers will recieve the beta as soon as we can get a workable version (obviously). We will simultaneously release a DEMO version for the general public - just to keep you all happy. However, the DEMO version I won't be asking for bug reports/ usability issues from. The DEMO will have NO tech support. The Beta will have the only Tech Support. We hope to run this a little more smoothely than the StarDraft evolution was.

At this point I also have to say that Camelot Systems probably will not be making the majority of the plugin utilities for HellDraft. If we do, it will largely be the responsibility of CodeLord (who is quite familiar with the technical aspects of Diablo). We plan to release the necessary info to the public to make user-created plugins feasable. Thankfully that means I have less Tech Support to worry about. :) Phew.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-04T03:57:00Z |title=HellDraft clarification |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A number of people (ok, a huge number) have emailed me about HellDraft. Since many people seemed to be confused about its details, etc, I thought I'd try to clear a few things up for y'all:

* HellDraft is not finished by any means. Merlin has begun work on it, but its end date (for full release) will be in around 5 weeks. This is due to a large number of reasons - most of which is that both Merlin and I have a lot of school work that needs to get done between now and then; hence the long delay.
* HellDraft is *not* as some people thought a cheat program for Diablo. If it were - what's the point? Diablo's already been hacked to (near) death that way anyways.
* HellDraft (with minor modifications) should work fine with Diablo II - unless Blizzard drastically changes the way that they store archives (with MPQ's).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-11-02T05:24:00Z |title=HellDraft Beta Testers Wanted... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

In the past I advertised on the site for Beta testers for various programs... and got varying responses. This time I'm going to let randomness (or persistence) pick who gets to Beta-test HellDraft when it's complete. (A limited demo version will be available to all while a non-limited Beta will be made available to those people who meet the following qualifications):

1. You must be running Windows 95. 98 and NT have given us lots of problems with StarDraft.
2. You must send me in a screenshot proving that you are one of several of the thousand-mark visitors (ex: 125000, 126000, etc).

I know... it's a cheap and desperate ploy for more hits. But I'm too tired to sort through a deluge of applications. This is the only fair way that I can think of. Thanks to Thrakerzog for this (evil?) idea. :)
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=October 1998}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-31T21:05:00Z |title=HellDraft poll results = HellDraft in the works. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As a result of the poll I had up this week and last (I think), Camelot Systems will attempt to create HellDraft. Thankfully the theory behind it is the same as StarDraft, so at least the tough part is already done (I hope :). I just have to talk to Merlin, who's been away this week, into doing it. I think when he knows how much support there was for it (80% of around 1000 votes), he'll have no problems with it. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-31T20:50:00Z |title=Regarding the Forum Problems |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Over the last two weeks, Camelot Systems (and most other [Warzone](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.warzone.com/) hosted sites) have been experiencing CGI problems. They decided to shut down all non-essential CGI until they could evaluate the code being used, etc. I have talked to Betamax from the Warzone staff, and he has assured me that he will do his best to get our forum CGI back online as soon as possible. In about two weeks, Warzone plans to have a brand-new form of forums for most of its sites to use, so we will be converting over to that when it becomes available. I'll try to announce when our current forum is back online, and when the new one becomes available. Thanks for your patience.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-31T04:53:00Z |title=Re: Blizzard BW "Complaints" |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I saw a post on [StarCraft Infoceptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.infoceptor.net/) made by Deathwing earlier today which mentioned a letter he had apparently recieved from Blizzard which complained about the amount of Brood War things that were being extracted and released from the MPQ file. While I realize what Blizzard is trying to do - protect their investment - I'm not selling the graphics, either. But I guess if they tell me to rip the gfx and info off the site, I'll have no choice but to do that. I'm kinda perplexed as to why Blizzard would send only Infoceptor an email, considering that I had many more "illegal" items on the page than they did... oh well. maybe Blizz doesn't know I've been a naughty boy. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-29T05:59:00Z |title=StarCraft.org has the new Brood Wars Medic unit sounds |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Hehe. I didn't realize quite how useful this was going to be, but yet again the new sc_data.txt file has come in handy as I was able to help Mutant at [StarCraft.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/) to extract all the sounds for the medic from the Brood Wars Beta for the new units.

[Medic Sounds](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/bw-beta/medicsounds.shtml)

Mutant plans to publish the rest of the new sounds later tonight or tomorrow, so check out SC.org for updates until I post the complete list of the sounds pages.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-29T05:45:00Z |title=New hero portraits and Disruption web graphics uploaded. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Mutant of [StarCraft.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/) just sent me the new portraits for the heroes of Brood War and the Disruption Web graphics which he extracted with the new SC_Data.txt file that I created last night.

* [Disruption Web](images/Disrupt.gif)
* [Sam? Duran (Ghost)](SCMod/tsam.zip)
* [Alex? Stukov (Ghost)](SCMod/talex.zip)
* [DeGalle (Ghost)](SCMod/TDugal.zip)
* [Artanis (Scout)](SCMod/partanis.zip)

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-28T06:45:00Z |title=Brood War AI's Released :) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-28T06:26:00Z |title=New MPQ Viewer data file uploaded. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-28T05:28:00Z |title=New Brood War Unit, Building, Hero, and AI Info! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-27T03:27:00Z |title=Brood War support to be added to Arsenal, SCUnit, and StarDraft. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Camelot Systems will be adding support for Brood War Beta files to be manipulated in Arsenal, SCUnit, and StarDraft as soon as we can over the next few weeks. We'll be posting the new versions of these programs after we've done some experimentation with the new data files and see what we can possibly do in BW that we can't in StarCraft.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-26T05:03:00Z |title=Sections updated with new layout |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I decided since I was giving the main page a facelift, it was only fair that I go around and update all the rest of the pages. :)I've added lots of images around the page to make your navigation more pleasant... since some people complained that there was too much of this boring text all over the place. :)

Anyways... I also added a new section - in case you haven't noticed yet - it's [SCMod](SCMod/), a place where I will be putting customs examples, screenshots, and other goodies. :) I was going to throw this stuff into SCForm, but it didn't seem appropriate there...

Also, the poll seems to have been consistent all day - around 80% in favor, and 20% against HellDraft. I'll leave the poll up for a few more days and then I'll probably replace it with another one.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-25T16:08:00Z |title=HellDraft Poll added |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I decided that I needed to add a poll to help us decide if we should work on HellDraft (a Diablo version of StarDraft). We're fairly confident that it is simple to do now that we have gotten StarDraft to work - they both work on the same principle - but we're not sure if there's enough demand to do it left still... so please vote using the above script. I guess after a week or two I will see how the results are going, and then well... make the decision based on that. :) Thanks for your participation!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-25T04:58:00Z |title=Layout update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok, obviously if you're here, you can see that a few things have changed on the site. I hope it's for the better... Just in case you're as delirious as I am, here's what we've changed:

* New "normal" and "highlight" buttons for the menu on the left of the page. I think they're easier to read than the older ones, and they're smaller so they take up less space... Please let me know if you think they're not either of those two things. :)
* I'm proud to announce my "invention" - the Software Live Wire :) It will have on it links to all the latest versions of Camelot Systems StarCraft software. I think it's much easier to get the latest software than to scroll through a couple of pages of text to find what you're looking for. :) It'll grow as we get more software, and I may end up adding other sections to it (non-starcraft stuff)
* Each of the employees now has their own jpg with their name on it. :) That makes it look cooler than when we were using small (hard to read) grey text. :) Let me know if it makes the load time horrendous.
* If I'm lucky, sometime this weekend there will be more layout updates as I get the chance. I'll probably be re-organizing the pages themselves, too.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-15T00:52:00Z |title=StarCraft.org Customs Section Opens! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[Mr. Book](mailto:Mr.Book@starcraft.org) of [StarCraft.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/) has just told me that he's officially opened up the [Customs Section](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/customs/) at our affiliate's site. He and his counterpart ([Mr. Sleep](mailto:Mr.Sleep@starcraft.org) were the two guys that Merlin and I trained. They also just uploaded their first custom - a handy and amusing little thing which enables many of the previously inaccessable units / things for use in the normal StarEdit map editor. While they realize that some of you may remember a previous custom which went along the same basis, this one is in fact quite different. This one actually sorts the new units into their appropriate places, unlike jumbling them all together into one mammoth menu. Eventually they plan to write up tutorials to show you all how they did it! Sounds really Nif-T (tm).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-04T16:24:00Z |title=Regarding Moneran |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok, I've gotten a lot of complaints / questions / comments the last few days about what happened to Moneran. Some people saw a post made by him (and later removed from) on StarCraft.org - saying that he'd joined their staff. That was not quite true. What had actually happened was that when Cleric of [Theory Squad](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.sclegacy.com/ts/) moved from [SC Legacy](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.sclegacy.com/) to our affiliates [StarCraft.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/), Moneran was still a part of Theory Squad, and somehow obtained FTP or CGI access to SC.org. We had fired him about a week before this happened, and I presume that he was seeking employment elsewhere in order to continue to be involved with the StarCraft online universe. That much I don't have a problem with. What does bother me is that so many people have flamed both the message board, my email address(es), and my ICQ UIN aboutmy firing him. We fired him for several very good and justifiable reasons. However, I do not believe that I owe you or anyone else an explanation of why he was fired. Camelot Systems is not an actual company (yet), you do not hold stock in our company, and you are not paying customers... so whatever we do we should be able to do without having to worry about public outcry. Obviously, you are all entitled to your opinions, but be aware that any requests to re-instate Moneran at Camelot Systems,or for an explanation of why he was fired will go un-answered by me or anyone else at Camelot Systems. We simply do not want to discuss the matter, and we do not believe that it needs to become a public controversy / debate. Thank you for your understanding, and that is the last time I will mention his name or anything else regarding him.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-02T02:13:00Z |title=StarCraft.org to open Customs section |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, folks I was wondering how long this would take! :) Our affiliates, [StarCraft.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://www.starcraft.org/) has finally announced plans to open a customs section. The two guys running it are [Mr. Sleep](mailto:MrSleep@starcraft.org) and [Mr. Book](mailto:MrBook@starcraft.org). Both of these guys were personally tutored by yours truly and Merlin. - Don't even bother to ask if we'll train you. Sorry, we trained these guys because we feel they can handle a lot of stuff on their own, and they are also personal (long-time) friends of ours... and the fact that I have no time makes it less likely that I'll train you, too. Supposedly the Customs section should be opening up over there fairly soon.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-02T02:10:00Z |title=Re: Camelot Systems Affiliation |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

A number of people have emailed me lately about possible affiliation between their site and Camelot Systems. They've also asked me to put up links to their sites. To the affiliaton question, I must answer the following: Camelot Systems is quite satisified with the affiliates we now have. Until such time that we feel we would likemore affiliates, or we decide to drop one for whatever reason, we aren't accepting offers of affiliation. As for links: I generally only put links up to sites that I generally visit. I detest reciprocal links mainly because they don't really show how much you value the site you've linked to. I may, however, add a section on the links page for Total Conversions... that being the only exception to the rule.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-10-02T02:07:00Z |title=StarZone relocates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

StarZone, one of Camelot System's Affiliates, has shut down at its old URL and will open up shortly here: [http://starzone.geminisector.com/](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224203/http://starzone.geminisector.com/). And yes, I will change the link as soon as they are done moving.
