{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-21T05:46:00Z |title=Arsenal v3.0 Uploaded (Finally!) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After about a week of getting new features added to Arsenal (most of them were added this weekend), I finally have Arsenal 3.0 ready for public release. And before you download it, I have to warn you... although there is support for 5 more dat files with it, the variables in 4 of those dat files are all unknown, and there is still no support for units.dat :(

Here's what you can edit:

* Weapons.dat
* Upgrades.dat
* Techdata.dat
* Images.dat
* Sfxdata.dat
* Portdata.dat
* Mapdata.dat
* Flingy.dat

No, Flingy.dat is not a joke. :) I don't know what it does yet, tho. So since I do have a lot of unknown variables here, and not much time to test stuff out in, if you find out what an unknown variable does, by all means let me know, and I'll add the correct label to 3.1

* [Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime Libraries](software/sysfiles.zip) (1.7mb)
* [Download Arsenal 3.0](software/arsenal.zip) (206kb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-21T05:44:00Z |title=TBLEdit v1.2 Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Since a number of people still had problems with version 1.1 of TBLEdit, I decided to try and fix the remaining problems I could find, and also add the ability for you to either Export or Import text files into the editor (and save them as TBL files if you want).

Basically that means you can now add strings to the names table (stat_txt.tbl) if you want. :)

Oh yeah, in case you didn't notice, the size of the zip file shrunk dramatically :) (15kb!!!)

* [Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 Runtime Libraries](software/sysfiles.zip) (1.7mb)
* [Download TBLEdit v1.2](software/tbledit.zip) (15kb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-20T03:52:00Z |title=New Version of SCM protector [link fixed] |author=Moneran |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SCM PROTECTOR v1.1 has been released. What's new? Much better encryption. [Get it](software/protect.zip), if you don't already have it.

Note: SCM protector features one-way encryption. The advantage of that is that it is more difficult for people to unencrypt the SCM. That also means that map creators should keep two versions of the map: The StarDraft editable version, and the released version. Think of it like source-code and executables.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-19T17:02:00Z |title=SCM Protector Released |author=Moneran |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ever annoyed by when you make a map, and then three days later there are six different versions of it, each with really lame modifications? (Sometimes even with someone else's name on it)

Worry no more! SCM Protector prevents people from opening your SCM in StarEdit. [Click here](software/protect.zip) to get it.

([E-mail me](mailto:beta@home.com) if you get any problems)

Special Thanks to TechnoSCUM (Theory Squad) for the SCM Protector idea.

(And, as always, no SCM-related programs would be possible without King Arthur's [SCForm](SCForm))
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-11T01:30:00Z |title=General |author=Merlin |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Yes! the fabled merlin does exist! :)
Well as you most know I have been devoting a large amount of time to CWAD manager, and I plan to have a new release of StarDraft soon.  Trust me it will make the minor pain of CWADs well worth it :) I must thank you all for your patience with StarDraft..

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-09T23:28:00Z |title=StarDraft 2.0C - no bug reports so far |author=Moneran |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

StarDraft 2.0C seems to be a pretty good release. No bugs yet. If you have any bug reports, [send them in](mailto:beta@rogers.wave.ca?subject=bugs), preferably with steps in order to reproduce the bug.

Note: On some files, StarDraft takes a *long* time to compress. You just have to wait it out. (Aka the 25+ files "bug" never existed)

In other news, King Arthur's town (Syracuse) was hit by a tornado a few days ago. No power, no telephones, no form of communication. So, he won't be posting or responding to any e-mails for a while.

Hopefully, I'll be able to fill in most of the gaps.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-08T18:01:00Z |title=SCunit v1.2 released |author=Moneran |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

What's new:

* new fields added, more stuff to edit
* can now save as
* bug fixes

Download it! [SCunit v1.2](scunit12.zip) (299kb)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-08T17:57:00Z |title=New Release: Stardraft 2.0C |author=Moneran |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

It beats me why Merlin called this StarDraft v2.0c... seems like a maintenance release.  Guess what? It's got compression - which compresses maps even better than MPQ's do. I tried extracting my scenario.chk from Total Mayhem and recompressing it with the CWAD compresser - and it was 39k. Just over HALF the size of the original map (74k). One word -- wow.

(In addition, all currently known bugs have been fixed)

* [Program Files](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224208/http://camelot.warzone.com/software/stardraft.zip) (366kb)
* [Borland C++ Builder DLLS](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224208/http://camelot.warzone.com/software/starsys.zip) (809kb, download this if Stardraft complains that it's missing some DLL files)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-07T04:15:00Z |title=Awesome new Starcraft map |author=Moneran |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just finished making a hot new Starcraft map - Total Mayhem. It's an original map. Different from anything you've seen before. But it's a hell of a lot of fun.

In the map, you pick four squadrons of units (of any race) and battle it out with six other players in a gorey ffa. Every time a squadron dies, you can replace it with a new group of units.

All the units have been tweaked for optimum balance. Every unit is strong at its task, but can be easily countered if you pick the right units.

Thanks to [SCform](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224208/http://camelot.warzone.com/SCForm/), I was able to hex edit this map for optimum efficiency. It's got dozens of sounds, nine players, and buildings from all three races on one team. But guess what? It's only 74k.

Good luck. I hope you have as much fun as my friends and I have had on this map.

Download it! [Total Mayhem (74k)](mayhem.zip)

(Unzip into your Starcraft\Maps directory. Play on Battle.net with lots of other players on USE MAP SETTINGS only)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-06T03:54:00Z |title=StarDraft v2.0 Announcement |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I just want to make one thing about StarDraft v2.0 perfectly clear: Directory loading is not supported in this version. This version will *only* load replacements contained in a CWAD file. Any other types of replacements will not work, unless you keep StarDraft 1.0 as well.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-04T01:59:00Z |title=Another SC Format update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Following last night's (or this morning's) upload of the initial version of the Weapons.dat specs, I have now refined that specs sheet to include references to the various variable values that you might need to use with Arsenal, etc. You can see the update at [SCForm](SCForm/), or [here directly](SCForm/WEAPForm.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-03T05:00:00Z |title=Weapons.dat specs, label values list, and Arsenal Update uploaded. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I have just finished polishing up the first incarnation of the public version of the weapons.dat file specifications. More details will be added later as I get the time to create values lists, etc. I have uploaded a 90% complete label value list as well, in order to assist you with using Arsenal. I'll upload an Icon one probably tomorrow or Friday. Both of these items are available on the [SCForm](SCForm/) page.

I have also uploaded a new weapon name identification file, which corrects some weapons previously misidentified, and adds a few newly identified ones. You can get that [here](software/names.txt). Simply replace your older names.txt file with this one.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-09-03T04:07:00Z |title=Starcraft Infoceptor World of Customs update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-30T03:26:00Z |title=GRP Converter, MPQ View, and Units palette Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After many requests to upload a graphic converter utility, I have done so. However, please understand that this utility was not made by Camelot Systems, but instead Alex Cech, who will not be giving Tech Support for it. For all tech support, please refer to the [WarCraft Technical FAQ](WTF/).

* [Download GFX Converter](software/gfxconv.zip) (136kb)

Also, because some people had trouble finding an MPQ Extractor, I have uploaded MPQ View to the site. It is written by Sean Mims, now a member of the Camelot Systems staff. A new special version is being made by Sean and Merlin for StarDraft's use. Support will be provided for this utility by contacting [King Arthur](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com).

* [Download MPQ View](software/mpqview.zip) (179kb)

Finally, since many people are going to be using the GFX/GRP converter, you will need a unit palette to go with it. This particular palette is in WarCraft II/StarCraft format. It was not made by me, but rather by the author of CEL/GRP Viewer.

* [StarCraft Unit Palette](software/sc_unit.pal) (1k)
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-29T03:56:00Z |title=New mod file uploaded! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, I've uploaded a modification file now that does pretty much all of the stuff that you have seen in the screenshots that I released yesterday. Basically all the changes have been made to the marine now, just so you know what to look for. You can download it [here](unitmod.zip). (Zip file) For instructions on how to use it, please refer to the [StarDraft FAQ](sdfaq.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-28T03:46:00Z |title=10 New StarDraft Screenshots! - Total of 29 now! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

These are really cool. The first two are from games that users of StarDraft sent me. The last 8 are from stuff I was fooling around with today. :) (You can get the other 19 from [here](images/).

* [Ghost Firebats 1](images/ghostbat.jpg)
* [Ghost Firebats 2](images/ghostbat2.jpg)
* [These marines must be **really** close.](images/closemarines.jpg)
* [This group of marines is permanently cloaked.](images/cloakedmarines.jpg)
* [This marine is a god-like figure.](images/godlikemarine.jpg) - I.e. it's walking on WATER???
* [Good thing this marine is invulnerable - otherwise that nuke would kill him. :)](images/invulnmarine.jpg)
* [Oh no! The barracks is going to squash all those poor marines!](images/marinecrush.jpg)
* [Fortunately this little marine got away and is now being **repaired**](images/marinerepair.jpg)
* [This marine must really like heights - he's climbed the Protoss Temple!](images/mountainmarine.jpg)
* [Finally, this last marine just can't seem to fit in the bunker... so he's sitting on top of it.](images/onbunker.jpg)

I should be releasing a patch tomorrow that allows you all to do what I have done with that marine. :) Useful little guy, ain't he? Actually, it balances out cause he can't kill anything anymore with that Lockdown Autogun he has.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-28T03:35:00Z |title=StarDraft FAQ updated with 30 new questions and answers! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I have just uploaded the newest version of the StarDraft FAQ to the site (and have updated the version in the StarDraft.zip file as well). The new FAQ now contains **60** of the most commonly asked questions and their answers. You can get the new FAQ [here](sdfaq.htm). (48kb)

A note about the tech support: so far I have been (I hope) pretty quick in emailing people back with responses. However, due to the large number of emails I get per day (now over 100 - some in praise of StarDraft, some with problems), I have had to standardize some of the responses to point to the FAQ - as I said I might do in the release notes.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-28T03:29:00Z |title=StarDraft Zip file updated. - License agreement added and FAQ updated. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After consulting with Merlin, we have decided to update the license agreement to forbid any reverse engineering, decompiling, disassembling of StarDraft. For more details, please read the Release Notes (updated) that come with the updated StarDraft.zip file. The new StarDraft zip file also includes the newest version of the FAQ.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-27T01:11:00Z |title=Sample StarDraft modification file uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

In response to many requests for a demonstration of what StarDraft really can do on your local computer (instead of all just looking at my screenshots), download [Mixed Weapons](software/weapmod.zip) which gives among other things, by popular demand: the Marine lockdown (no damage, tho). Later tonight or tomorrow I will upload a few other sample modification files. Documentation is available in the zip file for installing and running the modifications. Enjoy! (And send me some screenshots!) Note: if you want to use this modification on Battle.net, that is fine, but your opponent must also be running the same modification, too. (Just have them download it from our site).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-26T22:03:00Z |title=StarDraft now available for download! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Finally, what we've all been waiting for. :) The *FULL* version of StarDraft is now available for download (see link below this news item). On first glance, it will appear little different from the Demo version... in actuality it loads all patched files with no filename or extension restrictions. Blizzard did contact me and let me know that they have no files they want restricted. That does not mean, however, that Blizzard has authorized StarDraft... but at this point, who really cares? We all just want to use it! :) I will be uploading sample modifications as the night goes on - probably within the next couple of hours. You will be able to make changes like I have to .dat files sometime soon. Arsenal, the program that I have written to make the changes, should be available for download sometime soon, probably next week. More details on that at a later date.

* [Download StarDraft!](software/stardraft.zip) (287kb) - Created by [Merlin](mailto:Merlin@warzone.com). Tech Support: [King Arthur](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com)

If you are having trouble downloading StarDraft, our affiliates [StarCraft.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203164849/http://www.starcraft.org/) and [StarCraft:Infinity](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203164849/http://www.scinfinity.com/) should have copies up within the next hour or so for you to download.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-26T16:28:00Z |title=StarDraft FAQ Uploaded. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Because of all the emails I've been getting the last few days with questions about StarDraft, I decided to post a [StarDraft FAQ](sdfaq.htm) to help to answer some of the quesitons that I can remember being asked. Obviously all questions and answers aren't there, so if you find something that isn't on there that you need answered, just drop me an [email](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com) and I will reply with your answer, and update the FAQ as well. The FAQ will be updated in total anonymity of those submitting the questions, so newbies need not be discouraged if their questions sound silly. Now that I think of it, we're all newbies when it comes to StarDraft. 'Cept me and Merlin, and Moneran, thankfully. :) Who knows? Maybe by the time you're done reading it, StarDraft will be out! :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-26T16:01:00Z |title=The BIG day has arrived. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, today's the day that the full release of StarDraft gets uploaded. :) Yipee!!! I bet I'm not half as excited as some of you, though. :) In any case, it should be up sometime later this afternoon for you all to download. If you have trouble getting into the page to download it, shortly after we release it, [StarCraft: Infinity](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203164849/http://www.scinfinity.com/) and  [StarCraft.Org](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203164849/http://www.starcraft.org/), Camelot System affiliates, will have it mirrored on their servers for download.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-24T06:40:00Z |title=12 NEW StarDraft screenshots |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

At the request of several friends of mine, and the growing interest and popularity surrounding StarDraft, I have decided to release 12 new screenshots which demonstrate only a small portion of what StarDraft will ultimately be capable of. Please note that unlikethe screenshots sent in to StarCraft.org by Moneran, these screenshots were taken in single player games against 8 computer opponents with some cheats used to speed up the game. However, you can still see the effect of the editing in these pictures. For a glance at the entire directory, pleasego [here](images/). For descriptions, look below:

* [Firebats with EMP Shockwave](images/firebatemp.jpg)
* [Locked-down Hatchery](images/hatcherylocked.jpg)
* [Hydralisk with Stasis as weapon](images/hydrastasis.jpg)
* [Ultra with no immediately discernable target. :)](images/longrangeultra.jpg) Note also the "Arclite Cannon" icon for its weapon.
* [About 25 marines all shooting lockdown missiles constantly](images/marinelockdown.jpg) - at no engergy requirement :)
* [For some reason, the Goliaths and the Tanks have switched Turrets](images/newturret1.jpg) (Insert Evil Grin Here)
* [Oops. Looks like they switched turrets again. :)](images/newturret2.jpg)
* [Queen attacking with Defiler Plague](images/plaguequeen.jpg)
* [Lots of marines locking down this town](images/seriouslockdown.jpg)
* [Somehow the Dragoons have Tentacles and are attacking this *Battlecruiser*](images/tentaclecruiser.jpg)
* [The Battlecruiser again attacked by tentacles](images/tentaclecruiser2.jpg)
* [This Ultralisk is Irradiate capable.](images/ultrairradiate.jpg)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-24T06:38:00Z |title=Stardraft release date |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin, Moneran and I have been testing out the abilities of StarDraft and changes made to various files over the last several days on battle.net We have come to theconclusion that it is probably safe to release the full version of StarDraft as we have been using it (note: it's not the same as the demo distributed on the site now). The full releasewill most likely have the ability to replace *any* file in the game. To be fair, I will be calling Blizzard tomorrow to let them know of our intent to release it on *Wednesday* of this week.If all goes well, it will be there for you to download sometime on Wednesday. If not, then we will release a new demo with some additional files that you can edit and play around with.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-20T03:57:00Z |title=STARDRAFT IS HERE! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Excuse my enthusiasm. StarDraft is here. Well, in a demo mode anyways. This self-extracting archive download contains the required patcher and DLL file to run StarCraft or StarEdit with changes that you can make to pcx or wav files. Documentation has been provided, as well as a sample (the title screen has been adjusted to reflect our changes and hard work).

* [Download StarDraft Demo (Patch Loader)](software/sddemo.exe)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-19T02:33:00Z |title=StarDraft on the way! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Camelot Systems is pleased to announce the release of a StarCraft Utility you have all been waiting for: StarDraft. Although not finished, it is currently about 50% complete. Development is being carried out by Merlin and [King Arthur](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com). Merlin is currently unavailable to answer questions on the matter, so we ask that you direct all questions / comments / suggestions to [King Arthur](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com). StarDraft will (of course) allow you to import files into the MPQ archives that StarCraft and StarEdit use. In addition, files stored inside the MPQ archive will be editable or at least viewable from inside StarDraft much like WarDraft allowed you to do with the WAR data archives. A release date has not been set for StarDraft, but work is now heavily under way, and a release date should be available sometime soon, as well as Beta-testing information and sign-ups.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-18T00:46:00Z |title=SCForm Updated - CHK Specs |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks to BeeWarlock and Sean Mims, the CHK (Uncompressed SCM) Specs have now been updated with more information about the UNIT and UNIS sections.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-17T21:00:00Z |title=GifWarViewer released. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Camelot Systems and StarCraft:Infinity are proud to announce the beta release of GifWarViewer. Dioxide's original idea for the programconvinced me (King Arthur) upon seeing the program, to make a few improvements upon it. :) You can download it from Camelot Systems now, and eventually (whenDioxide gets back) from StarCraft:Infinity. This beta version requires that you have Microsoft Visual Basic 5.0 runtime libraries installed, as well as InternetExplorer, v4.0. A full release should be out by the end of the week with the required runtime libraries. Support for Netscape Navigator should follow shortly.

* Download [GifWarViewer](software/gifwar.zip) (Beta - Program Only)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-17T22:57:00Z |title=New version of Trigger Compiler |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Moneran has just released the new version of his Trigger Compiler. This is one cool utility. Actually, I take that back. It isn't a utility anymore.It's now part of the Windows 95 interface. All you have to do is open up explorer, select a trigger or a text file, right click on it, and it will present you withthe option of converting to the opposite format. Couldn't get any easier than that! :) Download it here:

* Download [Trigger Compiler](software/trig.exe) (Self-extracting Archive)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-17T22:53:00Z |title=Apology |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I owe you (my audience/fans, etc) an apology. Merlin went on vacation last saturday for 8 days, and I figured that I'd try to still get some work done. I did, but I never uploadedanything to the site during all that time. I guess I was just burned out. I have a real job, you see, as a computer programmer, and I was just tired of doing the same thing at home as I didat work. Well, I've dealt with that problem now, so hopefully you won't have any more un-announced vacations. :) Sorry to have let you down. Merlin should be back into the swing of thingslater tonight or tomorrow. Keep your browsers tuned to Camelot Systems for a surprise you won't want to miss coming up in a week or two. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-06T03:04:00Z |title=PalEdit updated (again) |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks to Merlin's hacking, I was able to determine that there is another Starcraft palette format out there - the *.wpe files in the MPQ archive. These are for tilesets. I have now added them as a valid auto-load/save format for you to play around with. Download below:

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-06T00:49:00Z |title=PalEdit bug fixed. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Dioxide, one of the first people to download PalEdit, kindly let me knowk that there was a bug in what he otherwise called a program that "rocks" and is "awesome." This from one of 2 kings of animated Gifs. :) Anyways, the bug arose when you loaded a palette, and then tried to save it in the same format... I've fixed that now, and you can download the most current version from either the link in this news post, or the one below. :) If you downloaded the System file version the first time, you only need to download the program this time. Sorry about the bug. :(

* Download [PalEdit](software/paledit2.zip) (Program and System Files)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-05T18:56:00Z |title=PalEdit uploaded! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After about a week of requests for me to add editing features to PalConverter (which some people couldn't figure out how to use), I have vastly improved the interface for PalConverter II (or PalEdit, as I call it). It now automatically detects the palette format, and loads it for you to see on-screen just like the Palette editor that Paintshop Pro has. You can copy and paste colors, edit existing ones, have it display RGB triplet and Hexadecimal format for the color values. In short, just about everything that I always wanted to have in a palette editor. :) Enjoy.

* Download [PalEdit](software/paledit.zip) (Program Only)
* Download [PalEdit](software/paledit2.zip) (Program and System Files)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-08-03T02:33:00Z |title=Trigger Compiler Source released. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Moneran has asked me to post the following letter that he has written in response to the feedback he has recieved for the [Trigger Compiler](software/).

> I released the Starcraft Trigger Compiler a few days ago... and I've beenamazed by the feedback I've been getting.  Already, I've recieved patchessolving bugs and issues with it.
>
> So I decided to make it official -- the Starcraft Trigger Compiler is"open-source".  Go ahead, download the source at [http://www.camsys.org/software/trigcomp.zip](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203164849/http://www.camsys.org/software/trigcomp.zip).  It's written in Perl --the most popular language used for CGI-Scripts.
>
> It's got possibilities -- from running on a web server to turning into afull-fledged programming language with variables, while loops, notcommands -- you name it.
>
> If the Trigger Compiler advances enough, perhaps Blizzard will include itwith the Brood Wars Expansion.
>
>Have fun editing the source -- the more people who work on the triggercompiler, the greater the possibilities.  If you have any suggestions,comments, or bug reports, e-mail me at hellcat5@mindless.com.
>
>Thanks,
> Moneran
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-07-22T23:53:00Z |title=PalConverter 1.0 Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The other day I sat down and decided to start messing with Palette files, in hopes of correcting the various StarCraft palettes out there that were all converting GRP files to strange-looking colors... Unfortunately, I didn't have a graphical interface to do this. However, I did remember that Paintshop Pro and Paintbrush both have fairly decent Palette Editing methods... so I got to thinking... maybe I should create a utility that converts palette formats. And that's what I ended up doing... :) PalConverter 1.0 is probably the simplest program to use, and shortest SC program I have done yet (actually, the only SC program). There's almost nothing more to this program than common dialog boxes and 3 radio boxes for you to select the format of the palettes... that's it. Simple stuff, really. Anyways, it supports **6** different types of conversions:

* JASC (Paintshop Pro) Palette Files-StarCraft Palette Files
* Microsoft Palette Files-StarCraft Palette Files
* JASC (Paintshop Pro) Palette Files-Microsoft Palette Files
* Microsoft Palette Files-JASC (Paintshop Pro) Palette Files
* StarCraft Palette Files-JASC (Paintshop Pro) Palette Files
* StarCraft Palette Files-Microsoft Palette Files

If I've left out any important palette formats, be sure to let me know. Actually, drop me an email [here](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com?subject=Palettes) and attatch the Palette file to the email. Enjoy!

* [Download PalConverter 1.0 (With MSVB 5.0 System Files)](software/palconverter1.zip) - 1.5Mb
* [Download PalConverter 1.0 (Program Only)](software/palconverter2.zip) - 12Kb
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-07-19T17:57:00Z |title=StarCraft GRP (Unit Graphics) Specs uploaded. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

My apologies to Klint. I forgot to upload his GFX specs sheet. You can now see it [here](SCUMFORM/GFXForm.htm). A note about GFX... GFX and GRP files are identical in format. So what he's outlined in his specs sheet applies to both WarCraft GFX files and StarCraft GRP files. You can decompress/convert extracted GRP files into bitmaps using WarDraft's Graphics Converter and the proper palette file. (Which I do not have, unfortunately).
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-08T20:28:00Z |title=SCuM Format RELEASED!!! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I decided after a few weeks of mulling over whether to release the SCuM format/specs that I had found, or to keep them hidden until a utility had been created for it, that I will be releasing the specs now, while the "appetite" for them is still out there. Camelot Systems will still be developing its own editor for the SCuMs, but I wanted to get the format out and to the public before we worked on an editor so that you all could know what it is we are capable of.

The specs are by no means complete. I still have work to do on them, and I will be finishing as much of it as I'm able to do.

Get the **[SCuM Format/Specs Sheet](SCUMForm.htm)**!!!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-07T03:40:00Z |title=Software, Feedback and MAGE pages updated. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Turalyon and I have gone through today and updated the [Software](software.htm) page with the new utilities for both StarCraft and WarCraft II, uploaded a form-mail section to the now fully functional [Feedback](feedback.htm) page which has all of our addresses on it for your convenience, and we updated the MAGE page with many new user-suggestions. Be sure to check them out [here](/web/20000204152449/http://camelot.warzone.com/MAGE)!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-07T03:39:00Z |title=Affiliate notice: Webmasters please read. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Over the last several weeks, I've gotten many requests to join and/or affiliate with other StarCraft or game sites due to the large quantity of software and information that Camelot Systems has been producing. Let me make one thing very clear: while we appreciate these offers very much, and are flattered that you would like us to join your page, we are quite happy right now where we are, and are currently already going to be affiliated with a new StarCraft site that will be debuting shortly. I cannot release the name of this site until the page is complete.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-06T03:27:00Z |title=MAGE progress update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin has now completed the first version of the S++ compiler. Work will now commence on the Virtual Machine that will link the binary-compiled files. There is now also a progress chart on the MAGE page so that you can see where we currently are in the scheme of things.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-06T03:17:00Z |title=FunCraft relocated |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

In order to trim down the size of our menu bar on the left, and to help reorganize things a little more efficiently, we have moved the FunCraft page to the "[Hosted Sites](hosted.htm)" page. Just wanted to avert any email complaints about where it was. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-06T03:14:00Z |title=WarCraft Utility Mac Conversion Underway |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Jonatan Uppstrom (Hanland) will be porting SPUD Farmer, PUDDraft, and/or WarHack to the Macintosh as soon as Cryo is able to completely debug SPUD Farmer and finish the PUD Drawing code, which should be some time soon.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-06T03:12:00Z |title=Work begun on new SCuM Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Scorpion, author of War2Unit, has begun work on a new SCuM Editor which will use the technical information that I have found about the SCuM format. Sir Gawain is now researching the MPQ format in hopes of arriving at a decompression/compression algorithm that we can use.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-06T03:10:00Z |title=SC Graphics format 90% dissected |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Our new employee, Klint Bicknell, has managed to decipher about 90% of the graphics file format so far. All that remains is basically value checking/referencing, etc, and then we should be able to make a graphics conversion utility.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-04T20:04:00Z |title=StarCraft Dissection continues... |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Work has begun to decipher the encryption key used in the MPQ Compression of the StarCraft Data files. With any luck, if we can find out what format of compression Blizzard has used to compress the data files, we will be able to write a data file Import/Export utility. To prepare for this utility, we are now researching the format of the many different types of files currently stored in the Data files, like the animation group files, etc. If you have previous hacking experience with MPQ or LZSS-type compression and would like to help, please [let me know](mailto:KingArthur@hotmail.com?Subject=MPQ) ASAP.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-02T03:08:00Z |title=StarCraft SCuM Format further explored. |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As of this post, I am 100% complete with both the Doodad and Unit lists for StarCraft User maps... What this means is that I have identified the values that correspond to the units and doodads when used in the SCuMs. The format of the SCuM itself is now about 65-75% complete. Once I have finished its dissection to my satisfaction, I will either be posting the format for public viewing, or take that information and write a few utilities based on it. Do *not* write to me asking for this information unless you have a *very* good reasons to. Please be patient with me. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-06-02T03:08:00Z |title=WarCraft and StarCraft Graphics Formats Identical |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The last few days I have been dissecting the format of the SCuMs, and while I was taking a break, a friend of mine made a comment that got me thinking... what if we wanted to port WarCraft over to StarCraft's engine? I thought it would be a daunting task, seeing as how the graphics for StarCraft look so much more complicated. However, I decided to do some checking into the format before I made a final decision, and to my amazement I discovered that they were indeed identical in format.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=May 1998}}

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-31T04:21:00Z |title=Editor's SCuM's Uploaded |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I've just uploaded a zip file with 5 Scums in it... They are *not* meant to be playable SCuMs, but SCuMs used for editing/hacking purposes only. These 5 SCuMs display all of the tiles for each of their respective tilesets. Currently, these can really only be used for reference only, but eventually they will be crucial to creating an advanced SCuM editing utility. You can download them [here](edmaps.zip) (486kb).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-29T03:41:00Z |title=StarCraft SCuM Specs to be published soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm putting off work on [WTF](WTF/) for the next several days to investigate the format of the StarCraft User Map files. Thankfully they are similar in nature to their relatives - PUD files. I will be posting information as soon as I think that I have a comprehensive outline of the file... for both versions of SCuMs (57 - Beta? and 59 - Current).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-28T04:34:00Z |title=StarCraft has room for 12 players |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

You read correctly. After a night of intense HEX editing, I finally came to the conclusion that StarCraft has room in it for 12 players. However, Battle.net won't let you play with more than 8. So what would the other players be used for? Well, one of them I suspect "owns" the resources, while the other three are just fillers... but I think that with a little editing, you could enable them as computer rescuable characters... just like I'm doing in SPUDs. :) MUAHAHAHAHAH! I'll be posting more info as I am able to.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-27T21:51:00Z |title=Tomorrow may go by without news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Turalyon is working on the form-mail section for the feedback section today, but he may not be able to do updates for the next couple of days: his ISP has been giving him some trouble, and he is also trying to work on his Total Annihilation site and get some new units up. I will try to make a few updates tomorrow, but I do have a big Government paper due that I have to work on, so I may not get to it. Don't despair, however! I'll be back to redouble my efforts on friday afternoon!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-27T21:49:00Z |title=Dioxide designing Camelot Systems Logo |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Dioxide of StarCraft: Infinity will be making Camelot Systems a logo (finally :) He's got the initial version up [here](camlogo.jpg), and should have the final edition completed soon. Let [me](mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com) know what you think of it.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-27T00:31:00Z |title=SCMapCentral SCuM Uploads |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sometime later tonight (with any luck), we will be getting an upload script for [SCMapCentral](SCMapCentral/) that will allow users to upload their own SCuMs to SCMapCentral for Moritz to review and post on the page. This script will also be available as a link on the following sites:

* [StarCraft Infinity](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://starcraft.warzone.com/)
* [The World of StarCraft](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://twos.starleague.net/)

If you are interested in placing a link to our SCuM upload page, please contact [King Arthur]mailto:KingArthur@warzone.com?Subject=SCuM Uploads) and I will do my best to get you set up with a link and the proper code. The main reason that these two very popular SC sites are going to be supporting our link on their page is because they have no SCuM archives of their own, and when I asked if they would like to be able to have their users still submit SCuMs (which were previously deleted) they can now direct them to where they can be of some use. Thanks to SC Infinity and TWOS for your support!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-27T00:27:00Z |title=New "Hosted Sites" Page |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Please do not apply for us to host your site - decisions to host sites are always made by King Arthur, and he generally hand-picks only the sites that he feels are worthy/qualified to be hosted here.

GameChat will be hosted by Camelot Systems at the following URL (when it has been uploaded): [http://www.camsys.org/GameChat](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://www.camsys.org/GameChat/). After about 2 hours, we managed to get everything re-uploaded with a few extra maps as well. SCMapCentral will be striving to post a SCuM/day, if we can get the SCuMs into SCMapCentral. Right now we have a few dedicated SCuM authors who are working on them, but SCMapCentral would like to get more people on board to help out, and produce a great-quality product at the same time. Both campaign authors, trigger-specialists, and regular authors are welcome as long as they are willing to make a SCuM/week commitment. Thanks for your support for such a high-quality SCuM archive.

{{begin-newsitem |time=1998-05-22T22:02:00Z |title=WarCraft Adventures: Lord of the Clans Canceled |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Today, [OGR (Online Gaming Review)](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://www.ogr.com/) announced that Blizzard plans to cancel work on WarCraft Adventures. If you ask me, I think that's a good move. It would probably never have made as much money as they wanted, and might have even ruined the chances for work on WarCraft III to commence. The full text of the story (and press release from Blizzard) below (source: [OGR's NewsNet](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://www.ogr.com/news.html) and [The World of StarCraft](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://twos.starleague.net/))

"After much internal discussion, Blizzard Entertainment decided today to stop development of Warcraft Adventures: Lord of the Clans. Following an intensive review of the game, the company determined that given the game's current status and the rapidly changing technology of the industry, it would not be possible to complete development of the game within a reasonable time frame.

While this has been an extremely difficult decision, we firmly believe that we cannot release a title that does not meet the exceptionally high standards that both we and our customers expect from the Blizzard label." [4:20 PM ET]
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-11 |title=SCMapCentral and SPUD Zone |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I plan to have SCMapCentral and SPUD Zone up and online by May 20th, possibly with a preview beforehand.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-11 |title=Wanted: Your SCuM's |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Wanted: Your SCuM's. No real requirements, but we'd like to concentrate on triggers, campaign-format SCuMs, and new types of games (like paintball). These will be posted on SCMapCentral when it goes online with full credit, of course. Please send them here: [King Arthur](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com). I request that you *please* put them into a compressed ZIP archive before emailing them to me. Thanks for your contributions! (And thanks, Klint for pointing out a previous error on this news item! :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-11 |title=SCMapCentral |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Along with the SCuM's that we're being sent, we are also having a few made for us in house. If you'd like to join the SCMapCentral staff as a SCuMBag, then drop me a line [here](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com). Please include a sample of your work (1 or 2 SCuMs should be fine zipped up) when applying.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-10 |title=New layout |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The new layout is now up ([here](test/))! Let [King Arthur](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com) know what you think of it. Thanks. (A few pages will not work until this layout becomes permanent.

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-10 |title=MAGE information released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

New information has just been posted on [MAGE](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://www.warzone.com/Camelot_Systems/MAGE/). I've also included in there a sample of what the scripting technology may look like when we're done.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-09 |title=MAGE information to be released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Tomorrow I plan to release to the public a TON of information about MAGE, like all the features we have planned, details about the implimentation of the engine, and proposed projects that will utilize this engine.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-09 |title=New Camelot Systems sections |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I have plans to start two new major sections for Camelot Systems: 1) SPUD Zone will now be moving from its former location at http://www.rtshq.com/spuds/ to our page, and 2) I will be creating SCMapCentral, which is intended to collect, distribute, and provide a forum for SCuMs with advanced triggers, AIs, etc. The ideas that are created there will help to develop the next RTS that Nilferax Labs will be undertaking, a story that will hopefully introduce a new realm and genre of RTS gaming to the fold. Check back soon for details. I plan to have both up and running by May 20th. I will not be the webmaster of these new sites. Checkmate will be the only one running them, and a few people (one from Nilferax Labs) will be designing SCuMs for you to download. The SPUD Zone will be open for business once the SPUDs are able to work correctly in a larger size than 128x128 maps for WarCraft II.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-09 |title=Redesigning WarCraft II String Editor |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm redesigning the WarCraft II String Editor (available from the [Software Page](software.htm)) to allow the new strings that you create to be used by units in specific Exe's. Currently, WarHack does not support the use of string numbers higher than 479 (the number of strings in the name entry), so I this feature will be added to the second version of the String Editor, making it a much more useful utility.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-07 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SPUD Farmer 2.0 News: Fixed the problem with the slow loading... now it doesn't take much longer than PudDraft to load a 128x128 map. Only problem is now the map isn't drawing correctly! I can't win! ARGH!!!! Units are all done and look good, and everything's set to go once the puds are drawn right. The memory problem is also solved with the GDI thing, so I only have the one thing to worry about now.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-07 |title=MAGE News |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

MAGE News: I had a suggestion today for MAGE that we allow terrain to be modifiable during the game (i.e. Build bridges, dig trenches/moats, etc), which would seem to expand on the natural effects that occur on the map.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-05 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta 5 improved loading speeds |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta 5 has improved in its loading speed now, and with all the graphics done, one would think I'd release it. Well, unfortunately I can't. There appears to be a bug innate to the Windows structure itself that will prevent me from displaying the maps the way I want to with the units there on top of them. In any case, the 16-bit GDI in Windows is preventing me from finishing SPUD Farmer version 2.0 right now. Fortunately, I've already got an alternate method in the works to counteract this unforseen problem. Make sure that you drop by [Microsoft](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://www.microsoft.com/) and throw a virtual pie in their face for me. Much obliged, and hopefully this situation will be fixed in the next day or two. Depends on whatever else I'm required to do.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-05 |title=MAGE suggestions |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I recieved a few emails today that addressed some issues in [MAGE](https://web.archive.org/web/20000919224230/http://www.warzone.com/Camelot_Systems/MAGE/). Essentially, they were suggestions for the engine itself, and I wanted to let you know what they were:

* Volcanoes (and other natural events) exist on the map, which either randomly spew out ash (covering the map or the fog of war) or destroying certain areas of the map to growth or even the units upon it. In the case of actual land destruction, regrowth will of course take place, and eruptions will either be randomly or (expensive) spell triggered.
* Larger maps than currently possible with existing engines (like WarCraft II and StarCraft). We have the potential to allow maps as large as your imagination, hard drive, and ram will restrict you to.
* Spherical maps are also in the works (i.e. a 3d-sphere where battle takes place... a gradually curved surface like that of the earth, only on a smaller visual scale. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-04 |title=No StarCraft editors |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

One more bit of bad news: Contrary to previous assumptions that I made about SC (before I actually bought the game and the StarHack situation arose), we cannot and will not be releasing any editor for StarCraft. It is strictly forbidden by Blizzard's License agreement, and we absolutely do not want to get in trouble with them or the law for doing so... However, we plan on making whatever progress we can, but we won't be releasing either our information or any utilites to the public. Perhaps they may be released by Blizzard, however, if things go well with them and they decide to make us an official offshoot of Blizz. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-04 |title=MAGE info posted |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Also, I reposted some earlier material and information on MAGE on the page of the same name, accessable from the menubar on the left.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-04 |title=Work continues on SPUD Farmer Beta 5 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

King Arthur is still working on SPUD Farmer Beta 5. He has not released it because the load time for the PUD files is incredibly long, and he is attempting to fix that problem before releasing it for Beta Testing. At this point, I regret to inform you that he knows not when it will be completed, but keep checking back until it is. I would estimate that it will probably take at least another week if not two. Thanks for your patience and understanding.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-04 |title=SPUD Farmer graphics completed |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The good news about SPUD Farmer Beta 5 is that the graphics were just completed late tonight, as I'm writing this, so there will definately be Forest graphics and tilesets in the next beta! :) Now if only King Arthur can get it to load quickly! :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-02 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 updates |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Graphical interface of SPUD Farmer 2.0 *might* not make it into Beta 5, as they are taking longer than expected. The rest of the Beta should be released (with save code in it this time. :), and we'll see how things go. With any luck, I will throw in a password or something to use the map-loading part once I can upload it... That will probably be a better solution to the problem for now... it'll let you all edit the SPUDs and save them while enabling me to finish up converting the graphics of the units... (the forest terrain is done, and the only terrain to be in the beta test... the others will have to wait). Also, SPUD Farmer's loading of strings from the data file will only support string archives with uncompressed #1 entries. (For example, Rituals of Rebirth compresses their string entry #1 (the names one), and I cannot read the compression format yet. For now, the bugfix is simply to extract the entry with WarDraft and re-import it without compression. The same goes for other compressed entries from other TC's.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-02 |title=GUI aspects of the game |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin is just about finished with the S++ compiler, which will be used for creating the Graphical User Interface and the regular graphics of the game. Sir Robin is working on the Graphic editing software portion (written in JAVA, with a stub for running it in Windows/Dos/Linux).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-01 |title=StarCraft utilities upcoming |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Once SPUD Farmer 2.0 has been completed and is out of beta testing, King Arthur will begin to work on utilities for StarCraft that will probably resemble what WarDraft was for WarCraft II.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-05-01 |title=Work continues on SaveHack |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Work also continues on SaveHack, thanks to Sir Gawain's efforts. It will be capable of performing just about all of the modifications that SPUD Farmer and PUD Draft are capable of in a WarCraft II saved game. This will make it easier to create custom screenshots at your will, large preplanned battles, etc :)

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-29 |title=SPUD Farmer Beta 5 progressing nicely |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SPUD Farmer Beta 5 is progressing nicely. I've got the visual loading of the PUD proceeding for the most part without a hitch, and the unit loading also needs minor tweaking before I am done. SPUD Farmer Beta 5 will only be using graphics from the Forest tileset, to make my life easier and to keep the size of the download to a minimum. Unfortunately, without more information on the format of the tilesets or the graphics, I will have to distribute bitmap files with the images already stored in them... Hopefully I'll be able to get that feature working for the full release. Also, I've got the string-loading feature of the WarCraft II String Editor ported over to SPUD Farmer, so it will now load the unit names, AI names, and upgrade names from strdat.war. The remaining names aren't included in any data entry that I can find, and so I'm stuffing them into an ini file (there are only around 30 of them left).

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-28 |title=MAGE Features |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Just to give you a taste of what MAGE has to offer, a list of currently planned features is listed below:

* The ability to design games from three main genres: StarCraft-type games, Diablo-type games, and RPG-type games. This was done because the Reptiles TC, version 2.0 requires features from all three genres.
* The ability to customize pre-provided units, add your own from templates, or custom-design your own.
* A scripting language (tentatively titled S++) will allow the unit programming, GUI programming, and other programming not accessable via the planned Map Editor (to be included in the engine's GUI)
* Custom compression of graphics and strings in new formats that can be read inside of data files, or converted outside.
* Extraction and importion of entries in the data archives will also be supported.
* Forces of nature: Volcanoes erupting, earthquakes, rainstorms, snow, etc are all planned, along with the ability to write spells and/or commands to inflict these natural disasters/occurences.
* Individual units will have the option (via a user-selection box) of either improving through experience or not.
* In fact, I suppose I should now mention that almost every main feature planned will be optional within the engine and the game itself, so that the game becomes more user-customizeable
* Full multiplayer support (Via IPX Networks, modems, direct connections, etc)
* Advanced map properties: Waypoint embedding, 3d terrain, 8 planned levels of terrain, LOS (Line of Sight), True light-sourcing, resource replenishing (i.e. forests regrow when it rains, etc).
* Advanced unit properties: Teleporting, multi-terrain transports, self-invoked morphing
* And of course, what everyone cares about: custom cheat codes... :)

See something missing from our feature list that you'd like us to include? Drop me a line here: [King Arthur](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-27 |title=Clarification |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

After I was informed of a post at [StarCraft Universe](https://web.archive.org/web/20000203102802/http://starcraft.gamestats.com/) which refers to a bit of news taken from this site, I feel I need to clarify several things for him, and for all of our new readers who have undoubtedly followed his link in interest.First of all, Camelot Systems is currently producing a number of utilities centered around the WarCraft genre/universe, including new versions of WarDraft (a WarCraft Archive Editor), WarHack (an Exe Editor), and PudDraft (a map editor), along with developing MAGE (Millenium Advanced Gaming Engine), which is intended to be one of the most comprehensive, advanced, and genre-spanning engines ever divised.MAGE also will be fully customizeable for many different projects, and its first major application (and raison d'etre) will be the 2nd version of the WarCraft II Reptiles Total Conversion, which is geared to closely follow the release of Reptiles 1.0. As co-president of Camelot Systems and co-leader of Nilferax Labs (Reptile's producers), I am currently in contact with Blizzard Entertainment about thepossibility of having them authorize and/or distribute version 2.0. Whether it will become a seperate game itself, or remain a TC/Expansion to WarCraft II, we intend to see this project through with Blizzard's cooperation, due to the many thousands of hours spent on this project since its conception a year or two ago. Although many things have changed (including leadership) since it was first divised,one goal remains clear: Nilferax Labs (and Camelot Systems) wish to provide end-users with a gaming experience they will never forget, and will help to carry the torch from one WarCraft game to another (originally Reptiles was intended to be a TC for people to play while waiting for WarCraft III). This brings up another fact that I should clear up... As of right now, I know of no plans to make this gameWarCraft III, however we do plan to try to get it published through Blizzard, and that may mean that it becomes yet another jewel in WarCraft's crown, helping to elaborate and enrich the story, saga, and genre. Thanks for your continued interest, and feel free to drop me a line (as always) with any suggestions/comments/questions you may have. - [King Arthur](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-26 |title=String Editors and Compressed Entries |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I suppose that I should reiterate that neither String Editor/Hacker that we've created so far will read compressed string entries. So if your name entry is compressed, you will have to decompress it to be able to use these advanced utilites to their full potential. To decompress the file, export the entry (#1 in most cases) from Strdat.war (or whatever you've renamed it) using WarDraft. Choose "Decompress before saving," and then save the entry. Import that same entry back into the same slot without compression, and the editor shouldwork without any problems. WCStred (King Arthur's utility) does not raise an error if the entry is compressed in this version. Unfortunately, the editor does not load the entry at all. It is unknown what errors (if any) may occur in String Hacker (Sir Galahad's utility) at this time.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-25 |title=Negotiations with Blizzard |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

In-depth negotiations with Blizzard Entertainment are now under-way to hopefully authorize Nilferax Lab's Reptiles TC, and with the help of Camelot Systems, it will become a new game and successor to WarCraft. So, this means that as of right now, all information regarding this project will be filtered somewhat to avoid thenasty possibiltiy of releasing sensitive information regarding the new game or engine. We will still, of course, work on both, but the depth of the information we can release will be reduced to protect both copyrights and our ideas.
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-22 |title=MAGE and future projects |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We have plans to work on two more games following the release of the MAGE engine. One will involve an upgrade/enhancement to MAGE for a new RTS. The other will involve modular programming that will see sections of a program released to the public as they're each completed. Hopefully this will be a game based on Star Wars, or a Star Wars type environment. Gameplay will consist of:

* The unification of managed-turn-based battles and plan formulation
* Real-time combat modes in space and on the ground in a wide variety of ships, giving you the opportunity to pilot:
   * Tie Fighters (All Types)
   * X-wings, Y-Wings, E-wings, B-Wings, A-wings, and K-Wings
   * Capital ships including: Corellian Corvettes, Nebulon B-Frigates, Mon Calimari Star Cruisers, and Imperial and Super class Star Destroyers
* Controls will be arranged so that you can command units of fighters, capital ships, and fleets, or individual fighters and capital ships themselves
* Land-based planet fighting is also being planned with AT-STs and AT-ATs.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-19 |title=New version of WarAdd |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin just completed a new version of WarAdd, aimed at making the addition of entries to warcraft archives even simpler. The program is now a visual, mouse-driven DOS program, and requires nocommand-line options that the previous version did. You can obtain it [here](downloads.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-18 |title=New development tools and staff |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Fortunately, we've decided to pursue creating a new set of TC development tools to coincide with and aid in the making of the Reptiles Total Conversion, which is being run by Nilferax Labs, our parent Company. The new tools will include:

* An improved version of WarHack, with both a new WORKING windows interface, and a dos version as well, it is being planned to support ALL versions, as well as the numerous improvements that Aurelius will be implimenting in WarDraft with scripting technology.
* A new version of WarDraft, which will be a visual, mouse-driven dos program, almost identical in all visual and functional aspects to the current incarnation, with additional features of course.
* PudDraft, UDTAhack, SPUD Farmer, and the planned SaveHack will be combined into one all-encompassing Map and UDTA editor with the capabilites of all 4 programs in one easy to use visual map editor.

Work on this new project will be commencing almost immediately, and will be developed concurently with the MAGE project.

To help us to take on these rather challenging tasks, we've recruited a few new people, among them: Darkloak and Pendragon, both graphics artists, Sir Galahad, Sir Gawain, Moritz, and Checkmate to help with tech support and programming, and last but certainly not least, a new main programmer: VTM (formerly of the War2 Command-adding utility fame).
{{end-newsitem}}
