For now, that will be the only way to contact anyone besides {{highlight |text=King Arthur}}.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-13 |title=MAGE info upcoming |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{highlight |text=Merlin}}, {{highlight |text=Brian Sawatzky}}, and {{highlight |text=Aurelius}} should be publishing information about {{highlight |text=MAGE}} sometime soon, and you'll be able to check out all of the planned aspects and features of our new engine which should be implimented in {{highlight |text=Nilferax Labs}}' (our parent company) {{highlight |text=Reptiles TC}}, version 2.0, should {{highlight |text=Blizzard}} give them their permission toutilize the copyrighted {{highlight |text=WarCraft II}} characters and background storyline.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-13 |title=Artists needed |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We are also in need of artists to help out with the creation of 3d-rendered unit graphics, first to test out {{highlight |text=MAGE}}, when completed, and later either for {{highlight |text=Reptiles 2.0}}, for an RPG, or for a 3D shooter that we're developing for sometime in the future.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-11 |title=Code lost |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Unfortunately, {{highlight |text=Merlin}} lost much of the source for all of his utilites and for the game engine, {{highlight |text=MAGE}}, which will set us back a bit. If anyone was sent the source code for any of the programs, please don't hesitate to contact [King Arthur](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com) with more info. Thanks for your help and patience.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-11 |title=Coders needed |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Also, I believe that {{highlight |text=Merlin}} still needs anyone capable of {{highlight |text=C++ sound system}} or {{highlight |text=network/multiplayer programming}} to join up, but if you know other stuff I'm sure that's ok, too. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-10 |title=SaveHack |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{highlight |text=Sir Gawain}} has begun work on a new tool, {{highlight |text=SaveHack}}, which will be based on the {{highlight |text=SPUD Farmer}} interface, and will be capable of many of the same things. A graphical map display is planned as well.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-10 |title=New message board |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Thanks to the efforts of {{highlight |text=Merlin}}, we now have a {{highlight |text=new message board}} that we're testing in-house before we release it publicly.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-10 |title=New page interface |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Watch for a {{highlight |text=new page format and interface}} within the next few days. The site will be getting a new look to prepare for our final destination: http://camelot.nilferax.com/
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-10 |title=New programmers |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We've recently {{highlight |text=added several new programmers}} to our staff. Among them is {{highlight |text=Brian Sawatzky}} from the {{highlight |text=StarWars TC}} and {{highlight |text=MacHelper}}, who both will be helping us in our {{highlight |text=game engine design}}. {{highlight |text=MacHelper}} also may end up porting the gameto the Mac platform when we're finished.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-10 |title=Tool plans |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Once {{highlight |text=King Arthur}} completes {{highlight |text=SPUD Farmer}}, he will begin work on a {{highlight |text=revamped}} (and working, hopefully) {{highlight |text=version of WarHack for Windows}}. {{highlight |text=Merlin}} also plans to {{highlight |text=upgrade the DOS version}} simultaniously to coincide with {{highlight |text=adding support for the new unitsand spells}} that {{highlight |text=Aurelius}} will be adding to the {{highlight |text=existing WarCraft II engine}}.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-10 |title=Company and staff profile soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Check back in a few days for a detailed company and staff profile, explaining our goals and who we are.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-09 |title=Thanks to BetaMax for help |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Many thanks to {{highlight |text=Warzone}}'s {{highlight |text=BetaMax}} for helping out with the site setup. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-09 |title=Completed move |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, the move is more or less complete now. The only thing that remains to be uploaded are several of the download files, and they should be going up sometime tomorrow morning if everything holds. The current addressthat you're now visiting is only a temporary one while we wait for our new domain to be set up. This site's address will redirect your browsers once we have obtained the new domain name. We apologize for the inconvenience, butwe felt that a temporary move was better than a temporary lapse in communication period.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-09 |title=Advances in WarCraft II engine capabilities |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Several advances have been made lately to the capabilities of the WarCraft II engine. Namely, Aurelius, one of the new guys at Camelot Systems, has found a way to add both spells and units (hopefully) to the game. Unfortunately,at this point the details are sketchy, so don't bother to write for more info just yet. This technology will mainly be available for the Reptiles TC to start, and then as it gets to be more stable, we will hopefully be adding those capabilities tosomething like a TCDK (Total Conversion Development Kit) for the other TC's to use.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-09 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta 5 now loading maps |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I have finally gotten the map to load correctly in SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta 5... took me a while to figure out what to do, but it works. Only problem is that it doesn't scroll at all when the map is bigger than 16x16. :( Working on that now. Once that's done, then I should be ready to release Beta 5 to the public, andprobably a week or two after beta testing completes on Beta 5, I'll go ahead and release the full version.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-09 |title=New message board soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We will be getting a new message board as well within the next few days, so we'll be making the transition between our current one (accessable via the nav bar on the left), and the new one that Merlin is programming in CGI. This should enable us to get together a more customized interface for Camelot Systems thanInsideTheWeb currently offers.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-09 |title=Programmers wanted |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Although we now currently have a staff of around 7, we're still looking for any programmers with some Java, C++, or Visual Basic experience to volunteer their services. May I remind those relucant to join that there is the possiblity of getting the new MAGE (Millenium Advanced Gaming Engine) system released or utilizedby either Blizzard or Nilferax Labs in implimenting version 2.0 of their TC. So your work will not go unnoticed, it will in fact be seen by many.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-05 |title=FTP access cut |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm afraid that my current ISP thought that I had moved, and cut off access to the FTP for me, so I apolozige for the delay in updating the page, although it was beyond my control. In any case, SPUD Farmer is still being worked on, and probably won't be done for a little longer... I'm trying to get the map andgraphical editing aspects done properly, like the generic War2 editor has... but that may take some time.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-05 |title=ISP problems |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

InterNIC appears to delight in making us wait for our domain... we may just send you guys to a temporary numerical address until we're officially up... Sorry for the delay in moving.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-01 |title=SPUD Farmer beta 5 info |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, we will have about 5 or 6 new staff members here when we make our move to the new address, which with any luck will be next week sometime, although it could be subject to delay if InterNIC doesn't cooperate. As you can see, SPUD Farmer beta 5 wasn't released when I had planned. I was sick over the weekendand had little time to work on it while nursing the flu... or something equally worse. In any case, have no fear, I'm still working on it as hard as ever. This version will include graphical editing of the pud (finally, you must be saying to yourselves... well, it's not easy at all... :( The hardest part isn't reallythe programming aspect of it just yet... right now it's finding the time and the energy to go thru and to extract all of the tiles for each tileset. The Beta will probably only include the Forest tileset for sake of my sanity. I will probably release the other three tileset gfx (as bitmaps for my program to read)sometime later when everything else is working ok. Merlin's also not here this week to give me pointers, so that's also one reason for the delay. And yes, the Save code will be enabled in the next version if all goes well...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-04-01 |title=Quiet about SPUD Farmer beta 4 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I still have not recieved any news of Beta 4, which surprises me quite a bit since many people have said they downloaded it, but then the conversations ended there... I am not sure if that's a good sign.
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=March 1998}}

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-27 |title=WarAdd is up |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, apparently a few things didn't get uploaded the other day like I thought... WarAdd is now up (the link was broken before), and you can now check out what it's good for, along with screen shots and product descriptions for all of the software that we currently have to offer [here](software.htm),the new software page that didn't get uploaded. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-27 |title=SPUD Farmer Save code nearly finished |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[King Arthur](mailto:king_arthur@rtshq.com) has nearly finished with the Save code, and the new BETA should be available sometime late tomorrow evening, if everything goes well. Certain sections may not save correctly (like the GFX section), but this problem should be solved soon.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-27 |title=StarCraft goes Gold |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, as most of you probably know by now, StarCraft has gone Gold, and as such, the limelight that WarCraft has enjoyed for the past several years will now be very dim indeed. However, I will continue to create new versions of SPUD Farmer until I'm satisfied with the final product, and to supplythe current TC authors (who are still working quite dilligently) with another tool that will be at their disposal.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-27 |title=SPUD Farmer new beta tomorrow |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Since I've recieved no notification of any bugs in the current BETA's code, I'm going to assume that it's bug-free, for now, and will do no further updates to the Open code for the Beta due out tomorrow. Tomorrow's Beta will probably remain in Beta version for about a week, and then go into full releasewhen I feel that it has been tested thoroughly enough.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-25 |title=SPUD Farmer Save code progress |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[King Arthur](mailto:king_arthur@rtshq.com) is now working on the Save code for [SPUD Farmer](software.htm). It should be completed sometime this weekend most likely. Beta testers should try to finish bug-testing the current BETA by Saturday, when I expect to releaseBeta 5, which will include the Save code. Beta 6 will include a minimap representation of the actual PUD, and will support the lack of optional sections in the PUDs like the UDTA, UGRD, and ALOW sections. SPUD Farmer currentlyrequires that UGRD and UDTA sections be present in order to read the PUD properly. ALOW, however, is optional. SPUD Creation should also be completed for BETA 6, as will support for external files for the UGRD, UDTA, and ALOW sections.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-25 |title=New engine gfx conversion done |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The gfx conversion for the NEW game engine has been completed. [Merlin](mailto:merlin@rtshq.com) is now working on the Map editor and Graphical User Interface. The Map editor will be included inside the game engine's interface, unlike War2's
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-25 |title=New location soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Within the next few days we will be moving to: [http://camelot.nilferax.com](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204231350/http://camelot.nilferax.com/). There will be a re-directing page up for about two weeks after we move hopefully.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-22 |title=SPUD Farmer BETA 4! |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

NEW [SPUD Farmer BETA 4!](downloads.htm)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-22 |title=New Software page |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Uploaded new Software page. Check it out. Supposed to be a supplement to the Downloads page, listing all of the software that we have made.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-22 |title=New tool by Merlin |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[Merlin](mailto:merlin@rtshq.com)'s just uploaded a new utility to the [Downloads](downloads.htm) page. It allows the addition of entries to data files.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-21 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 BETA forum and mailing list |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

For those of you who are BETA testers, to get access to the beta forum, and to join my informal mailing list, you'll have to drop me an email too.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-21 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 BETA testing |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

If you wish to become a BETA tester, please give me an email: [King Arthur](mailto:king_arthur@rtshq.com)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-21 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 BETA 3 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Uploaded BETA 3... usual spot.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-20 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 Second Beta |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

BETA testers: The second Beta is now out. Get it on the [Downloads](downloads.htm) page.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-19 |title=Wanted: Staff |author=Merlin |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Wanted: GFX artists for our web site, programmers to help with software, and a webmaster with lots of time... contact [King Arthur](mailto:king_arthur@rtshq.com) for details.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-18 |title=Note to SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta testers |author=Merlin |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Important notice for Beta testers: you must place the spud.ini file in "C:\War2\" otherwise you will not be able to load the editor. This will be fixed in future versions.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-18 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta available |author=Merlin |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Check the [Downloads](downloads.htm) page for the SPUD Farmer 2.0 BETA test.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-17 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 Beta soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok... obviously I have been on vacation this last week+... I apologize for not giving you guys advance notice, but it couldn't be helped. In any case, I'm back now and should be updating more or less regularly...at least if I can spare the time. SPUD Farmer 2.0 should be available for BETA testing sometime by the end of the week (I have friday off to work on it. :) so contact me if you want to help out...An important note: the beta version will NOT have the save code implimented (on purpose - it's not quite finished yet), but as the open code is all working ok for me, the save code shouldn't be too much more complicated (I hope)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-08 |title=No release date promises |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I also don't want to post a release date as of right now... things have not gone as planned, and I don't want to turn into Blizzard. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-08 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 coming soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I'm delaying the release of SPUD Farmer 2.0 until I can write some decent help files for it... the people who have been good enough to download it either can't figure it out, or are quick to set it aside... and that's not good.I may also delay it so that it might be able to change stats of units in the new engine, but that may wait for 2.1 or beyond...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-07 |title=No 256x256 puds without new engine |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sorry for the delay in updating. Been busy with SPUD Farmer 2.0 In any case, more news on the SPUD front: we will not be doing 256x256 puds without the new engine. Unfortunately there is no workaround at this time. However,the way I understand it, 255x255 puds should be absolutely ok... at least I think so, from what I have been told.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-07 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 soon |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SPUD Farmer 2.0 should be out tomorrow... at the latest on Tuesday. It'll be adding several new editors as well as bug-fixing the old ones. Unit data, restrictions, upgrades, and gfx editors are NEW.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-03 |title=SPUD Farmer 2.0 will handle restrictions |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Warning! Anyone who tries to open a pud with a restriction (ALOW) section in their pud will not be able to do so until version 2.0 of SPUD Farmer, due out soon. Thanks for your patience.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-03 |title=SPUD Creation working |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

SPUD Creation appears to be working in SPUD Farmer 2.0 (NOT RELEASED YET), and it will allow you to specify the dimensions of your choice.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-03 |title=Unit Data editor finished |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Unit Data editor for SPUD Farmer finished. GFX editor almost done, and will be followed by the Upgrade and Restriction editors.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-03-01 |title=Color section in EXE located |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Wonderful news! I located (finally) the section in the EXE which loads the colors for the players. Fortunately, that means that instead of the new players all having red, they'll now be able to have whatever I want. :)
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=February 1998}}

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-27 |title=Engine progress |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Fortunately the new engine is progressing along nicely. All suggestions should be made before tomorrow. After that, Merlin will be finalizing everything, and will not be accepting any more stuff.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-27 |title=Good and bad SPUD Farmer news |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Good news and bad news: I'll give the bad news first. Our lead hacker says that he may not be able to fix the SPUDs like he thought eariler. We'll both be conducting exhausting trials to try and see what we can do.
On another note, SPUD Farmer 1.5 will be out sometime tomorrow (finally) and will let you create the spuds, although I don't know what good that'll do now...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-25 |title=Suggestion list |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

As promised, I've uploaded the suggestion / feature list that I have so far. Have a gander [here](Ideas.rtf).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-24 |title=Feature list for RTS engine |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Sometime tomorrow (with any luck) I should be uploading a list of features that we hope to include in the new RTS Engine that we're developing for your input. I'll probably place it both here (on the page somewhere)
and on the forum. Feel free to submit any ideas you think that I have left out, and we'll be more than happy to consider them for inclusion.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-21 |title=Removed ads and adding logo |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Also, due to some complaints, I took the ads down... also, in the next few days we should be getting a new logo... should be cool if it's what I think it'll look like. :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-21 |title=SPUD Farmer 1.5 delay |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok... slight delay in 1.5, but that's ok as it gives you a chance to catch up and learn about 1.4... hopefully 1.5 or something will be up tomorrow for you to play around with. My guess is, if that does everything it should, that'll be the last version I do.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-19 |title=SPUD Farmer 1.4 released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, the code is certainly more stable in [SPUD Farmer v1.4](downloads.htm). Eventually, when I get around to figuring out how, I'll be improving upon even this major revision. For those of you with small screen resolutions (i.e. less than 800*640), you'll be happy to know that I've totally re-designed the interface here. No longer will you be stuck with that gigantic screen of indiscernable info. From now on, you can have your pick from three seperate editors that you can look at in a new multiple document interface! Sound like fun? Probably not, unless you're familiar with programming... you'll get what I'm talking about when you download it. Enjoy!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-18 |title=SPUD Farmer 1.3 released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

I feel like Crazy Eddie here... you know, the guy who can't stop selling used cars until he goes mad? Well, I can't stop improving upon SPUD Farmer... TONS of new abilities have been added to it... so many that I had to create a whole new editing screen! SHEESH! And I'm not planning on stopping updating this sucker until I'm happy with it! That's the way it should be, right? Anyways, go to the [downloads page](downloads.htm) once again to grab the latest version.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-17 |title=SPUD Farmer 1.2 released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[SPUD Farmer v1.2](downloads.htm) Uploaded. Anyone who downloaded v1.0 should download ONLY the 1.2 file. Anyone else should download everything in the SPUD Farmer section of the downloads page.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-17 |title=Minor SPUD Farmer bugs |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Minor bugs in SPUD Farmer 1.0, and in 1.1 (which I didn't upload). I think they've all been routed out with v1.2
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-17 |title=Done uploading |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok... done uploading. Also, I'll be posting SPUD Farmer v1.1 later today or early tomorrow. It includes a slightly improved interface that fixes a few minor bugs as well.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-17 |title=Uploading Funcraft |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Uploading Funcraft today... should be done by midday or afternoon. The download's a little more complicated this time. As it was so difficult for me to get it uploaded in one piece, I sent it up in 1.44 meg chunks, and put each file up in its own directory. You'll have to download each file seperately, and then save each file to a disk, where you can finally unzip the thing. Believe me, I'd like to put it all in one file, but right now with my slow connection speed, it's just not possible. Besides, if it gets stuck downloading, it's easier to try redownloading a 1.4 meg file rather than a 4.5meg file, right? I mean, come on, no complaints! It's FREE!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-16 |title=SPUD Farmer screenshot |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[SPUD Farmer Screenshot](screenshot.jpg).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-16 |title=SPUD Farmer info |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

What does it do, you ask? Well, in short, it provides you with the ability to add extra players into the game. And do a few other interesting things in the process. I'll have a tutorial up in a few days time for you to use with it. To get you going for now, make sure you use an already made map file, and that you have units designated in there that you want to convert to other player's units and start locations. Just select the unit you desire, check its coordinates, and change it to whatever you want! Have fun!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-16 |title=SPUD Farmer v1.0 released |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Merlin has been heckling me to get a piece of actual software out... he felt quite alone, and that's understandable... :) This is hard stuff to do! In any case, I've now created a util I call SPUD Farmer v1.0. It's available for your downloading pleasure [here](downloads.htm).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-12 |title=Email RTS game suggestions |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

[Email](mailto:samuel_l_jackson@hotmail.com?subject=Suggestions) us your suggestions for the new RTS game! Please include features that you'd like to see, or feel should be left out!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-12 |title=New RTS engine |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Also, we're working on a new game so advanced, its capabilities will completely blow StarCraft out of the water! Check at this site in the next few weeks for more details. The possibility of this game forever changing the way games are created is certainly not unreasonable.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-12 |title=Will upload Funcraft |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Ok... after the move to the new server, I will be uploading Funcraft... in small (mega)byte-sized chunks. (No pun intended).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-11 |title=New server address |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, due to circumstances beyond my control, we will be moving shortly to: [http://camelot.rtsdomain.com/](https://web.archive.org/web/20000204001541/http://camelot.rtsdomain.com/)
Sorry to do this to you again. Hopefully this will be the final move. :) I'm just as sick of it as you are. Finally when I get used to the new address, they go and merge with another page!!! :) Go figure.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-10 |title=SPUD problems will be fixed |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Our programming guys should be finished fixing the SPUD problems sometime within the next week or two, or so they tell me :) Wish them luck!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-10 |title=SPUD Farmer Alpha testers |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Alpha testers: we will be testing the extra players soon in the SPUDs (they are also part of the new SPUDs...), so be prepared to get back to work again!
Anyone interested should apply via [email](mailto:king_arthur@rtshq.com?Subject=128x+Puds). Thanks!
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-06 |title=WarCraft II patch 1.33 |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

For those users and Alpha testers without the 1.33 patch, you can now download it [here](downloads.htm) (as Blizzard no longer has it available).
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-06 |title=Pre-alpha testing update |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Pre-alpha testing is wrapping up... at least the single player version. Soon we will be having multiplayer tests on Kali. The results have been about half and half as of right now... in terms of who can run the SPUDs. Obviously, we hope that number will increase as we are able to properly reallocate the memory.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-04 |title=Expansion required |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

The Alpha seems to be proceeding quite well. Unfortunately, we have determined that a new exe will be required, and therefore version 1.33. Sorry for those of you who haven't gotten the expansion yet... might as well do so soon, it's pretty cheap now anyways.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-04 |title=WarCraft modding info comming |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Soon to come: Information on making units burrow in WarCraft (amongst other things)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-03 |title=Preliminary Alpha being shipped out |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We are now "shipping out" the Preliminary Alpha to our testers... if haven't received an email about the Pre-Alpha from us, drop us a line...
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-03 |title=Funcraft download temporarily down |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Unfortunately, the Funcraft download is temporarily down... I'll let you know when it's back up. Sorry for the delay.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-02-02 |title=Re-organization at CamSys |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We've recently re-organized to redistribute our priorities... Sir Lancelot will now be helping out Merlin with the programming, as will King Arthur, who is now responsible for the web page... :)
{{end-newsitem}}



{{heading |level=2 |title=January 1998}}

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-01-31 |title=Preliminary Alpha of SPUD Farmer |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

We're planning on distributing the Preliminary Alpha test soon to our testers... it does have several known bugs in it, but we figured that itwas more important to make sure that it really worked, etc... at least somewhat.
To apply for a testing position, please [email](mailto:king_arthur@rtshq.com?Subject=128x+Puds) me using that link.
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-01-23 |title=Changed layout |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Changed the layout around some... let me know if you like it or not... hopefully it should be easier to use...
{{end-newsitem}}

{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-01-23 |title=Announcements |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

Well, unfortunately my computer crashed the other day, so I had to reinstall everything. :( I do have some important announcements to make, however.
1. Cameron Buschardt ([Merlin](mailto:merlin@rtshq.com)) has officially combined his page with mine. This is to save him the trouble of updating his page, and allowing us to consolodate our efforts.
2. Therefore, all software previously available at his page has now moved here. Later today, I will be uploading new pages which will explain each of his pieces of software in great detail, so that you can know what you're downloading :)
3. Also, thanks go out to Clone for making our new site gfx. A huge improvement over the old ones, wouldn't you say? :)
4. Finally, we will be adding an old news/archive section, so that you can all read our history :)
{{end-newsitem}}


{{begin-newsitem |date=1998-01-20 |title=Daily counter |author=King Arthur |site=Camsys |categories=Camsys}}

{{end-newsitem}}
